19.2 Subject to clauses 2.9 and 19.1 above SCDC and/or the County Council shall give the Owners the opportunity to discuss the breach with SCDC and/or the County Council as appropriate and then remedy it.

19.3 Subject to clauses 2.9 and 19.1 above if the Owners do not remedy the breach within the stipulated timescale or such longer time period as may be reasonably proposed by
the Owners in writing and approved by SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable), SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) shall be able to pursue legal enforcement remedies.

19.4 Nothing within this clause 19 shall prevent SCDC and/or the County Council exercising their statutory rights or taking legal proceedings to enforce their obligations set out in this Agreement.

19.5 The Councils shall be entitled to rely on the enforcement provisions contained within Section 106(5) to Section 106(8) of the 1990 Act but if any provision is held to be a contractual provision outside of the 1990 Act, the Councils shall be entitled to use usual contractual enforcement methods including claim for damages where there is a financial payment due, injunction where obligations have a negative trigger and specific performance in respect of any positive obligation to deliver on-site infrastructure or facilities or put in place relevant Security.

19.6 The Owners shall permit the Councils and their authorised employees and agents upon reasonable written notice to enter the Site at all reasonable times for the purpose of verifying whether or not any obligation arising hereunder has been performed or observed.

19.7 Without prejudice to any other right, remedy or power herein contained or otherwise available to the Councils if there is a breach of a requirement in a Planning Obligation to carry out any operations in, on, under or over the Site the Councils may subject to clauses 19.2 and 19.3:-

19.7.1 enter the Site and carry out the operations; and

19.7.2 recover from the Owners any expenses reasonably and properly incurred by the Councils in doing so as a debt due and owing.

20. MATERIAL DEFAULT

20.1 In the case of any ongoing material default(s) of compliance by the Owners of the obligation to properly complete and/or manage and maintain any Facility and/or Amenity or part thereof in accordance with the obligations under this Agreement which results in a material adverse impact on the Development SCDC and/or the County Council may:

20.1.1 Subject to clauses 2.9 and 19.1 above provide written notification to the Owners setting out:

(a) the nature and extent of the material default; and

(b) the Facility and/or Amenity or part thereof to which the material default relates including a full note with details of the same; and

(c) the steps which are reasonably necessary and which the Owners are required to take in order to rectify the material default; and

(d) a reasonable timeframe for the Owners to rectify the material default

PROVIDED ALWAYS THAT SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) has:

(a) used Reasonable Endeavours to notify the Owners of such material default within a reasonable period having regard to the nature/seriousness of the default and following SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) becoming aware of the material default; and
(b) invited the Owners to enter into discussions with SCDC and/or the
      County Council (as applicable) with a view to rectifying that default.

20.1.2 In the event that the Owners dispute any of the matters within 20.1.1 the
      matter may be referred to the Expert pursuant to clause 17 and for the
      avoidance of doubt this may include a dispute in respect of the 20.1.1(d) in
      which case the timeframe for rectification of the material default set by
      SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) shall not apply.

20.2  In the event that the Owners do not rectify the material default to the reasonable
      satisfaction of SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) within the timeframe
      set out in the notice or within the timeframe set by the Expert SCDC and/or the County
      Council (as applicable) may then:

20.2.1 provide written notification to the Owners that SCDC and/or the County
      Council (as applicable) intends to take steps to rectify the material default;
      and

20.2.2 take any of the steps set out in the notice which may include the following;
      and which are in addition to any statutory powers it may have in this regard:
  * (a) To enter upon the area of the Facility and/or Amenity with or
          without vehicles and equipment;

  * (b) To carry out the steps identified at 20.1.1(c) within the timeframe
          identified at 20.1.1(d); and

  * (c) To recover SCDC's and/or the County Council's (as applicable)
          reasonable costs for carrying out such steps from the Owners.

20.3  In the event that there is an on-going material default in respect of any one given part
      of any Facility and/or Amenity which results in a material adverse impact on the
      Development for a period of 12 months or more and PROVIDED THAT notice has
      already been served pursuant to 20.1.1 then SCDC and/or the County Council (as
      applicable) may:

20.3.1 provide written notification to the Owners setting out:

  * (a) the nature and extent of the ongoing material default; and

  * (b) the Facility and/or Amenity or part thereof to which the material
          default relates including a full detailing the same; and

  * (c) the steps which are reasonably necessary and which the Owners
          are required to take in order to rectify the material default; and

  * (d) a reasonable timeframe for the Owners to rectify the material
          default; and

  * (e) that SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) may call for
          the transfer of the Facility and/or Amenity or part thereof to it or the
          Town Council in the event that the material default is not rectified
          within the timeframe.

20.3.2 In the event that the Owners dispute any of the matters within  20.1.1 the
      matter may be referred to the Expert pursuant to clause 17 and for the
      avoidance of doubt this may include a dispute in respect of the 20.3.1(d) in
      which case the timeframe for rectification of the material default set by
      SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) shall not apply.

20.4 In the event that paragraph 20.3 applies and the Owners do not rectify the ongoing material default to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) within the timeframe set out in the notice or within the timeframe set by the Expert SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) may then call for a transfer of the Facility and/or Amenity or part thereof.

20.5 These provisions shall apply mutatis mutandis in favour of the Owners where SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) or if relevant the Town Council has taken ownership of an area of any Facility and/or Amenity pursuant to this Agreement.

20.6 Where it is intended to transfer a Facility and/or Amenity to a particular Alternative Body and there has been an Insolvency Event in relation to that Alternative Body who was to be the transferee of such Facility and/or Amenity then the Transfer shall not proceed to that Alternative Body and instead the Transfer shall only proceed to an Alternative Body which has not been the subject of any Insolvency Event.

20.7 Where:

20.7.1 any Facility and/or Amenity has been transferred to a particular Alternative Body; and

20.7.2 an Insolvency Event has subsequently occurred in relation to that Alternative Body

then:

20.7.3 the Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours to procure a Transfer the Facility and/or Amenity to an Alternative Body which has not been the subject of any Insolvency Event; and

20.7.4 in the event that there is an outstanding material default which has not been rectified the provisions of clauses 20.1 to 20.4 shall apply and as applicable SCDC may call for transfer of the Facility and/or Amenity or part thereof to SCDC or the Town Council and the County Council may call for transfer of the Facility and/or Amenity of part thereof to the County Council

21. LOCAL LAND CHARGE REGISTRATION

21.1 SCDC and/or the County Council shall register this Agreement in its Register of Local Land Charges as soon as practicable after the date of this Agreement.

21.2 Where the Agreement is released in part by a future Agreement, SCDC will place a note to that effect against the entry made in the Local Land Charges Register.

21.3 Where the Agreement comes to an end under clause 9.1 SCDC shall vacate or cancel the entry made in the Local Land Charges register in relation to this Agreement or otherwise record the fact that it has come to an end and no longer affects the Site.

21.4 If the Owners make a request in writing for SCDC to place a note against the entry made in the Local Land Charges Register stating which obligations under this Agreement have been wholly or partially discharged released and/or complied with, SCDC will place such a note against the entry PROVIDED THAT such obligations have been discharged released and/or complied with.

22. CONTRACTS (RIGHTS OF THIRD PARTIES) ACT 1999

22.1 The Parties intend that no terms of this Agreement may be enforceable pursuant to the Contract (Rights of Third Parties) Act 1999 by any person who is not a Party to this Agreement save for

22.1.1 any successor in title to the Owners; or

22.1.2 any successor in function to SCDC and the County Council; or

22.1.3 where relevant the Alternative Body

23. INDEXATION

23.1 All financial amounts set out in this Agreement or the Schedules hereto relating to Contributions or Works In Kind are stated as at the date of this Agreement but where any such amounts fall due for payment or are paid after the date of this Agreement and are stated to be subject to Indexation the relevant figure shall be adjusted to reflect changes in the Index specified in the Indexation Table as attached at Appendix 2 between the date of this Agreement and the relevant index termination date as shown in the Indexation Table.

23.2 The Parties to this Agreement acknowledge that when indexation is calculated on any payment then the relevant Index figures to calculate the indexation amount applying for the period between the date of signing of this Agreement and the relevant index termination date as shown in the Indexation Table shall be the figures set out in the latest Index published at the relevant index termination date as shown in the Indexation Table whether the figures in that latest published index show forecast or other figures applicable for the date of signing of this Agreement and the relevant index termination date.

24. INTEREST ON LATE PAYMENT AND OCCUPATION RESTRICTION(S)

24.1 If any payment(s) due from the Owners under any of the provisions of this Agreement are not made on or before the date on which the payment is due (for which purpose a sum shall be taken as due not later than the day before the relevant event occurs in relation to Occupation and/or Commencement of Development or otherwise prior to which the payment is to be made) the sum due shall bear Interest from the due date until the date of payment and the Owners covenant with SCDC and the County Council to pay any Interest accrued at the same time and to the same recipient as the principal sum on which it has accrued is paid.

25. COMPANY GUARANTEE

25.1 In respect of relevant of the Secured SCDC Obligations and Secured County Obligations as set out in the Table of Securities to be undertaken by the Owners to benefit SCDC and the County Council respectively pursuant to this Agreement the Guarantor Company hereby guarantees to SCDC and the County Council (i) the performance in full of each and all of those obligations as to Works In Kind and/or Contributions as identified in the Table of Securities as being secured by way of charge and (ii) the payment in full of each and all sums due from the Owners under this guarantee within 30 days of receipt of a written demand for the full particulars of the amounts due and how they have arisen) from SCDC and/or the County Council as applicable.

25.2 Further the Guarantor Company covenants that in case of default in performance of those obligations as to Works in Kind and/or Contributions the Guarantor Company shall pay and make good to SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) all losses damages costs and expenses thereby reasonably incurred or arising including any works which should have been carried out by the Owners in accordance with the terms of this Agreement but which are actually carried out by or on behalf of SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) PROVIDED ALWAYS AND it is hereby agreed that any neglect or forbearance of SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) in endeavouring to enforce performance of the said covenants of the Owners or to obtain payment and any time which may be given to the Owners by SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) shall not release exonerate or
affect the liability of the Guarantor Company under the Guarantee given under this clause 25.

25.3 The guarantee set out in Clause 25.1 and 25.2 shall be limited and shall only apply to the relevant Secured SCDC Obligations or the relevant Secured County Obligations or part thereof specified in the relevant clause and FOR THE AVOIDANCE OF DOUBT this shall not extend to any other liability or obligation of the Owners pursuant to this Agreement including (without limitation) any claim, demand, loss, damages, costs or expense for which they or any of them may be liable resulting from any non-observance, non-performance or breach of this Agreement.

25.4 Following the performance by the Owners of the Secured SCDC Obligations being secured or the Secured County Obligations being secured SCDC or the County Council (as applicable) shall within 10 Working Days of a written request by the Owners issue a certificate to the Guarantor Company in such form as the Owners may reasonably request acknowledging the payment of the said sum or contribution or the performance of the said obligation and authorising the reduction of the level of Company Guarantee and if the obligations have been performed in full the Company Guarantee shall thereby be released in full.

26. BONDS

26.1 In respect of the Secured County Obligations to be made by the Owners to the County Council by way of Bond the Owners shall provide a Bond or Bonds in favour of the County Council in the form appended to this Agreement unless otherwise varied or amended as a result of the provisions of clause 3.2 as set out in the Table of Securities by way of security for the discharge of the obligation and such bond shall be put in place no later than one month before any payment required pursuant to the relevant Secured County Obligation becomes due.

26.2 The County Council shall accept the Bond or Bonds as security for payment of the relevant Secured County Obligations pursuant to clause 26.1.

26.3 Following the payment of any Contribution secured by way of or the performance of any obligation secured by way of a Bond or Bonds to the County Council the Owners shall serve the Payment Notification on the County Council.

26.4 The County Council covenants with the Owners that it shall within 5 Working Days of receipt of the Payment Notification issue a Release Letter to the Surety in respect of the relevant Bond acknowledging the payment of the said sum or Contribution or the performance of the said obligation and authorising the reduction of the Bond Sum and shall thereby be reduced to nil the said Release Letter shall authorise the release of the relevant Bond by the Surety thereunder.

27. LEGAL CHARGE(S)

27.1 The Owners covenant with SCDC with the exception of items to be secured by way of Company Guarantee as set out in Clause 25 and the Table of Securities that they will prior to Implementation provide security to SCDC by provision of a Legal Charge in the form of the draft Legal Charge as set out in Appendix 6 for those obligations identified in the Table of Securities as those to be secured by way of a Legal Charge:

27.1.1 over the area shown edged red on the plan as set out in Appendix 6 provided that SCDC (acting reasonably) shall be satisfied that the said area constitutes adequate security for the payments and obligations referred to; or

27.1.2 over such other area agreed between SCDC and the Owners as constituting adequate security for the payments and obligations referred to and subject
to the area of land for affordable and open market residential development.
for such area being agreed between the Owners and SCDC.

and the Owners shall not Implement the Development until the Legal Charge including
an agreed plan has been executed by the Owners and delivered to SCDC.

27.2 Implementation shall not be permitted to proceed unless the Legal Charge has been
executed and delivered to SCDC in accordance with Clause 27.1 or there is in place
an alternative form of security in the form of a cash payment or the deposit of a bond
which has been provided to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC

27.3 The Owners hereby covenant with SCDC that they will through themselves or through
their agents not proceed with or permit Implementation and will after Implementation
cease all works on the Site at any time that the Legal Charge is not in place and such
works shall not be resumed until the Legal Charge is in place or an alternative form of
security has been provided to the satisfaction of SCDC.

28 LAND TRANSFERS AND BUILD LICENCES

The Owners SCDC and the County Council covenant with one another as follows:

28.1 Where any of the provisions of this Agreement include a requirement on the Owners
to offer any part of the Site or Facilities or Amenities to SCDC or the County Council
and the Owners retain a right under this Agreement to construct lay out install or
otherwise equip such Facility or Amenity then the Owners shall offer to transfer the
Bare Land to SCDC or the County Council (as applicable) within 5 (five) years from
the date of this Agreement subject to:

28.1.1 such transfer of the Bare Land including the terms set out in the relevant
Schedule to this Agreement applying to the type of land use transfer; and

28.1.2 a Building Licence being completed simultaneously with the completion of
the relevant transfer to enable the Owners to access the relevant piece of
land and construct lay out install or otherwise equip the Bare Land as may
be provided by the terms of this Agreement.

PROVIDED THAT where this Agreement requires the land which is the subject of the
land transfer to be Serviced then the Owners shall not be obliged to transfer Serviced
land within the 5 (five) year period but shall be obliged to transfer Bare Land subject to
obligations restrictions and covenants being contained in the relevant land transfer
supported by a Building Licence (as necessary) to enable the Owners to retain the
necessary rights to access the land which has been transferred for the purposes of
completing its obligation under the relevant part of this Agreement to provide Serviced
land to SCDC or the County Council (as applicable) AND PROVIDED FURTHER
THAT for the avoidance of doubt the obligation to transfer land to SCDC or the County
Council (as applicable) within 5 (five) years from the date of this Agreement shall also
apply in relation to the transfer of land alone rather than just to transfers of land and
works to provide open space having been undertaken and the
circumstances be entitled to transfer Bare Land within the 5
(year) period retaining the obligation (if imposed by the
Agreement) to provide Serviced land by the deadline referred to in the relevant
Agreement in which the obligations relating to the said piece of land
are contained.

28.2 The Owners shall pay any Stamp Duty Land Tax liability arising in relation to the
completion of any land transfer to SCDC or the County Council pursuant to the terms
of this Agreement if such a transfer occurs after a date being 5 (five) years after the
date of this Agreement PROVIDED THAT for the avoidance of doubt if delay on the
part of SCDC or the County Council (as applicable) results in the completion of the
land transfer occurring after the 5 (five) year deadline and so long as the Owners have
delivered executed transfer(s) and where relevant Building Licence(s) to SCDC or the County Council (as applicable) 30 Working Days prior to the expiry of the 5 (five) year period then SCDC or the County Council (as applicable) will be responsible for any Stamp Duty Land Charge liability arising.

28.3 SCDC and the County Council covenant with the Owners:

28.3.1 not to refuse to accept the offer of the Bare Land provided it contains the terms as set out in the relevant Schedule to this Agreement applying to the type of land use transfer and will be accompanied by simultaneous completion of a Building Licence; and

28.3.2 to act with time being of the essence to complete any land transfer of the Bare Land and associated Building Licence; and

28.3.3 that they shall not be entitled to require that the Owners pay and be responsible for the costs associated with any Stamp Duty Land Tax liability PROVIDED THAT the relevant land transfer is completed within 5 (five) years of the date of this Agreement PROVIDED THAT for the avoidance of doubt if delay on the part of SCDC or the County Council (as applicable) results in the completion of the land transfer occurring after the 5 (five) year deadline and as the Owners have delivered executed transfers to SCDC or the County Council (as applicable) 30 Working Days prior to the expiry of the 5 (five) year period then SCDC or the County Council (as applicable) will be responsible for any Stamp Duty Land Charge liability arising.

29. OVERALL CAP ON CONTRIBUTIONS.

The Owners SCDC and the County Council covenant with one another as follows:

29.1 Save as provided for in this clause 29, the Owners' total liability in respect of all Contributions and Works in Kind is limited to no more than £30 million (the "Overall Costs Cap") and which shall together cover:

29.1.1 any monies paid by the Owners to SCDC or the County Council by way of a Contribution; and

29.1.2 the Actual Cost of any Works in Kind carried out

PROVIDED THAT the Parties to this Agreement acknowledge and agree that:

29.1.3 the Overall Costs Cap shall be taken as being the aggregate of all Contributions and Actual Costs of any Works In Kind as described in this Agreement before any inflationary effects are taken into account by the imposition of the obligation to index Link any such Contributions or Actual Costs of any Works In Kind as may be specified in this Agreement; and

29.1.4 any savings that may be made in relation to the On-Site SUDS Contribution arising out of the operation of clause 29.5 or otherwise such that monies are not required to be used for the On-Site SUDS Contribution shall be allocated and paid into the Account as defined and provided for in Schedule 14 SUBJECT ALWAYS to the Overall Costs Cap continuing to apply AND PROVIDED FURTHER THAT any savings made in relation to the On-Site SUDS Contribution paid into the Account shall not be construed to reduce the sum payable pursuant to paragraph 6 of Part 5 of Schedule 14; and

29.1.5 the costs of
(a) any contribution towards the Interim Primary School Facility as provided for in Schedule 2, and
(b) the costs of compliance with the Affordable Housing obligations in Schedule 7; and
(c) the costs of compliance with the ordnance obligations in Schedule 12; and
(d) the costs of compliance in delivering all of the Amenities

shall be borne by the Owners irrespective of the Overall Costs Cap; and

29.1.6 the costs of putting in place the Company Guarantees, Bonds and Legal Charges as referred to respectively in clauses 25, 26 and 27 of this Agreement shall not be taken into account in determining if the Overall Costs Cap has been reached or exceeded and such costs shall be borne by the Owners irrespective of the Overall Costs Cap; and

29.1.7 where any remedial works or works to address Defects are to be undertaken by the Owners then the costs of undertaking such works shall fall outside of the Overall Costs Cap and shall not be taken into account in determining if the Overall Costs Cap has been reached or exceeded and such costs shall be borne by the Owners irrespective of the Overall Costs Cap; and

29.1.8 in the event the Actual Costs exceed the Allocated 106 Costs then the Owners shall be responsible for bearing such excess Actual Costs and shall not be taken into account in determining if the Overall Costs Cap has been reached or exceeded and such costs shall be borne by the Owners irrespective of the Overall Costs Cap; and

29.1.9 the payment by the Owners to the County Council pursuant to paragraph 15 of Part 2 of Schedule 2 to this Agreement shall not be taken into account in determining if the Overall Cost Cap has been reached or exceeded.

29.2 The Owners shall continue to make full payments of the Contributions and complete the Works in Kind as required by this Agreement until such time as the Overall Costs Cap has been reached after which subject to the provisions of clause 29.1 above the liability of the Owners for those obligations that remain to be discharged in full or in part under this Agreement in respect of Contributions and Works in Kind shall cease (and these Contributions and Works in Kind shall be known as the "Remaining Obligations") and thereafter SCDC and the County Council shall pursuant to the respective obligations in paragraph 1.2 in Schedule 17, and paragraph 1.6 in Schedule 18 use all Reasonable Endeavours to identify and secure all such necessary funding of the Remaining Obligations or part thereof can be discharged as soon as possible having regard to the relevant trigger for such Remaining Obligations in accordance with the provisions of this Agreement whether this requires SCDC and/or the County Council to procure Completion of (i) any works to which Contributions would otherwise have been used or (ii) any Works in Kind that remain to be Completed.

29.3 In the event that a situation where a Clawback Contribution arises then the provisions of clause 29.4 shall apply as regards how any monies are to be held retained used expended or applied by SCDC and the County Council.

29.4 SCDC and the County Council covenant with one another and with the Owners that should either be in possession of any monies that would by operation of this Agreement be considered a Clawback Contribution (who shall for the purposes of this clause 29 be defined as "the Retaining Authority") then:

29.4.1 the Retaining Authority shall immediately place the Clawback Contribution into a designated account from which monies shall only be permitted to be withdrawn either (i) to discharge any Remaining Obligations which by operation of clause 29.2 the Owners shall have been released from performing and discharging or where all Remaining Obligations under this Agreement have been discharged in full, or (ii) to refund to the relevant party (i.e that originally paid the relevant monies which now form the Clawback Contribution) any remaining monies in the account that relate to the Clawback Contribution together with interest accrued thereon (such interest to be calculated from the date the Clawback Contribution arose through the operation of the paragraphs recited in the definition of Clawback Contribution until the date of repayment of the relevant monies) and for the avoidance of doubt SCDC and the County Council shall either respectively create their own designated accounts or jointly create a joint designated account where they become a Retaining Authority PROVIDED THAT where the Retaining Authority cannot for whatever reason create a designated account it shall propose an alternative means of ring fencing the Clawback Contribution(s) to the Owners that will suitably protect the Clawback Contribution(s) such that it shall only be capable of being drawn against for the purposes as set out above in this clause 29.4.1 and the Retaining Authority shall before implementing the alternative means obtain the Owners' prior written approval or deemed approval pursuant to clause 18 to the alternative means PROVIDED ALWAYS that such alternative means of ring fencing shall not be acceptable where interest would not accrue on the Clawback Contribution(s) and PROVIDED FURTHER THAT until such time as an alternative means has been approved or deemed approved by the Owners the Retaining Authority shall ensure that the Clawback Contribution(s) is ring fenced and is not used or otherwise committed or expended and is attracting interest; and

29.4.2 SCDC and the County Council shall together identify the Remaining Obligations and any Clawback Contribution(s) held by either SCDC or the County Council shall be used and expended to discharge those Remaining Obligations in the order of priority that such Remaining Obligations fall due to be performed or discharged as provided for in this Agreement; and

29.4.3 SCDC and the County Council shall confirm to one another and the Owners in writing every six calendar months commencing two years after Commencement of Development (i) the amount of Clawback Contributions (if any) they hold pursuant to clause 29.4.1 and which notification shall also identify the Contribution that the Clawback Contribution(s) originated from and the amount of the relevant original Contribution which remains unexpended and (ii) where any Clawback Contribution has been expended to discharge any Remaining Obligations the notification shall identify which Remaining Obligation(s) has been so discharged AND PROVIDED FURTHER THAT SCDC and the County Council shall produce and serve:
* (a) a written notification pursuant to this clause 29.4.3 within 40 Working Days of the date when the last of the Remaining Obligations has been performed or discharged PROVIDED ALWAYS that for the purposes of this clause 29.4.3(a) the Remaining Obligations shall not include any On Site SUDs Contribution payable to the County Council pursuant to paragraph 3.6.4 of Schedule 4 of this Agreement; and
* (b) a final written notification pursuant to this clause 29.4.3 within 40 Working Days in the event that the On Site SUDs Contribution is payable to the County Council pursuant to paragraph 3.6.4 of Part 1 of Schedule 4

29.4.4 Where applicable SCDC and the County Council shall identify those Clawback Contributions that they each hold and shall agree as between themselves whether any monies need to be transferred as between SCDC and the County Council or vice versa in order to ensure (subject to there being sufficient monies held by SCDC or the County Council in the relevant designated or other approved account) that the next Remaining Obligation is then having regard to the relevant trigger performed or discharged in full PROVIDED THAT it is acknowledged that this may involve transfer of all or part of any Clawback Contribution held by a Retaining Authority to SCDC or the County Council (as applicable) to be used to procure the carrying out of the Works in Kind or payment of Contributions and that SCDC and the County Council shall be permitted to utilise any and all Clawback Contributions that either hold at any time a Remaining Obligation falls due to be performed or discharged and to do so in priority to the use of any other funds that SCDC or the County Council shall secure pursuant to the obligations imposed by clause 29.2;

29.5 In the event that:

29.5.1 a Clawback Contribution is payable to the Owners pursuant to clause 29.4.3 then:
* (a) subject to the overall savings in respect of the Contributions and Works in Kind pursuant to this Agreement totalling at least £3,275,500 before Indexation; and
* (b) following service of the written notifications pursuant to clause 29.4.3

  the Retaining Authority shall:

* (c) when serving the written notification pursuant to clause 29.4.3(a) above return any unexpended monies together with interest thereon to the party that paid the Contribution that the Clawback Contribution originated from; and
* (d) in the event that the On Site SUDS Contribution is payable to the County Council pursuant to paragraph 3.6.4 of Part 1 of Schedule 4 of this Agreement then when serving the final written notification pursuant to clause 29.4.3(b) above the County Council shall return any unexpended monies together with interest thereon to the party that paid the Contribution that the Clawback Contribution originated from

and any unexpended sum relevant to each Clawback Contribution shall be as a result of the final written notification served pursuant to clause 29.4.3 PROVIDED THAT where the obligations in paragraphs 1.5 and 1.6 of Schedule 17 and 1.2,1.3 and 1.4 of Schedule 18 apply and subject to the overall savings in respect of the Contributions and Works in Kind pursuant to this Agreement totalling at least £3,275,500 before Indexation and where the Remaining Obligations have all been performed or discharged by the relevant time period(s) in the said paragraphs have expired then SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) shall within 20 Working Days of the expiry of the relevant time period repay any unexpended balance to the person that paid the relevant Contribution together with any interest accrued (less any tax which may be required to be deducted by law) SAVE THAT where the construction of any buildings or works in relation to which such contribution(s) have been paid have been completed prior to the expiration of relevant time period then this paragraph shall operate subject to the overall savings in respect of the Contributions and Works in Kind pursuant to
this Agreement totalling at least £3,275,500 before Indexation so as to obligate SCDC and/or the County Council (as applicable) to repay any unexpended balance to the party that paid the relevant Contribution within 20 Working Days of the issue of a certificate or equivalent that confirms the completion of the construction of the relevant building and completion of any maintenance or defects period.

29.6 In the event that there has been a Procurement Cost Saving in respect of any particular Facility then provided that:

29.6.1 All obligations in respect of both the Works in Kind and the Contributions have been discharged under this Agreement; and

29.6.2 the overall savings in respect of the Contributions and Works in Kind payable pursuant to this Agreement total at least £3,275,500 before Indexation

29.7 then Once the Works in Kind have been Completed or a Council Final Certificate has been issued (as applicable) in respect of the particular Facility pursuant to this Agreement, then the Owners shall be entitled to retain all of the Procurement Cost Saving relevant to the particular Facility in question.

29.8 Once the Works in Kind have been Completed or a Council Final Certificate has been issued (as applicable) in respect of any particular Facility pursuant to this Agreement within 15 Working Days of such occurrence, the Owners shall notify SCDC in respect of SCDC Matters or the County Council in respect of County Matters of the following:

29.8.1 the Actual Cost of the Works In Kind; and

29.8.2 confirmation of whether there has been any Procurement Cost Savings made in respect of the Works In Kind and demonstrating such Procurement Cost Savings and identifying the quantum to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC in respect of SCDC Matters and the County Council in respect of County Matters and the means by which it will be demonstrated which shall be applied in all instances shall be agreed between the Owners the County Council and SCDC.

29.9 In the event the Owners are entitled by the operation of any of the provisions in this clause 29 to retain any part of a Procurement Cost Saving or be paid a Clawback Contribution then it is agreed that the Owners shall not be required to apply or use such monies in relation to Works in Kind and/or the Contributions

29.10 The Owners SCDC and the County Council covenant with one another and agree that once the Overall Cost Cap has been reached (taking account of the provisions of clauses 29.1.3, 29.1.5, 29.1.6, 29.1.7 and 29.1.8 and the provisions of Affordable Housing in accordance with Schedule 7) the Owners shall have no further liability in respect of or responsibility for and shall be released from discharging those covenants and obligations in this Agreement as to payment of Contributions by the provision of Works in Kind by the Owners and SCDC and the County Council covenant with the Owners that they shall both comply with the provisions of clause 29.2 in relation to the completion of relevant works and obligations which then remain outstanding.

30. JURISDICTION

30.1 This Agreement is governed by and interpreted in accordance with the Laws of England.

31. COUNTERPARTS

31.1 This Deed may be executed in any number of counterparts each of which shall
constitute an original and all the counterparts shall together constitute one and the
same agreement.

IN WITNESS whereof the Parties have duly executed this as a Deed the day and year first
before written.

# SCHEDULE 1 - OWNERS' TITLE
