# SCHEDULE 2 - EDUCATION

## RELEVANT DEFINITIONS

"Building Bulletin 99"
means the Design Bulletin 99 (Briefing Framework for Primary School Projects)(second edition) (internal and external fittings) incorporating revisions to BB82: Area Guidelines for Schools 2006 published by The Stationery Office ISBN-13 978 0 11 271153 7 and ISBN-10 0 11 271153 7 or any replacement or revision thereof in force at the time the Detailed Primary School Building Works Specification is agreed;

"Building Bulletin 98"
means the Design Bulletin 98 (Briefing Framework for Secondary School Projects incorporating revisions to BB82: Area Guidelines for Schools (Secondary edition) published by The Stationery Office or any replacement or revision thereof in force at the time the specification for the Secondary School Building is agreed;

"Delivery Date"
means the date being full three academic school terms following the date of completion of the Primary School Land Transfer;

"Detailed Primary School Building Works Specification"
means the detailed specification and plans of the Primary School Building addressing construction materials programme and methods of working which shall be approved by the Project Core Team;

"Highways Agreement"
means an agreement made under section 38 and/or section 278 of the Highways Act 1980 as otherwise agreed between the Owners and the County Council;

"Highway Works"
means the works as shown indicatively at Plan 13 to provide a permanent vehicular access within the Site to the boundary of the Site closest to the Secondary School Land and whose approximate location is shown on the Secondary School Site Plan at Plan 7 which works are required to make good a deficiency in infrastructure provision arising from the Development, and any detailed design subsequently approved by the County Council;

"Interim Primary School Facility"
means the provision by the Owners at their sole cost of a temporary facility in a location within the vicinity of the Primary School Building or such other location as may be approved by the Project Core Team to be used for the purposes of providing primary education until the Delivery Date or if earlier the date at which the Primary School Building is Completed such that it is capable of being opened to pupils and the size extent nature and specification for such a temporary facility shall be approved by the Project Core Team PROVIDED THAT nothing shall prevent the Owners utilising existing buildings located on the Site to provide such temporary facilities PROVIDED THAT THE Owners shall only be responsible for the capital cost of providing the physical structure of the temporary facility which shall also include the cost associated with fitting out the temporary facility and not for the revenue and running costs of operating the temporary facility which shall be borne by the County
Council;

**"Interim Primary School Facility Specification"** means the specification for the Interim Primary School Facility attached at Appendix 7 PROVIDED ALWAYS THAT the total amount due by the Owners whether by way of monetary contribution or by way of Works in Kind in providing the Interim Primary School Facility as agreed between the Owners and the County Council shall not exceed £300,000;

**"Necessary Highway Consents"** means any by-law approvals, and other consents, licences, permissions and orders required from any competent authority, statutory undertaker or person for the carrying out of the Highway Works;

**"Northstowe Phase 2 Site"** means the subsequent phase of development to Phase 1 for the Northstowe area to be submitted by the Homes and Communities Agency;

**"Practical Completion"** means completion of the Highway Works that enables the Highway Works to be used for the purpose for which they were designed, save for any minor defects and Practically Complete shall be construed accordingly;

**"Primary Contract Award Notice"** means a written notice together with written evidence from the County Council that the County Council have unconditionally entered into a contract for the construction of the Primary School Building;

**"Primary School Approval"** means the grant of full planning permission or Reserved Matters Approval for the provision of the Primary School Building on the Primary School Land;

**"Primary School Building"** means an establishment provided under the Education Act 1996 that provides education to children from birth to 11 years on the Primary School Land and which in this case shall be a three form entry primary school facility with three classes of early years provisions and a science laboratory and which shall further also include car parking landscaping and outdoor play provision constructed in accordance with guidance on standards as set out in Building Bulletin 99 and constructed in accordance with the Detailed Primary School Building Works Specification and in accordance with the approved drawings, plans, specification and other documents;

**"Primary School Building Construction Contribution"** means the sum of £9,800,000 (Nine million eight hundred thousand British pounds);

**"Primary School Building Design Contribution"** means the sum of £1,200,000 (one million two hundred thousand British pounds);

**"Primary School Land"** means the area of land within the Site shall be 3 hectares in size to be provided by the Owners whose approximate location is shown on the indicative Locations - Land Uses Plan at Plan 5 or such other location as shall be agreed between the County Council and the Owners the exact location of which shall be approved in accordance with the provisions of this Schedule PROVIDED THAT in any
event the Primary School Land shall be accessed by a
road which shall be built to an adoptable standard AND
PROVIDED FURTHER that the Owners shall use
reasonable endeavours to enter into a section 38 highway
agreement as to future adoption of such road within a
reasonable timeframe having regards to the Delivery Date
of the Primary School Building;

**"Primary School LandTransfer"**
means a transfer by the Owners to the County Council of
the unencumbered freehold interest in the form of the
draft transfer appended hereto at Appendix 2 or such
other form as the parties may otherwise agree relating to
the transfer of the Primary School Land and each party
shall bear their own legal costs incurred in connection
with the negotiation and completion of such transfer on
terms to be agreed but which shall nevertheless:

1.  not include any terms which would restrict use
    of the land as a Primary School and any and all
    purposes reasonably ancillary thereto which for
    the avoidance of doubt may include community
    uses; and
2.  not include any terms which would materially
    adversely and unreasonably affect the
    construction servicing or occupation of any part
    of the Site that is retained by the Owners; and
3.  include reasonable reservation of rights of
    access over the Primary School Land for the
    benefit of any other part of the Site where such
    rights are reserved for the purpose of laying
    managing maintaining replacing renewing
    cleaning and repairing services including but not
    limited to as applicable sustainable urban
    drainage measures, water, gas, sewerage,
    drainage or electricity; and
4.  include for the benefit of the Primary School
    Land the grant of any and all reasonable rights
    of access over any adjoining land which rights
    are reasonably required for the management
    and maintenance of the Primary School Land
    for its purpose for primary education;
5.  be at nil consideration; and
6.  include a covenant that the Primary School
    Land shall not be used for any purpose other
    than for use as a primary education and
    associated ancillary facilities which for the
    avoidance of doubt may include community
    uses.

**"Primary School Works Notice"**
means a notice providing evidence in writing of the date
that construction works on the Primary School Building
were commenced pursuant to an unconditional contract
for the construction of the Primary School Building;
"Project Core Team" means a steering group comprised of representatives of the County Council (who will be permitted one vote in relation to any decision making of the Project Core Team), the School Promoter that will operate and manage the primary school that will be located within the Primary School Building (who will be permitted one vote in relation to any decision making of the Project Core Team), Gallagher or their nominee (who will be permitted one vote in relation to any decision making of the Project Core Team and which group shall be solely responsible for taking any decision in relation to any and all of the following:

1.  detailed Primary School Building Works Specification including for the avoidance of doubt the construction standards, design, layout, fixtures and fitting applicable to the construction of the Primary School Building;
2.  any decisions relating to matters and works arising out of RIBA Work Stages 0 to 5 as set out in the RIBA Outline Plan of Work;
3.  approving the timetable for the design procurement and construction of the Primary School Building;
4.  approve the Employer's Requirements for the Primary School Building;
5.  approve any significant changes to the design proposed by the contractor by the County Council to construct the Primary School Building

PROVIDED THAT each party acting reasonably may be permitted to invite other interested parties to the meetings as agreed by the other parties in advance;

**"Project Core Team Terms of Reference"** means those matters referred to in Part 6 to this Schedule which are the responsibilities and powers and extent of the Project Core Team's decision making;

"RIBA Plan of Work" means the RIBA Plan of Work 2013 or any replacement or revision thereof and includes the following Work Stages and any reference in this Agreement to RIBA Works Stages shall be Work Stages 0 to 5 inclusive set out in the RIBA Plan of Work 2013;

"School Promoter" means the group, company, charitable body, or other such organisation which has been selected and approved by the Secretary of State for Education to establish a school. The promoter is then responsible for all aspects of running and managing the school including admissions, teaching and learning, governance and employment of staff;

"Secondary School Building" means a twelve form entry secondary school facility to be built on the Secondary School Land which may be built on a phased basis which shall also include car parking landscaping and outdoor play provision constructed in
accordance with guidance on standards as set out in
Building Bulletin 98;

"Secondary School Construction Contribution" means the sum of £7,000,000 (Seven Million British
pounds);

"Secondary School Design
Contribution" means the sum of £1,000,000 (One Million British
pounds);

"Secondary School
Contribution Purposes"
means the use of the Secondary School Design
Contribution and the Secondary School Construction
Contribution towards the design and construction of the
Secondary School Building on the Secondary School
Land or alternatively towards providing additional
secondary school provision or improvement within the
vicinity of the Site which is capable of meeting the
additional demand that has arisen from the Development
as a result of the increase in the projected child yield;

"Secondary School Land"
means the area of land within the Northstowe Phase 2
Site which shall be 14.1 hectares in size to be provided
by the Homes and Community Agency whose
approximate location is shown on the Secondary School
Site Plan at Plan 7.

## PART 1 - LOCATION OF THE SCHOOL SITE

1. The exact location of the Primary School Building (together with the Primary School
Land) shall be approved by or deemed to have been approved by the County Council
either:

1.1 as part of Reserved Matters Application for the Development Parcel in which the
Primary School Building is located; or

1.2 as Reserved Matters Application solely for the Primary School Building

PROVIDED ALWAYS THAT in the event that the County Council rather than the Owners
submits the Reserved Matters Application or other form of planning application for the
Building then prior to submission of the Reserved Matters Application or
planning application the County Council shall have obtained the prior
written approval or deemed approval (pursuant to clause 18) of the Owners to the design
and the exact size shape and location of the Primary School Building.

## PART 2 - BONDING CONSTRUCTING AND FUNDING THE PRIMARY SCHOOL BUILDING

The Owners and the County Council covenant with one another as follows:

1. Prior to Occupation of any Dwellings the Owners shall deliver to the County Council by
delivery the Primary School Land Transfer properly executed by the
Owners in relation to Bare Land PROVIDED THAT notwithstanding the foregoing
provision of this paragraph the Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours to deliver
the properly executed Primary School Land Transfer to the County Council pursuant
to this paragraph as early as practically possible and taking account of the need for
the Owners to undertake certain works to the Primary School Land to make it ready
for the intended use stated in the transfer SAVE THAT the longstop date for such
delivery shall be prior to Occupation of any Dwellings.

2. The Primary School Land shall be Serviced by the Owners at the Owners' cost no
later than 9 (nine) months after the date of completion of the Primary School Land
Transfer PROVIDED THAT notwithstanding the foregoing the Primary School Land shall be Serviced no later than the date when the Primary School Building is open to pupils.

**2.1** The Owners will provide a Bond or Bonds for each of the payments due in paragraph 2.2 to guarantee the payments due under paragraphs 2.3.1 to 2.3.8 and each Bond or part thereof shall be in place no later than 20 Working Days prior to the relevant sum falling due for payment pursuant to this Agreement

**2.2** Following each and every payment by the Owners pursuant to 2.3.1 to 2.3.8 the Bond or element of the Bond provided in respect of that sum shall be discharged or reduced accordingly and will be discharged absolutely on the date of payment of the final instalment pursuant to paragraph 2.3.8.

**2.3** The Owners shall pay the Primary School Building Design Contribution to the County Council in the following instalments:

**2.3.1** £150,000 (one hundred and fifty thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council on or before the date which is 36 calendar months after the date of Implementation;

**2.3.2** £150,000 (one hundred and fifty thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council on the date which is 20 Working Days after the date referred to in paragraph 2.3.1;

**2.3.3** £150,000 (one hundred and fifty thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council on the date which is 40 Working Days after the date referred to in paragraph 2.3.1;

**2.3.4** £150,000 (one hundred and fifty thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council on the date which is 60 Working Days after the date referred to in paragraph 2.3.1;

**2.3.5** £150,000 (one hundred and fifty thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council on the date which is 80 Working Days after the date referred to in paragraph 2.3.1;

**2.3.6** £150,000 (one hundred and fifty thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council on the date which is 100 Working Days after the date referred to in paragraph 2.3.1;

**2.3.7** £150,000 (one hundred and fifty thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council on the date which is 120 Working Days after the date referred to in paragraph 2.3.1;

**2.3.8** £150,000 (one hundred and fifty thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council on the date which is 140 Working Days after the date referred to in paragraph 2.3.1.

**2.4** The Owners will provide a Bond or Bonds for each of the payments due in paragraph 2.5 to guarantee the payments due under paragraph 2.5.1 to 2.5.4 and each Bond or part thereof shall be placed no later than 20 Working Days prior to the relevant sum becoming payable PROVIDED ALWAYS that the County Council shall have first served the Primary School Works Notice on the Owners in writing AND PROVIDED FURTHER if the County Council shall not have served the Primary School Works Notice on the Owners in writing then the obligation to put in place a Bond or Bonds shall not apply until such notice has been served.

**2.5** The Owners shall pay the Primary School Building Construction Contribution to the County Council in the following instalments:
2.5.1 £2,450,000 (Two million four hundred and fifty thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council on the latter of the date:

(a) which is 36 calendar months after the date of implementation; or

(b) which is 36 calendar months after receipt or deemed receipt by the Owners of the Primary School Works Notice.

2.5.2 £2,450,000 (Two million four hundred and fifty thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council within 90 Working Days of the date of payment of the first instalment pursuant to paragraph 2.5.1;

2.5.3 £2,450,000 (Two million four hundred and fifty thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council within 180 Working Days of the date of payment of the first instalment pursuant to paragraph 2.5.1;

2.5.4 £2,450,000 (Two million four hundred and fifty thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council within 270 Working Days of the date of payment of the first instalment pursuant to paragraph 2.5.1.

2.6 Following each and every payment by the Owners pursuant to 2.5.1 to 2.5.4 the Bond or element of the Bond provided in respect of that sum shall be discharged or reduced accordingly and will be discharged absolutely on the date of payment of the final instalment pursuant to paragraph 2.5.4.

3. The County Council covenants with the Owners to construct and complete the Primary School Building and open the Primary School Building to pupils no later than the Delivery Date which shall be evidence by:

3.1 In relation to the construction of the Primary School Building by the issuing of a certificate issued in relation to the satisfactory completion of the construction of and any remedial works to Primary School Building pursuant to the contract for the construction of the Primary School Building;

3.2 In relation to the Primary School Building being open to pupils by evidence that the school operator has been appointed and that pupils have been awarded a place in the new school such place being located within the Primary School Building rather than any temporary or interim building(s) and that pupils are physically being taught within the Primary School Building.

4. The County Council shall keep the Owners informed and updated in writing no less frequently than once per calendar month of progress and in any event at Project Core Team meetings in relation to all key stages of the Primary School Building following the RIBA Plan of Work Key Work Stages 0 to 5 inclusive.

5. The County Council shall use Reasonable Endeavours to seek cost savings and efficiencies in relation to the costs of design construction and furniture and fittings of the Primary School Building including but not limited to not seeking enhanced standards to those specified in Building Bulletin 99.

6. The County Council and Gallagher shall as soon as reasonably practicable following the date of this Deed establish the Project Core Team PROVIDED THAT The County Council shall not be permitted to undertake any work actions decisions or otherwise as are described as being within the Project Core Team Terms of Reference.

7. The County Council shall invite Gallagher or its nominee (who shall be permitted to invite any Owners of any Development Parcel that adjoins the Primary School Land (subject to prior notification to be provided to the Project Core Team) to all Project Core Team meetings that are convened whether such meetings are held in person or
by telephone or videoconferencing and FOR THE AVOIDANCE OF DOUBT this provision shall also apply to the County Council and the School Promoter.

5. The County Council covenants to convene Project Core Team meetings at least once every 4 weeks.

8. The Project Core Team Terms of Reference shall be as set out in Part 6 of this Schedule.

9. The Project Core Team meetings will continue to operate from the date of establishment until the completion of the Primary School Building by the County Council and will be reconvened in advance of and for the duration of the design and construction of any works to the Primary School Building required after the Primary School Building is opened to pupils that are necessitated to convert any part of the Primary School Building that has been temporarily used for uses other than as a primary school back to sole use for primary educational purposes.

10. The County Council shall consider, take into account, incorporate and action any proposals, suggestions, recommendations or comments made by the Owners whether the Owners make them in the Project Core Team meetings or writing served on the County Council and for the avoidance of doubt the Project Core Team shall approve any actions, variations, amendments, documentation and information before the County Council and/or the contractor appointed to construct the Primary School Building take any such action, make such variation or amendment or finalise documentation or otherwise expend any monies related to tasks and stages referred to in the definition of Project Core Team in this Agreement.

12. The County Council shall serve the Owners in writing with a copy of:
    12.1 the Primary Contract Award Notice within 20 Working Days of the date an unconditional contract has been entered into by the County Council for the construction of the Primary School Building or within 20 Working Days of the date of this Agreement in the event such unconditional contract has been entered into before the date of this Agreement.
    12.2 the Primary School Approval within 20 Working Days of receipt of the same; and
    12.3 the certificate referred to in paragraph 3.1 within 10 Working Days of receipt of the same.

13. The County Council and the Owners agree that nothing in this Schedule shall be deemed to prevent the County Council using alternative resources to the Primary School Building Design Contribution and the Primary School Building Construction Contribution in order to construct a Primary School Building larger than that required to mitigate the impact of the Development or to a higher standard than that set out in the Primary School Building Works Specification PROVIDED THAT should larger building or facilities or high standard of construction or fit out or equipping be proposed by the County Council that the County Council shall not seek additional contributions from the Owners and PROVIDED FURTHER THAT the County Council shall still be obliged to comply with the provisions of paragraphs 3 to 12 of this Schedule 2.

14. Prior to the transfer of the Primary School Land described in paragraph 1 of this Part 2 of this Schedule 2 the Owners will permit the County Council to access the Primary School Land to carry out surveys and collect other data required in connection to the construction of the Primary School Building.

15. The Owners covenant with the County Council to pay the County Council within 40 Working Days of receipt of a written demand for payment from the County Council such sum as represents the annual interest charges incurred by and charged to the County Council in relation to the amount of prudential borrowing from the Public
Works Loan Board that the County Council has drawn down for the preceding 12 calendar month period or such other form of borrowing as the County Council may have secured as an alternative to and in the event Public Works Loan Board prudential borrowing is not available PROVIDED THAT such alternative funding shall first have been approved in writing by the Owners AND PROVIDED FURTHER THAT any demand shall only be deemed to be validly served if it contains written evidence of the amount of the annual interest charges attributable to the amount of prudential borrowing from the Public Works Loan Board that the County Council has drawn down for the preceding 12 calendar month period or for such other form of borrowing as the Owners may have first approved and the County Council may have secured as an alternative to and in the event Public Works Loan Board prudential borrowing is not available

16. The obligation in paragraph 15 above shall continue until all of the borrowing attributable to the design and construction of the Primary School Building has been paid off in full.

17. In addition to the annual interest charges referred to in paragraph 15 above the Owners shall also pay to the County Council when paying the sums due under the provisions of paragraph 15 an amount representing 1 per cent above the annual rate of interest and such sum shall only fall due to be paid where the written notification served on the Owners by the County Council pursuant to paragraph 15 also contains a calculation which clearly sets out the annual rate of interest applicable each month, the actual interest charged by the loan body whether the Public Works Loan Board or other entity (as applicable) on the drawn down funds and the sum attributable to the 1 per cent increase.

18. The Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours to procure that with effect from the deadline contained in paragraph 2 above the benefit of any and all guarantees or warranties relating to the surveys, investigations and works undertaken by the Owners before the Primary School Land is transferred to the County Council are assigned to the County Council.

## PART 3 - SECONDARY SCHOOL CONTRIBUTION

1. The Owners will provide a Bond or Bonds for each of the payments due in paragraph 2 in a form approved by the County Council to guarantee the payments due under paragraph 2.1 to 2.4 and each Bond or part thereof shall be placed no later than 20 Working Days prior to the relevant sum falling due for payment pursuant to this Agreement

2. The Owners shall pay the Secondary School Building Design Contribution to the County Council in the following instalments:

* 2.1 £250,000 (Two hundred and fifty thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council prior to Occupation of 100 Dwellings
* 2.2 £250,000 (Two hundred and fifty thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council no later than six (6) months prior to Occupation of 175 Dwellings;
* 2.3 £250,000 (Two hundred and fifty thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council six (6) months prior to Occupation of 225 Dwellings
* 2.4 £250,000 (Two hundred and fifty thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council six (6) months prior to Occupation of 300 Dwellings

3. Following each and every payment by the Owners pursuant to 2.1 to 2.4 the Bond or element of the Bond provided in respect of that sum shall be discharged or reduced accordingly and will be discharged absolutely on the date of payment of the final instalment pursuant to paragraph 2.4

4. The Owners will provide a Bond or Bonds for each of the payments due in paragraph 5.1 in a form approved by the County Council to guarantee the payments due under paragraph 5.1 to 5.8 and each Bond or part thereof shall be placed no later than 20 Working Days prior to the relevant sum falling due for payment pursuant to this Agreement.

5. The Owners shall pay to the County Council the Secondary School Construction Contribution to be used for the sole purpose for the construction and completion of the Secondary School Building as follows:

* 5.1 £875,000 (Eight Hundred and Seventy Five Thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council prior to Completion of 300 Dwellings;
* 5.2 £875,000 (Eight Hundred and Seventy Five Thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council prior to Completion of 350 Dwellings
* 5.3 £875,000 (Eight Hundred and Seventy Five Thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council prior to Completion of 400 Dwellings
* 5.4 £875,000 (Eight Hundred and Seventy Five Thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council prior to Completion of 450 Dwellings
* 5.5 £875,000 (Eight Hundred and Seventy Five Thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council prior to Completion of 500 Dwellings
* 5.6 £875,000 (Eight Hundred and Seventy Five Thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council prior to Completion of 600 Dwellings
* 5.7 £875,000 (Eight Hundred and Seventy Five Thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council prior to Completion of 700 Dwellings
* 5.8 £875,000 (Eight Hundred and Seventy Five Thousand British pounds) to be paid to the County Council prior to Completion of 800 Dwellings

6. Following each and every payment by the Owners pursuant to 5.1 to 5.8 the Bond or element of the Bond provided in respect of that sum shall be discharged or reduced accordingly will be discharged absolutely on the date of payment of the final instalment pursuant to paragraph 5.8.

## PART 4 - INTERIM PRIMARY SCHOOL PROVISION

The Owners covenants with the County Council the following:-

1. to provide the Interim Primary School Facility upon receipt of a written request from the County Council to the Project Core Team stating that the Interim Primary School Facility is now required which should set out the size, duration, extent, nature and specification for the Interim Primary School Facility and which decision shall be approved or deemed approved by the Project Core Team no later than 3 months from the receipt of the written request from the County Council PROVIDED THAT such request from the County Council shall also set out the assessment undertaken to justify the need for the Interim Primary School Facility on the Site demonstrating the need for primary education provision to serve any primary aged pupils arising from the Development where such demand arises in advance of the Primary School Building being open to pupils AND FURTHER PROVIDED THAT such request from the County Council shall so far as reasonably practicable be provided no less than nine (9) months in advance of the date by which in the opinion of the County Council the Interim Primary School Facility is to be provided.

2. not to use or allow or permit the Interim Primary School Facility to be used other than for the use of education provisions until such time as the Primary School Building is
Completed and if capable of being opened to pupils and ensure the Interim Primary School Facility remains open and accessible and available to users until the Delivery Date or is earlier the date at which the Primary School Building is Completed such that it is capable of being open to pupils;

3. that the Owners shall maintain the Interim Primary School Facility

4. to manage the access arrangements to the Interim Primary School Facility PROVIDED THAT this shall not necessitate provision of adopted highways or footpaths to the Interim Primary School Facilities but shall require vehicular and pedestrian access to be provided to the Interim Primary School Facility; and

5. not to prevent or limit access to the Interim Primary School Facility until such time as the Primary School Building is Completed and is capable of being opened to pupils other than at the following times:-

5.1 closure in case of emergency, for public safety or for security purposes;

5.2 temporary closure for maintenance, repair, renewal, cleaning and other necessary or required works;

5.3 temporary closure during the carrying out of works of construction (including development or redevelopment of adjoining buildings and structures or for the placing or replacing of underground services) on grounds that continued use would be unsafe or otherwise inappropriate;

6. The County Council shall use Reasonable Endeavours to minimise the scale of interim provision for primary education purposes including for the avoidance of doubt whether such interim provision for primary education purposes is required.

## PART 5 - PROVISION OF HIGHWAY WORKS

1. No later than 12 months before the earlier of the two time periods referred to in paragraph 2 below the Owners shall:

1.1 enter into a Highways Agreement with the Council to secure the design, construction and completion by the Owners of the Highway Works and their subsequent adoption as publicly maintainable highway.

1.2 obtain all Necessary Highway Consents

PROVIDED THAT the Owners shall only be obliged to comply with this paragraph 1 if the exact location and boundaries of the Secondary School Land are known prior to Occupation of any Dwellings AND PROVIDED FURTHER THAT if the exact boundaries of the Secondary School Land are not known until after Occupation of any Dwellings the Owners shall instead by obliged to comply with this paragraph 1 within 6 months of the date the location and boundaries are fixed for the Secondary School Land

2. Subject to a Highways Agreement having been entered into pursuant to paragraph 1 above then prior to the earlier of Occupation of the 675 Dwelling or where Implementation has occurred on the 1 June 2018 or such other later date agreed with the County Council the Owners shall carry out the Highway Works with carriageway and footpaths completed to binder course with street lighting at its own expense in accordance with the terms of the Highways Agreement and the Necessary Highway Consents.

3. No later than 6 months following a written request from the County Council or such other later date agreed with the County Council the Owners shall provide a suitable temporary construction access (as approved by the County Council) within the Site to
allow construction traffic to access the Secondary School Land PROVIDED THAT the written request is received no earlier than 6 months after the date of Implementation. The cost of providing the temporary construction access shall be met solely by the Owners.

4. The Owners shall give to the Council at least 4 weeks advance notice in writing of its intention to carry out any part of the Highway Works.

## PART 6 - PROJECT CORE TEAM TERMS OF REFERENCE

1. **Purpose**

The County Council and Gallagher have agreed to establish, as soon as reasonably practicable and in advance of any of the work, actions, decisions or otherwise as being within the Terms of Reference referred to below being undertaken or made, a Project Core Team of relevant qualified persons to deliver the actions necessary to deliver in the design and construction of the Primary School Building. The purpose of the Project Core Team is to oversee the design and construction of the Primary School Building and to ensure that the County Council, Gallagher, and the School Promoter act collaboratively on the decisions that have to be made in delivery of the Primary School.

2. **Representation**

2.1 Initially, the Project Core Team shall consist of the following:

2.1.1 Cambridgeshire County Council

2.1.2 Gallagher

2.1.3 School Promoter

2.2 New Project Team members may be added as the needs of the Project requires by mutual agreement between the parties. The Project Core Team will meet every month unless decided otherwise by the parties on the Project Core Team.

2.3 Approvals by the Project Core Team shall unless stated otherwise be decisions approved/agreed by a majority of the parties on the Project Core Team.

3. **Terms of Reference**

The terms of reference of the Project Core Team will be as follows:

3.1 to approve the design brief for the Primary School Building to the County Council,

3.2 to approve any changes to the design brief,

3.3 to approve the Detailed Primary School Building Works Specification,

3.4 to approve the timetable for the design, procurement and construction of the Primary School Building,

3.5 to approve the scheme design (up to Stage 5 of the RIBA Plan of Work) for the Primary School Building. The scheme design will explore the possibility of phasing the Primary School Building which shall only be considered where it can be demonstrated that the Primary School Building can be provided and the cost of constructing the Primary School Building in phases will be no more than £11 million (eleven million British pounds)

3.6 to approve the Employer's Requirements for the Primary School Building. To approve any significant changes to the Stages 0 to 5 of the RIBA Plan of Work design proposed by the Contractor;

3.7 to receive the notification on the need for an interim Primary School Facility on the Site and where it is accepted that such need arises to approve the appropriate provision mechanism (to include the location) of the Interim Primary School Facility.

3.8 to receive / review monthly update reports on progress provided by the Primary School project manager/contractor.

3.9 to approve the details of the planning application, and its accompanying drawings, plans, specifications and other documents to be prepared by the County Council in order to secure the Reserved Matters Approval or other form of planning approval for the Primary School

# SCHEDULE 3 - HOUSEHOLD WASTE RECYCLING CENTRE

## RELEVANT DEFINITIONS

"Household Waste
Recycling Centre"
means the household waste recycling centre to be provided for use by the County Council as a Household Waste Recycling Centre to serve Northstowe in accordance with the Cambridgeshire Minerals and Waste Plan;

"Household Waste
Recycling Centre
Contribution"
means the sum of £190,395 (one hundred and ninety thousand three hundred and ninety five British pounds) to be paid by the Owners to the County Council for the capital cost of construction of the buildings and facilities on the Household Waste Recycling Centre Land;

"Household Waste
Recycling Centre Land"
means the area of Serviced land within the Site shown indicatively edged yellow on Plan 5 "Indicative Locations -- Land Uses Plan" which shall be not less than 1.25 hectares and not more than 1.26 hectares in size to be provided by the Owners and capable of providing the Household Waste Recycling Centre or such other area of Serviced land of similar size and shape in a location as may be submitted by the Owners to and approved by the County Council;

"Household Waste
Recycling Centre Land
Transfer"
means the transfer of the unencumbered freehold interest in the form of the draft transfer appended hereto at Appendix 11 or such other form as the parties may otherwise agree relating to the transfer of the Household Waste Recycling Centre Land by the Owners to the County Council and each party shall bear their own legal costs incurred in connection with the negotiation and completion of such transfer which shall nevertheless:

1. not include any terms which would restrict public access; and

2. not include any terms which would directly or indirectly affect the construction servicing or occupation of the part of the Site that is retained by the Owners; and

3. include reservation of rights of access and services over the Household Waste Recycling Centre Land for the benefit of any other part of the Site for the purpose of laying managing maintaining replacing renewing cleaning and repairing services including but not limited to as applicable sustainable urban drainage measures, water, gas, sewerage, drainage or electricity subject to the provisions of paragraph 1.5 of this Schedule 3; and

4. include for the benefit of the Household Waste Recycling Centre Land the grant of any and all reasonable rights of access and services over any adjoining land which rights are reasonably
required for the use, management and
maintenance of the Household Waste Recycling
Centre Land for its purpose for household waste
recycling services.

5. be at nil consideration; and

6. the transfer shall include a covenant that the
Household Waste Recycling Centre Land shall
not be used by the County Council for any
purpose other than household waste recycling
services.

"Household Waste
Recycling Centre
Specification"
means a specification to be prepared by or on behalf of
the County Council by a suitably qualified and
professionally approved person experienced in preparing
and reviewing specifications for buildings of this sort
which specification shall be approved and deemed
approved by the Owners and which shall include the
following:

1. location of the building on the Site;

2. the design including the proposed materials and
finishes;

3. the gross floor area;

4. the anticipated noise generation; and

5. lighting arrangements.

## 1. HOUSEHOLD WASTE RECYCLING CENTRE LAND

1.1 The exact location of the Household Waste Recycling Centre (together with the
Household Waste Recycling Centre Land) shall be approved by or deemed to have
been approved by SCDC either:

1.1.1 as part of Reserved Matters Applications for the Development Parcel in
which the Household Waste Recycling Centre is located; or

1.1.2 as a Reserved Matters Application solely for the Household Waste Recycling
Centre

PROVIDED ALWAYS THAT in the event that the County Council rather than the
Owners submits the Reserved Matters Applications or other form of planning
application for the Household Waste Recycling Centre then no later than 3 months
before the anticipated date of the submission of the Reserved Matter Application the
County Council shall submit the Household Waste Recycling Centre Specification to
the Owners for the Owners approval or deemed approval and shall thereafter
construct the Household Waste Recycling Centre in accordance with the Household
Waste Recycling Centre Specification as approved or deemed approved.

1.2 Prior to the Occupation of 1400 Dwellings the Owners shall undertake the following:

1.2.1 Completion of all necessary works required to ensure that the Household
Waste Recycling Land is Serviced; and
1.2.2 delivery to the County Council by hand or recorded delivery of the agreed Household Waste Recycling Land Transfer properly executed by the Owners and/or anyone else with an interest in the Site

1.3 In the event that the County Council refuses in writing to accept the offer for the transfer of the Household Waste Recycling Centre Land within 60 Working Days from the date of compliance by the Owners of the requirements set out in paragraph 1.2.2 above the obligation to provide the Household Waste Recycling Centre Land pursuant to this paragraph 1 shall cease to have effect and for the avoidance of doubt the Owners shall be entitled to use the Household Waste Recycling Centre Land for alternative uses.

1.4 No more than 1400 Dwellings shall be Occupied until the following shall have occurred:

1.4.1 all necessary works required to ensure that the Household Waste Recycling Land is Serviced have been Completed; and

1.4.2 delivery to the County Council by hand or recorded delivery of the agreed Household Waste Recycling Land Transfer properly executed by the Owners

1.5 The Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours:

1.5.1 to locate any Utilities and/or Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage within the Service Corridor (which shall have been previously approved or deemed to have been approved by the County Council relevant for the Household Waste Recycling Centre) which Service Corridor shall not be located underneath the actual or anticipated building footprint of the Household Waste Recycling Centre PROVIDED THAT if the County Council has not advised the Owners in writing of the actual or anticipated building footprint before the said Service Corridor has been approved or deemed to have been approved then the Owners shall be permitted to locate any Utilities and/or Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage within the approved or deemed approved Service Corridor notwithstanding that the footprint of the Household Waste Recycling Centre building may ultimately be constructed within the Service Corridor, and

1.5.2 not to locate any Utilities and/or Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage in under or over the Household Waste Recycling Centre Land which are outside of the Service Corridor

SAVE THAT at any time prior to completion of the Household Waste Recycling Centre Transfer the Owners wish to locate services in under or over the Household Waste Recycling Centre Land they shall first obtain the prior written approval or deemed approval of the County Council to the proposed location of such services and the provisions of clause 18 shall apply in relation to the seeking of such approval.

## 2. HOUSEHOLD WASTE RECYCLING CENTRE CONTRIBUTION

2.1 Prior to Occupation of 1400 Dwellings the Owners shall pay the Household Recycling Centre Contribution to the County Council to be used solely for the purpose of providing ongoing waste recycling services within the vicinity of the Development.

2.2 In the event that within ten years of the date of any transfer pursuant to paragraph 1.4 of this Schedule the County Council either:

2.2.1 is not operating a Household Waste Recycling Centre on the Household Waste Recycling Centre Land; or

2.2.2 has not
(a) obtained Reserved Matters Approval or other alternative form of planning permission that permits the construction of the Household Waste Recycling Centre on the Household Waste Recycling Centre Land; and
(b) entered into unconditional contracts to construct the Household Waste Recycling Centre on the Household Waste Recycling Centre Land and as appropriate has entered into unconditional contracts relating to the management of the Household Waste Recycling Centre

then the following shall apply:

2.2.3 the Household Waste Recycling Centre Land shall be returned to the original transferee free from any encumbrances or restrictions as to its future use at nil cost and the County Council shall bear the legal and other costs incurred by the original transferee in completing the transfer; and

2.2.4 the Household Waste Recycling Centre Contribution shall be returned to the payor by the County Council within 40 Working Days after the ten year anniversary of the date of the transfer; and

2.2.5 in the event the Household Waste Recycling Centre Contribution has not been paid by the ten year anniversary of the date of the transfer the obligation in paragraph 2.1 of this Schedule 3 shall cease to have effect.

2.3 No more than 1400 Dwellings shall be occupied until the Owners shall have paid the Household Recycling Centre Contribution to the County Council.

# SCHEDULE 4 - SUSTAINABLE URBAN DRAINAGE SYSTEM

## RELEVANT DEFINITIONS:

"Alternative Body On-Site SUDS Land Transfer" means a transfer by the Owners of the unencumbered freehold or leasehold interest in the On-Site SUDS and each party shall bear their own legal costs incurred in connection with the negotiation and completion of such transfer on terms to be agreed but which shall nevertheless include:

1. not include any terms which would restrict public access; and
2. not include any terms which would directly or indirectly affect the construction servicing or occupation of the part of the Site that is retained by the Owners, and
3. include reservation of rights of access and services over the On-Site SUDS for the benefit of any other part of the Site for the purpose of laying managing maintaining replacing renewing cleaning and repairing services including but not limited to as applicable sustainable urban drainage measures, water, gas, sewerage, drainage or electricity subject to the provisions of paragraph 3.8 of this Schedule 4; and
4. include the grant of any and all rights reasonably necessary for the proposed end use of any adjoining land;
5. include for the benefit of the On-Site SUDS the grant of any rights of access over any adjoining land which rights are reasonably required for the management and maintenance of the On-Site SUDS for its purpose for public open space, water attenuation and surface water drainage purposes;
6. be at nil consideration; and
7. the transfer shall include a covenant that the On-Site SUDS shall not be used for any purpose other than for open space, water attenuation and surface water drainage purposes associated with the Development.

"On-Site SUDS" means surface water drainage measures/features which will be located within the Development and which shall include the following to serve the Development.

1. attenuation ponds and a surface water pumping station;
2. swales watercourses and ditches;
3. ponds and infiltration basins;
4. soakaways, and
5. all associated inlet/outlet structures, control
   devices, headwalls silt traps catchpits culverts
   and manholes

PROVIDED ALWAYS THAT for the purposes of the On-
Site SUDS Land Transfer(s) the On-Site SUDS shall
exclude any surface water drainage measures/features
located wholly within and only serving the adopted
highway

"On-Site SUDS Contribution" means:

  transferred to the County Council the sum of
  £1,015,967 (one million fifteen thousand nine
  hundred and sixty seven British pounds) to be
  paid to the County Council; and

2. in the event that the On-Site SUDS are to be
  transferred to the Alternative Body such sum as
  is agreed between the parties by reference to
  any statutory or local guidance in place at the
  time of the transfer to be paid to the Alternative
  Body

to be paid by the Owners towards the cost of the
management and maintenance of the On-Site SUDS and
On-Site SUDS Pond and replacement (as necessary) of
the pump for the on-site balancing pond;

"On-Site SUDS Plan"                means the plan marked "Indicative Locations - SW
                                   Pumping Stations, SUDS and Water Bodies" appended to
                                   this Agreement marked Plan 6;

"On-Site SUDS Land Transfer"                means the transfer of the:

1. unencumbered freehold interest in the land in
  the event that the transfer is to the County
  Council or;

2. unencumbered freehold or leasehold interest in
  the land in the event that the transfer is to the
  Alternative Body

in the form of the draft transfer appended hereto at
Appendix 12 or such other form as the parties may
otherwise agree relating to the transfer of the On-Site
SUDS and any Incidental Open Space by the Owners to
the County Council or the Alternative Body and each
party shall bear their own legal costs incurred in
connection with the negotiation and completion of such
transfer which shall nevertheless:

1. not include any terms which would restrict public
  access; and

2. not include any terms which would directly or
indirectly affect the construction servicing or
occupation of the part of the Site that is retained
by the Owners; and

3. include reservation of rights of access and
services over the On-Site SUDS for the benefit
of any other part of the Site for the purpose of
laying managing maintaining replacing renewing
cleaning and repairing services including but not
limited to as applicable sustainable urban
drainage measures, water, gas, sewerage,
drainage or electricity subject to the provisions
of paragraphs 3.8 and 3.9 of Part 1 of this
Schedule 4; and

4. include the grant of any rights reasonably
necessary for the proposed end use of any
adjoining land;

5. include for the benefit of the On-Site SUDS the
grant of any rights of access over any adjoining
land which rights are reasonably required for the
management and maintenance of the On-Site
SUDS for its purpose for public open space,
water attenuation and surface water drainage
purposes;

6. be at nil consideration, and

7. the transfer shall include a covenant that the
On-Site SUDS shall not be used for any
purpose other than for public open space, water
attenuation and/or surface water and ancillary
drainage purposes associated with the
Development.

"On Site SUDS
Maintenance Period" means:

1. in the event the On Site SUDS are to be
transferred to:

1.1 the County Council; or

1.2 an Alternative Body not being a statutory water
undertaker that is legally permitted to adopt own
or otherwise manage and maintain surface
water drainage features and measures; or

1.3 a Management Company; or

1.4 the Owners retains the On Site SUDS which are
then maintained by a Management Company

the period shall be up to 24 months from the date of the
relevant Council Completion Certificate or such shorter
period as a statutory undertaker that is legally permitted
to adopt or otherwise manage and maintain surface water
drainage features may reasonably require whether or not
the On-Site SUDS are transferred to that statutory
undertaker; or 2. in the event the On Site SUDS are to be transferred to a statutory water undertaker that is legally permitted to adopt or otherwise manage and maintain surface water drainage features and measures the period shall be up to 24 months from the date of the relevant Council Completion Certificate or such shorter period as agreed by that statutory undertaker.

"On Site SUDS
Specification" means a detailed specification to be prepared by or on behalf of the Owners by a suitably qualified and professionally approved person and which detailed specification shall include:
1. Hydraulic calculations
2. Details of all control devices and flow control measures;
3. Geotechnical design criteria;
4. Permeability assessment;
5. Proposed landscape treatment;
6. Proposals designed to minimise long term maintenance and siltation; and
7. Proposals designed to maximise ecological benefit;

"On Site SUDS Pond" means the pond to be provided as part of the On Site SUDS being the area which is contained within the maximum designed retained water level and which is shown indicatively on the On Site SUDS Plan.

## PART 1 - ON-SITE SUDS

1. The Owners shall lay out and construct the On-Site SUDS within the Site in accordance with details submitted and approved pursuant to planning condition 23 appended to the Planning Permission.

2. No later than Occupation of any Dwelling the Owners shall offer the On-Site SUDS Land Transfer to an Alternative Body PROVIDED THAT it shall notify the County Council of the same and such notification shall set out the name, address, company registration number (where appropriate) and evidence that the Alternative Body is constituted and this information shall be provided to the County Council in order that the County Council can make a decision whether or not to approve the Alternative Body a suitable entity or organisation as regards the management and maintenance of the On-Site SUDS SAVE THAT the County Council shall not be entitled to refuse to approve the Alternative Body if the Alternative Body proposed by the Owners is a statutory water authority that is legally permitted to adopt or otherwise manage and maintain surface water drainage features and measures PROVIDED FURTHER THAT the written offer of a transfer of the On-Site SUDS to the Alternative Body will not be permitted until such time as the Alternative Body has been approved or deemed approved by the County Council pursuant to the Approval Process AND PROVIDED FURTHER if the Alternative Body
accepts the offer of the On-Site SUDS the actual transfer will be completed when the
Council Final Certificate is issued AND PROVIDED FURTHER THAT if the Alternative
Body is a statutory water undertaker the actual transfer will be completed as soon as
practicable following the completion of the On-Site SUDSs and any On-Site SUDS
Maintenance Period as agreed between the Owners and the statutory water
undertaker.

3. Subject to paragraph 2 in the event that the Alternative Body declines in writing or
does not respond to the offer to have transferred to it the On Site SUDS within 40
Working Days of service of the offer to transfer the Owners shall serve written notice
on the County Council which contains one of the following options that the Owners
intend to pursue:

3.1        The written notice shall include an offer to transfer the On-Site SUDS to the County
Council pursuant to the On-Site SUDS Land Transfer PROVIDED THAT if the County
Council accepts the offer of the On-Site SUDS the actual transfer will be completed by
the parties following the issue of the Council Final Certificate.

3.2        The written notice will confirm that the Owners will either:

3.2.1    retain and maintain the On-Site SUDS and to appoint a Management
           Company to maintain and manage On-Site SUDS on the Owners' behalf; or

3.2.2    transfer the On-Site SUDS to the Management Company

PROVIDED THAT the Owners shall not be required to commit to which of these two
options in paragraph 3.2 the Owners will pursue at the date the written notice is
served and the Owners shall only be required to notify the County Council that they
intend to pursue an option involving a Management Company.

3.3        In the event that the County Council declines in writing or does not respond to the
offer to have transferred to it of the On-Site SUDS within 20 Working Days of service
of the respective offer to transfer the Owners shall:

3.3.1    Where the offer has been made to the County Council the Owners will notify
           the County Council in writing that it intends either to

* (a)      transfer the On-Site SUDS to a Management Company; or

* (b)      retain the On-Site SUDS and appoint a Management Company to
          manage and maintain the On-Site SUDS on the Owners' behalf

3.3.2    The Owners shall be entitled to transfer or otherwise deal with On-Site
           SUDS in accordance with the provisions of the notice served pursuant to
           paragraph 3.3.1 PROVIDED THAT a transfer to the Alternative Body will not
           be permitted until such time as the Alternative Body has been approved or
           deemed approved by the County Council pursuant to the Approval Process.

3.3.3    Where the offer has been made to an Alternative Body the Owners will notify
           the County Council in writing that it intends to either

* (a)      transfer the On-Site SUDS to a Management Company; or

* (b)      retain the On-Site SUDS and appoint a Management Company to
          manage and maintain the On-Site SUDS on the Owners' behalf

And the Owners shall be entitled to transfer or otherwise deal with the On-
Site SUDS in accordance with the provisions of the notice served pursuant
to this paragraph 3.3.3

3.4 In the event that the Alternative Body accepts the transfer of the On-Site SUDS the
    Owners shall offer to pay the On-Site SUDS Contribution to the Alternative Body and
    for the avoidance of doubt there shall thereafter be no contribution payable to the
    County Council pursuant to this Schedule PROVIDED THAT where the sum of the
    On-Site SUDS Contribution agreed between the Owners and the Alternative Body is
    less than the sum which would have otherwise been paid pursuant to paragraph 3.6
    below, the difference shall be transferred by the Owners into the Account set out in
    Schedule 14.

3.5 In the event the On-Site SUDS are to be managed and maintained by a Management
    Company then the Owners may elect to transfer the On-Site SUDS Contribution to the
    Management Company PROVIDED THAT where the Owners elect not to transfer or
    transfer only part of the On-Site SUDS Contribution to the Management Company,
    the difference between the sum which would have otherwise been paid pursuant to
    paragraph 3.6 below and the actual sum provided to the Management Company shall
    be transferred by the Owners into the Account set out in Schedule 14.

3.6 In the event that the County Council accepts the transfer of the On-Site SUDS the
    Owners shall pay the On-Site SUDS Contribution to the County Council to be used for
    the maintenance and management of the On-Site SUDS as follows:

3.6.1 £200,000 (two hundred thousand British pounds) will be paid to the County
        Council on transfer of the On-Site SUDS; and

3.6.2 £271,989 (two hundred and seventy one thousand pounds nine hundred and
        eighty nine British pounds) will be paid to the County Council on the fifth
        anniversary of the transfer of the On-Site SUDS; and

3.6.3 £271,989 (two hundred and seventy one thousand pounds nine hundred and
        eighty nine British pounds) will be paid to the County Council on the tenth
        anniversary of the transfer of the On-Site SUDS; and

3.6.4 £271,989 (two hundred and seventy one thousand pounds nine hundred and
        eighty nine British pounds) will be paid to the County Council on the fifteenth
        anniversary of the transfer of the On-Site SUDS.

AND FOR THE AVOIDANCE OF DOUBT there shall thereafter be no contribution
payable to an Alternative Body pursuant to this Schedule.

3.7 Prior to Commencement the On Site SUDs Specification shall be submitted to and
    approved by or deemed to have been approved by the County Council.

3.8 The Owners shall not locate any Utilities and/or Foul/Surface Water Drainage and
    Sewerage in under or over the On Site SUDs Pond.

3.9 Subject to paragraph 3.8 above and for the avoidance of doubt the Owners may locate
    any Utilities and/or Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage in under or over any
    other part of the On Site SUDs Land.

## PART 2 - COVENANTS RELATING TO LAYING OUT AND CERTIFICATION

1. In the event the On-Site SUDS are to be transferred to an Alternative Body that is a
    statutory water authority then the provisions of paragraphs 2 3 and 4 of this Part 2
    shall not apply.

2. The Owners shall:

2.1 provide the County Council with written notification that it intends to supply the
    Certificate of Practical Completion in respect of the On-Site SUDS no less than 20
Working Days before supplying it in accordance with paragraph 2.2 of this Part 2 of this Schedule 4; and

2.2 supply the County Council with a Certificate of Practical Completion in respect of the On-Site SUDS at any time following Completion of the On-Site SUDS.

2.3 serve notice on the County Council inviting them to inspect the On-Site SUDS and issue a Council Completion Certificate confirming that such works have been completed to their reasonable satisfaction;

2.4 if the County Council chooses to inspect the On-Site SUDS and identifies necessary remedial works, complete such remedial works to the reasonable satisfaction of the County Council as soon as reasonably practicable;

2.5 upon completion of any remedial works, serve notice on the County Council inviting them to inspect the remedial works identified by them pursuant to paragraph 2.4 of this Part of this Schedule and issue a Council Completion Certificate confirming that the On-Site SUDS have been completed to their reasonable satisfaction,

PROVIDED THAT if the County Council fails to inspect the On-Site SUDS within 40 Working Days of receipt of the notice of invitation from the Owners or fails to issue a Council Completion Certificate within 40 Working Days of the inspection where no remedial works have been identified then the Council Completion Certificate shall be deemed to have been issued at the end of those specified periods PROVIDED FURTHER THAT the inspection procedure identified in paragraphs 2.4 and 2.5 shall be repeated until such time as the County Council issue a Council Completion Certificate or a Council Completion Certificate shall been deemed to have been issued in relation to the On-Site SUDS.

3. Following issue or deemed issue of a Council Completion Certificate in respect of the On-Site SUDS the Owners shall maintain the On-Site SUDS for the On Site SUDS Maintenance Period to the reasonable satisfaction of the County Council.

4. On expiration of the On Site SUDS Maintenance Period the Owners shall:

4.1 serve notice on the County Council inviting them to inspect the On-Site SUDS and issue a Council Final Certificate confirming that such works have been maintained to their reasonable satisfaction;

4.2 if the County Council chooses to inspect the On-Site SUDS and identifies necessary remedial works, to complete such remedial works to the reasonable satisfaction of the County Council as soon as reasonably practicable;

4.3 upon completion of any remedial works, to serve notice on the County Council inviting them to inspect the remedial works identified by them pursuant to paragraph 4.2 and issue a Council Final Certificate confirming that the On-Site SUDS have been maintained to the reasonable satisfaction of the County Council.

PROVIDED THAT if the County Council fails to inspect the On-Site SUDS within 40 Working Days of invitation from the Owners or fails to issue a Council Final Certificate within 40 Working Days of the inspection where no remedial works have been identified then the Council Final Certificate shall be deemed to have been issued at the end of those specified periods PROVIDED FURTHER THAT the inspection procedure identified in paragraphs 4.2 and 4.3 shall be repeated until such time as the County Council issue a Council Final Certificate or a Council Final Certificate shall been deemed to have been issued in relation to the On-Site SUDS.

5. The Owners shall properly maintain the On-Site SUDS or any part thereof in accordance with:

5.1 the On Site SUDs Specification; and

5.2 the details submitted and approved pursuant to planning condition 23 appended to the Planning Permission; and

5.3 in any event to ensure that the existing Awards Drains operated by SCDC are not materially adversely affected.
unless and until such time that the On-Site SUDS Land Transfer or the Alternative Body or Management Company who shall thereafter become responsible for the maintenance of the On-Site SUDS PROVIDED THAT transfer of the On-Site SUDS may be completed on a phased basis following the issue of Council Final Certificates in relation to such part of the On-Site SUDS.

# SCHEDULE 5 - OFF SITE FLOOD MITIGATION WORKS

## 1 RELEVANT DEFINITIONS:

"Drainage Upgrade and Maintenance Contribution" means the sum of £46,500 (forty six thousand and five hundred British pounds) payable as a commuted sum which shall be used for the Drainage Works Purposes;

"Drainage Works Purposes" means the works for the upgrade and/or maintenance of surface water drains in connection with the management and maintenance of the Awards Drains within and adjacent to the Site or other Awards Drains that are impacted by the Development to the extent that management and/or maintenance is reasonably required to them;

"Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land" means the areas shown on the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land Plan in which the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works will be located;

"Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land Plan" means the plan appended to this Agreement marked Plan 8;

"Off Site Flood Mitigation Works" means two balancing ponds off Hatton's Road which provide off-site flood mitigation in accordance with NAAP Policy NS/21 and comprising the Off Site Flood Mitigation 1st Works, the Off Site Flood Mitigation 2nd Works and the Off Site Flood Mitigation 3rd Works

"Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Contribution" means the sum of £31,500 (thirty one thousand five hundred British pounds) to be paid by the Owners to SCDC towards the cost of the management and maintenance of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works;

"Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land Transfer" means the transfer:

1. to SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC and if such Town Council is constituted at the relevant time) of the unencumbered freehold interest in the form of the draft transfer appended hereto at Appendix 13 ("the Council form of Transfer") or such other form as the parties may otherwise agree relating to the transfer of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land (or relevant parts thereof as per the arrangements set out in this Schedule where the whole of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land is not to be the subject of a single transfer); or

2. to the Alternative Body of the unencumbered freehold interest in the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works (or relevant parts thereof) and including any associated Incidental Open Space agreed with the Alternative Body as per the arrangements set out in this Schedule where the whole of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land is not to be the subject of a single transfer) and the form of transfer will be agreed between
the Owners and the Alternative Body provided that the final form of Transfer shall be approved or deemed approved by SCDC,

and each party shall bear their own legal costs incurred in connection with the negotiation and completion of such transfer which shall nevertheless

1.  not include any terms which would directly or indirectly affect the construction servicing or occupation of the part of the Site that is retained by the Owners; and

2.  include reservation of rights of access and services over the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land for the benefit of any other part of the Site or other land adjacent to the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land that is retained by the Owners for the purpose of laying managing maintaining replacing renewing cleaning and repairing services including but not limited to as applicable sustainable urban drainage measures, water, gas, sewerage, drainage or electricity subject to the provisions of paragraphs 3.10 and 3.11 of Part 1 of this schedule 5; and

3.  include the grant of any and all rights reasonably necessary for the proposed end use of any adjoining land;

4.  include for the benefit of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land the grant of any and all reasonable rights of access over any adjoining land that is retained by the Owners which rights are reasonably required for the management and maintenance of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land for its purpose for water attenuation and surface water drainage purposes;

5.  be at nil consideration; and

6.  the transfer shall include a covenant that the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land shall not be used for any purpose other than for water attenuation and surface water drainage purposes associated with the Development and which may include any and all purposes reasonably ancillary thereto.

“Off Site Flood Mitigation 1st Works” means that part of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works being the area which forms the “Awards Drains” in such location and which is shown indicatively on the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Plan and which will be identified precisely as part of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Specification;

“Off Site Flood Mitigation 2nd Works” means the pond and by-pass channel(s) to be provided as part of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works being the area which is contained within the maximum designed
retained water level and which is shown indicatively on
the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Plan and which will be
identified precisely as part of the Off Site Flood Mitigation
Works Specification;

"Off Site Flood Mitigation
3rd Works "
means the balance of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works
which are not part of the Off Site Flood Mitigation 1st
Works and the Off Site Flood Mitigation 2nd Works and
which is shown indicatively on the Off Site Flood
Mitigation Works Plan and which will be identified
precisely as part of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works
Specification

"Off Site Flood Mitigation
Works Specification"
means a detailed specification to be prepared by or on
behalf of the Owners by a suitably qualified and
professionally approved person and which detailed
specification shall include:

1. Hydraulic calculations;
2. Details of control devices and flow control
   measures;
3. Geotechnical design criteria;
4. Permeability assessment;
5. Proposed landscape treatment;
6. Proposals designed to minimise long term
   maintenance;
7. Proposals designed to maximise ecological
   benefit; and
8. Any other design feature(s) as reasonably
   required to ensure the effective operation of the
   Off Site Flood Mitigation Works

## PART 1 – OFF SITE FLOOD MITIGATION WORKS

1. The Owners shall:

1.1 lay out construct and complete the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works prior to the
    Occupation of 800 Dwellings or if earlier within 24 months of the substantive
    commencement of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works and in either case complete in
    accordance with the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Specification and the details
    submitted and approved pursuant to planning condition 26 appended to the Planning
    Permission.

1.2 provide SCDC with written notification that it intends to serve a written notice offering
    SCDC a right of first refusal for SCDC (or the Town Council on the nomination of
    SCDC if such Town Council is constituted at the relevant time) to take a transfer of the
    Off Site Flood Mitigation Works or part thereof and such notification shall be served no
    less than 60 Working Days before supplying a notice served in accordance with
    paragraph 1.3 of this Part 1 of this Schedule 5.

1.3 serve a written notice offering SCDC a right of first refusal for SCDC (or the Town
    Council on the nomination of SCDC if such Town Council is constituted at the relevant
    time) to take a transfer of whichever it chooses of the following:

1.3.1 the Off Site Flood Mitigation 1st Works; or

1.3.2 the Off Site Flood Mitigation 1st Works and the Off Site Flood Mitigation 2nd Works; or

1.3.3 the Off Site Flood Mitigation 1st Works and the Off Site Flood Mitigation 2nd Works and the Off Site Flood Mitigation 3rd Works.

2. SCDC covenants with the Owners that it will within 20 Working Days of receipt of a notice served pursuant to paragraph 1.3 above accept one of the offers referred to in paragraph 1.3 above or reject/decline each of such offers PROVIDED THAT SCDC shall not be required to complete the Off-Site Flood Mitigation Works Land Transfer until such time as the Council Final Certificate relevant to the Off-Site Flood Mitigation Works has been issued or deemed to have been issued.

3. In the event that SCDC or the Town Council (if so nominated by SCDC) declines in writing or does not respond to the offer to have transferred to it the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land (or a relevant part or parts thereof) within 20 Working Days of service of the offer to transfer then the Owners shall serve written notice on SCDC which contains one of the following options that the Owners intend to pursue:

3.1 The written notice shall notify SCDC that the Owners wish to offer the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land to an Alternative Body PROVIDED THAT in such notification to set out the name, address, company registration number (where appropriate) and evidence that the Alternative Body is constituted and this information shall be provided to SCDC in order that SCDC can make a decision whether or not to approve the Alternative Body a suitable entity or organisation as regards the management and maintenance of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land SAVE THAT SCDC shall not be entitled to refuse to approved the Alternative Body if the Alternative Body proposed by the Owners is a statutory water authority that is legally permitted to adopt own or otherwise manage and maintain surface water drainage features and measures PROVIDED FURTHER THAT the written offer of a transfer of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land to the Alternative Body will not be permitted until such time as the Alternative Body has been approved or deemed approved by SCDC pursuant to the Approval Process AND PROVIDED FURTHER if the Alternative Body accepts the offer of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land the actual transfer will be completed when the Council Final Certificate is issued; or

3.2 The written notice will confirm that the Owners will either:

3.2.1 retain and maintain the freehold interest in the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land and to appoint a Management Company to maintain and manage Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land on the Owners' behalf; or

3.2.2 transfer a leasehold interest of not more than 10 years in the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land to the Management Company subject to paragraph 3.8 of Part 1 of Schedule 5 below

PROVIDED THAT the Owners shall not be required to commit to which of these two options in this paragraph 3.2 the Owners will pursue at the date the written notice is served and the Owners shall only be required to notify SCDC that they intend to pursue an option involving a Management Company PROVIDED always that the which of these options is being chosen within 12 months of the Certificate of Practical Completion.

3.3 In the event of 3.2.1 of Part 1 of this Schedule 5 but where the Alternative Body declines in writing or does not respond to the offer to have transferred to it the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land within 20 Working Days of service of the respective offer to transfer the Owners shall either:

3.3.1 transfer a leasehold interest of not more than 10 years in the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land to a Management Company subject to paragraph 3.8 of Part 1 of this Schedule 5 below.

3.3.2 retain the freehold interest in the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land and appoint a Management Company to manage and maintain the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land on the Owners' behalf.

3.4 The Owners shall be entitled to transfer or otherwise deal with the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land in accordance with the provisions of the notice served pursuant to paragraph 3.2 of Part 1 of this Schedule 5 PROVIDED THAT a transfer to the Alternative Body will not be permitted until such time as the Alternative Body has been approved or deemed approved by SCDC pursuant to the Approval Process.

3.5 Where paragraphs 3.3.1 or 3.3.2 of this Part 1 of Schedule 5 applies and the Owners have notified SCDC in writing that it intends to either

3.5.1 transfer the leasehold interest in the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land to a Management Company; or

3.5.2 retain the freehold interest in the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land and appoint a Management Company to manage and maintain the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works on the Owners' behalf

the Owners shall be entitled to transfer or otherwise deal with the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works in accordance with the provisions of the notice served pursuant to paragraphs 3.3.1 or 3.3.2 of this Part 1 of Schedule 5 subject to paragraph 3.8 of this Part 1 of Schedule 5 below.

3.6 In the event of a transfer of a leasehold interest to a Management Company the Owners shall be under an obligation to use all Reasonable Endeavours to complete such leasehold transfer as soon as reasonably possible following the date of appointment of the Management Company and if within the first 6 months of the said appointment of the Management Company the leasehold transfer has not been completed the Owners shall, if requested, be required subject to SCDC acting reasonably in making any such request, to provide evidence to demonstrate why there is any continuing delay in completion of the leasehold interest being transferred and for the avoidance of doubt the Owners shall thereafter continue to be under an obligation to use all Reasonable Endeavours to complete the leasehold transfer as soon as reasonably possible

3.7 In the event that SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) accepts the transfer of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land including at least the Off Site Flood Mitigation 1st Works and the Off Site Flood Mitigation 2nd Works the Owners shall pay the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Contribution to SCDC on completion of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works to be used for the maintenance and management of such part of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works.
