3.8 In the event that a Management Company is appointed by the Owners to manage the Mitigation Works or the Management Company takes a transfer of a leasehold interest in the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land then within a period of 10 years from the date a Management Company is appointed by the Owners or the leasehold transfer to the Management Company is Completed the following shall apply:

3.8.1 then the Owners shall offer to transfer all of the freehold interests that it retains in the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Land to SCDC or if SCDC so nominates the Town Council provided that the Town Council has been constituted and has the ability to accept a transfer of the land for nil
consideration pursuant to the relevant Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Transfer; and

3.8.2 in the event the SCDC (or if applicable the Town Council) has not accepted the offer within 60 Working Days then the offer shall be treated as withdrawn and the Owners shall be permitted to transfer all of the freehold interests that it retains in the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works to the Management Company

PROVIDED ALWAYS THAT nothing shall compel the Owners to terminate an existing lease other than in accordance with any pre-existing break clause within that lease AND FOR THE AVOIDANCE OF DOUBT nothing within this paragraph shall require the Owners to incur any expense in transferring the freehold interests to SCDC or if SCDC so nominates the Town Council save that the Owners and SCDC or if applicable the Town Council shall bear their own legal and administrative costs in relation to the transfer of the freehold interests.

3.9 Prior to Occupation of any Dwellings the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Specification shall be submitted to and approved by or deemed to have been approved by SCDC.

3.10 The Owners shall not locate any Utilities and/or Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage in under or over the Off Site Flood Mitigation 2nd Works and/or the Off Site Flood Mitigation 1st Works.

3.11 Subject to paragraph 3.10 above and for the avoidance of doubt the Owners may locate any Utilities and/or Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage in under or over any other part of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works.

## PART 2 - COVENANTS RELATING TO LAYING OUT AND CERTIFICATION

1. The Owners shall:

1.1 provide SCDC with written notification that it intends to supply a Certificate of Practical Completion in respect of all the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works or specified parts thereof no less than 20 Working Days before supplying it in accordance with paragraph 1.2 of this Part 2 of Schedule 5; and

1.2 supply SCDC with a Certificate of Practical Completion in respect of all the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works or specified parts thereof as soon as reasonably practical following Completion of the relevant part of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works; and

1.3 serve notice on SCDC inviting them to inspect all the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works or specified parts thereof and issue a Council Completion Certificate confirming that such works have been completed to their reasonable satisfaction; and

1.4 if SCDC chooses to inspect the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works and identifies necessary remedial works, complete such remedial works at its own cost to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC as soon as reasonably practicable; and

1.5 upon completion of any remedial works, serve notice on SCDC inviting them to inspect the remedial works identified by them pursuant to paragraph 1.4 of this Part 2 of this Schedule 5 and issue a Council Completion Certificate confirming that all the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works or specified parts thereof have been completed to their reasonable satisfaction.

PROVIDED THAT if SCDC fails to inspect the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works within 40 Working Days of receipt of the notice of invitation from the Owners or fails to issue a Council Completion Certificate within 40 Working Days of the inspection where no remedial works have been identified then the Council Completion Certificate shall be deemed to have been issued at the end of those specified periods PROVIDED
FURTHER THAT the inspection procedure identified in paragraphs 1.3 to 1.5 of this Part 2 of Schedule 5 shall be repeated until such time as SCDC issue a Council Completion Certificate or a Council Completion Certificate shall be deemed to have been issued in relation to the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works.

2. Following issue or deemed issue of a Council Completion Certificate in respect of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works the Owners shall maintain:

2.1 the Off Site Flood Mitigation 1st Works and the Off Site Flood Mitigation 2nd Works for a maintenance period of 1 year; and

2.2 the Off Site Flood Mitigation 3rd Works for a maintenance period of 2 years

and in all cases the maintenance is to be to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC.

3. On expiration of

3.1 the Maintenance Period for the Off Site Flood Mitigation 1st Works and the Off Site Flood Mitigation 2nd Works, and

3.2 the maintenance period of 2 years for the Off Site Flood Mitigation 3rd Works

the Owners shall:

3.3 in each case serve notice on SCDC inviting them to inspect the relevant parts of Off Site Flood Mitigation Works specified in the notice and issue a Council Final Certificate confirming that such works have been maintained to their reasonable satisfaction;

3.4 if SCDC chooses to inspect the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works specified in the notice and identifies necessary remedial works, complete such remedial works to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC as soon as reasonably practicable;

3.5 upon completion of any remedial works, serve notice on SCDC inviting them to inspect the remedial works identified by them pursuant to paragraph 3.4 of this Part 2 of this Schedule 5 and issue a Council Final Certificate confirming that the relevant part of Off Site Flood Mitigation Works have been maintained to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC.

PROVIDED THAT if SCDC fails to inspect the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works within 40 Working Days of invitation from the Owners or fails to issue a Council Final Certificate within 40 Working Days of the inspection where no remedial works have been identified then the Council Final Certificate shall be deemed to have been issued at the end of those specified periods PROVIDED FURTHER THAT the inspection procedure identified in paragraphs 3.3 to 3.5 of this Part 2 of this Schedule 5 shall be repeated until such time as SCDC issue a Council Final Certificate or a Council Final Certificate shall be deemed to have been issued in relation to the relevant parts of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works.

4. The Owners shall maintain the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works in accordance with:

4.1 the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works Specification; and

4.2 the details submitted and approved pursuant to planning condition 26 appended to the Planning Permission; and

4.3 in any event to ensure that the existing Awards Drains operated by SCDC are not materially adversely affected
PROVIDED THAT transfer of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works may be completed
on a phased basis of no more than 3 (three) phases (unless otherwise agreed
between the Owners and SCDC) following the issue of Council Final Certificates in
relation to such relevant part(s) of the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works.

## PART 3 - DRAINAGE UPGRADE AND MAINTENANCE CONTRIBUTION

1. At any time after Commencement SCDC may notify the Owners in writing of the works
to be undertaken for the Drainage Works Purposes and the Owners shall pay the
Drainage Upgrade and Maintenance Contribution on the latter of receipt of service of
the said notice on the Owners or Occupation of any Dwelling and such contribution is
to be used by SCDC for the Drainage Works Purposes only.

# SCHEDULE 6 - TRANSPORT INCLUDING THE CAMBRIDGESHIRE GUIDED BUSWAY

## RELEVANT DEFINITIONS

"Annual Transport
Monitoring Contribution"
means the annual payment of a sum of £10,000 (ten thousand British pounds) to be paid by the Owners on seven occasions as provided for in this Schedule to the County Council on an annual basis for monitoring the transport effects of the Development through:

1.  downloading traffic count data from the proposed permanent traffic counters, and
2.  undertaking infill traffic counts at other sites; and
3.  other forms of traffic surveys if agreed at the Northstowe Transport Working Group;

"Bus Funding
Contribution"
means a sum of £1,320,000 (one million three hundred and twenty thousand British pounds) to be paid in instalments by the Owners to the County Council as revenue support for the Enhancement Service;

"Capital Transport
Monitoring Measures
Contribution"
means the sum of £860,000 (eight hundred and sixty thousand British pounds) to be paid in instalments as provided for in this Schedule by the Owners to the County Council for investment into capital works and equipment to facilitate additional traffic monitoring, traffic calming, traffic management and highway improvement measures for the Transport Monitoring Measures Area;

"CGB Capital Funding
Contribution"
means the sum of £2,380,500 (two million three hundred and eight thousand and five hundred British pounds) to be paid in instalments by the Owners to the County Council towards the cost of implementing the Cambridgeshire Guided Busway scheme and for no other purpose;

"Cambridgeshire Guided
Busway"
means the public transport scheme which forms part of the transport corridor consisting of road improvements bus lanes and bus priority lanes of which 16 miles (25 km) is dedicated for the use of the Guided Busway. The Guided Busway connects the population centres of Cambridge Huntingdon and St. Ives in Cambridgeshire;

"Community Transport
Contribution"
means a total sum of £80,000 (eighty thousand British pounds) to be paid in instalments by the Owners to the County Council as revenue and capital support for the Community Transport Service;

"Community Transport
Service"
means a service or network of transport services capable of providing essential journeys for those who have difficulty accessing conventional public transport. These shall include (but are not limited to) Dial-a-Ride services,
voluntary car schemes and community bus schemes;

"Construction
Environmental
Management Plan(s)"
means the various site-wide and Strategic Engineering
Element(s) plans and the information to be contained
within such plans as are referred to in planning condition
31 imposed by the Planning Permission;

"CEMP Cost Cap"
means the sum of £100,000 (one hundred thousand
British pounds) being the combined cost of implementing
the various elements, works, measures, assessments
procedures arrangements, actions and other provisions
contained with the Construction Environmental
Management Plans approved pursuant to planning
condition 31 imposed by the Planning Permission which
for the avoidance of doubt will include the CEMP
Contribution if payable;

"CEMP Contribution"
means the sum of £100,000 (one hundred thousand
British pounds) which may be paid by the Owners to
SCDC in accordance with this schedule;

"Off Site Footpath and
Cycleway Contribution"
means the total sum of £932,000 (nine hundred and thirty
two thousand British pounds) which shall be paid
instalments by the Owners to the County Council for the
purpose of the Off Site Footpath and Cycleway Works
and for no other purpose

"Off Site Footpath and
Cycleway Works"
means works to create a new cycleway and footway from
Longstanton to Bar Hill alongside the B1050 which shall
include cycle lanes, signage and traffic free routes to be
provided by the County Council;

"Busway Link Road"
means the unguided road (which shall be for the sole use
of scheduled local service buses) to be constructed by
the Owners at its sole cost and to a specification to be
approved by the County Council which shall connect the
bus only road (as built) within the Longstanton Park and
Ride site to the dedicated busway route provided through
the Site pursuant to condition 19;

"Busway Link Road
Contribution"
means the sum of £100,000 (One Hundred Thousand
Pounds) to be paid by the Owners in accordance with this
Schedule 6;

"Enhancement Service"
means the Northstowe bus service which runs between
Cambridge and Bar Hill and which shall be diverted to
serve the Development upon first Occupation of 50
Dwellings;

"Framework Travel
Plan(s)"
means the framework travel plans being the Residential
Framework Travel Plan, Workplace Framework Travel
Plan and School Framework Travel Plan that have been
submitted by or on behalf of the Owners as part of the
Application and which have already been approved by the
County Council in consultation with SCDC;

"Highways Agreement" means an agreement under section 38 and/or section 278 of the Highways Act 1980 or as otherwise agreed between the Owners and the County Council;

"Parcel Travel Plan" means the travel plans for each separate Development Parcel to be submitted by or on behalf of the Owners for approval by the County Council in consultation with SCDC which shall be consistent with the Framework Travel Plan(s) relevant to the uses within that Development Parcel;

"Street Furniture Cost Cap" means the sum of £30,000 (thirty thousand) British pounds) being the combined cost of implementing the various elements associated with the design and/or provision of street furniture as set out in the Street Signs and Furniture Specification;

"Street Furniture Specification" means a detailed specification to be prepared by or on behalf of the Owners by a suitably qualified and professionally approved person and which detailed specification shall include details of type form location and installation details for all street benches waymarking refuse bins bollards and other elements of street furniture deemed necessary within the proposed highway PROVIDED THAT the specification shall not require or otherwise specify standards or materials associated with the design and/or provision of the street furniture that would result in a total cost that would exceed the Street Signs and Furniture Cost Cap;

"Transport Monitoring Measures Area" means the area shown edged red on the plan attached to this Agreement marked Plan 10;

"Travel Plan Co-ordinator Contribution" means the sum of £75,000 (seventy five thousand British pounds) which shall be used for the appointment and employment of a Travel Plan Co-ordinator;

"Travel Plan Contribution Purpose" means an assessment of the Parcel Travel Plan, dealing with the Travel Plan Co-ordinator on the approval and implementation of the final travel plan for the particular Development Parcel and the receipt and consideration of monitoring data and tracking of actions supplied by the Travel Plan Co-ordinator

"Travel Plan Co-ordinator" means the person who shall be responsible for implementing monitoring and reviewing the approved Parcel Travel Plan liaising with the County Council where necessary and for the avoidance of doubt the Travel Plan Co-ordinator shall not be responsible for the preparation of any of the travel plans;

"Travel Plan Cost Cap" means the sum of £175,000 (one hundred and seventy five thousand British pounds).

"Travel Plan Steering
Group Costs Cap" means the sum of £2,500 (two thousand five hundred
British pounds) which for the avoidance of doubt is
included within the Travel Plan Cost Cap.

### 1. CGB CAPITAL FUNDING CONTRIBUTION

1.1 The Owners will provide a Bond or Bonds for each of the payments due in paragraph
1.2.1 to 1.2.3 in a form approved by the County Council to guarantee the payments
due under paragraph 1.2.1 to 1.2.3 and each Bond or part thereof shall be placed no
later than 20 Working Days prior to the relevant sum falling due for payment pursuant
to this Agreement.

1.2 The Owners shall pay the CGB Capital Funding Contribution to the County Council by
way of the following instalments:

1.2.1 £380,500 (three hundred and eighty thousand and five hundred British
pounds) of the total CGB Capital Funding Contribution prior to first
Occupation of the 200th Dwelling;

1.2.2 £1,000,000 (one million British pounds) of the total CGB Capital Funding
Contribution prior to Occupation of the 700th Dwelling; and

1.2.3 £1,000,000 (one million British pounds) of the total CGB Capital Funding
Contribution prior to Occupation of the 1400th Dwelling.

1.3 Following each and every payment by the Owners pursuant to 1.2.1 to 1.2.3 the Bond
or element of the Bond provided in respect of that sum shall be discharged or reduced
accordingly and will be discharged absolutely on the date of payment of the final
instalment pursuant to paragraph 1.2.3.

### 2. BUS FUNDING CONTRIBUTION AND COMMUNITY TRANSPORT CONTRIBUTION

2.1 The Owners shall pay the Bus Funding Contribution to the County Council for the
purposes of supporting and providing the Enhancement Service as follows:

2.1.1 £50,000 (fifty thousand British pounds) prior to first Occupation of the 50th
Dwelling;

2.1.2 £50,000 (fifty thousand British pounds) on the first anniversary of the date of
first Occupation of the 50th Dwelling;

2.1.3 £280,000 (two hundred and eighty thousand British pounds) on the second
anniversary of the date of first Occupation of the 50th Dwelling;

2.1.4 £280,000 (two hundred and eighty thousand British pounds) on the third
anniversary of the date of first Occupation of the 50th Dwelling;

2.1.5 £280,000 (two hundred and eighty thousand British pounds) on the fourth
anniversary of the date of first Occupation of the 50th Dwelling;

2.1.6 £280,000 (two hundred and eighty thousand British pounds) on the fifth
anniversary of the date of first Occupation of the 50th Dwelling;

2.1.7 £100,000 (one hundred thousand British pounds) on the sixth anniversary of
the date of first Occupation of the 50th Dwelling.

2.2 In the event at any time the Enhancement Service (or such other service as may be deemed to replace it from time to time) becomes financially viable in its own right the Owners shall no longer be required to comply with the outstanding payment obligations pursuant to paragraph 2.1 above.

2.3 The Owners shall pay the Community Transport Contribution to the County Council for the purposes of providing the Community Transport Service as follows:

2.3.1 £10,000 (ten thousand British pounds) prior to Occupation of the first Dwelling;

2.3.2 £10,000 (ten thousand British pounds) on the first anniversary and subsequent anniversaries of the date of Occupation of the first Dwelling with the final instalment of £10,000 (ten thousand British pounds) falling due on the seventh anniversary of the date of Occupation of the first Dwelling.

2.4 The County Council shall or shall procure that the bus operator shall provide to the Owners in writing details of the costs, revenue and patronage figures as follows:

2.4.1 for the Enhancement Service and the Community Transport Service annually commencing from the date of Occupation of the 50th Dwelling until the sixth anniversary of the date of the Occupation of the 50th Dwelling; and

2.4.2 where the Enhancement Service becomes financially viable in its own right for a period of not less than 6 (six) successive months the County Council shall or shall procure that the bus operators notify the Owners in writing as soon as possible after the information as regards financial viability becomes available to the bus operators and in any event no later than 20 Working Days before payment of the next instalment of the Community Transport Funding Contribution that may fall due to be paid.

PROVIDED ALWAYS that the County Council will use Reasonable Endeavours to secure compliance and performance of this obligation notwithstanding it may request that the bus operator provide the details referred to above.

### 3. CAPITAL TRANSPORT MONITORING MEASURES CONTRIBUTION

3.1 Subject to paragraphs 3.2 and 3.3 the Owners shall pay the Capital Transport Monitoring Measures Contribution to the County Council as follows:

3.1.1 £60,000 shall be paid on the date 3 months after the grant of Planning Permission;

3.1.2 £267,000 (two hundred and sixty seven thousand British pounds) shall be paid on Occupation of the 300th Dwelling;

3.1.3 £267,000 (two hundred and sixty seven thousand British pounds) shall be paid on Occupation of the 900th Dwelling;

3.1.4 £266,000 (two hundred and sixty six thousand British pounds) shall be paid on Occupation of the 1400th Dwelling.

PROVIDED THAT in the event the County Council serves a written notice on the Owners requesting payment of the Bus Link Road Contribution then the Owners shall within 90 Working Days of deemed receipt of such a notice pay the Bus Link Road Contribution to the County Council AND PROVIDED FURTHER THAT the Owners shall be released from its obligation in this paragraph 3.1 of paying £100,000 of the Capital Transport Monitoring Measures Contribution to the County Council and the next instalment that would otherwise fall due to be paid under this paragraph 3.1 following service of the notice by the County Council shall be treated as discharged in
full by the payment of the Bus Link Road Contribution and the second instalment that would otherwise fail due to be paid under this paragraph 3.1 following service of the notice by the County Council shall be reduced by a sum being the difference between the Bus Link Road Contribution and the first instalment that shall be treated as discharged in full.

3.2 Save for the payment made pursuant to paragraph 3.1.1 the payments referred to in paragraphs 3.1.2, 3.1.3 and 3.1.4 shall only fail due if the County Council shall have first provided written evidence to the Owners that:

3.2.1 the proposed mitigation works are solely the direct result of Phase 1 of the Northstowe development within the Transport Monitoring Measures Area; and

3.2.2 it has entered into a binding contract with a reputable contractor to carry out additional traffic calming and highway improvements necessary to mitigate the increased traffic impact on the surrounding road network directly attributable to the Development; and

3.2.3 it has obtained all relevant consents permissions and authorisations required for the implementation of such traffic calming traffic management and highway improvements.

3.3 In the event the County Council shall not have provided the evidence referred to in paragraph 3.2 above by the respective due dates the Owners shall not be obliged to make the payments referred to in paragraph 3.1 until such time as the said evidence has been provided and on receipt of such evidence the Owners shall pay the relevant contribution referred to in paragraph 3.1 no later than 40 Working Days after receipt of the said evidence.

### 4. ANNUAL TRANSPORT MONITORING CONTRIBUTION

4.1 The Owners shall pay to the County Council:

4.1.1 the first Annual Transport Monitoring Contribution on the date 3 months after the grant of Planning Permission; and

4.1.2 thereafter the Annual Transport Monitoring Contribution on the first anniversary and subsequent anniversaries of the date of Occupation of the first Dwelling with the final Annual Transport Monitoring Contribution falling due on the sixth anniversary of the date of Occupation of the first Dwelling.

### 5. STREET FURNITURE

5.1 Prior to Occupation of any Dwellings the Street Furniture Specification shall be submitted to and approved by or deemed to have been approved by SCDC.

5.2 The Owners shall provide and install the street furniture in accordance with the Street Furniture Specification approved or deemed to have been approved in accordance with paragraph 5.1.

5.3 Prior to the earlier of the following events occurring:

5.3.1 Occupation of more than 75% of the Dwellings located on any street or road within a Development Phase; or

5.3.2 Occupation of more than 25% of the Dwellings within the Development Phase.
the Owners shall provide and install all of the street furniture as is referred to in the approved or deemed approved Street Furniture Specification relevant to the Development Parcel to which the aforementioned Occupations relate.

5.4 For the avoidance of doubt the Owners shall provide and install adequate street signage on any street prior to Occupation of that street and for the avoidance of doubt this provision shall apply whether that street is or is proposed to be adopted highway or a private street.

### 6. OFF-SITE FOOTPATH AND CYCLEWAY CONTRIBUTION

6.1 The Owners will provide a Bond or Bonds for each of the payments due in paragraphs 6.2.1 to 6.2.3 to guarantee the payments due under paragraphs 6.2.1 to 6.2.3 and each Bond or part thereof shall be placed no later than 20 Working Days prior to the relevant sum falling due for payment pursuant to this Agreement PROVIDED ALWAYS in relation to the payments required pursuant to paragraphs 6.2.2 and 6.2.3 the Bond shall not be required to be put in place until receipt of the notice referred to in those paragraphs.

6.2 The Owners shall pay the Off-Site Footpath and Cycleway Contribution to the County Council as follows:

6.2.1 £50,000 (fifty thousand British pounds) on Commencement;

6.2.2 £441,000 (four hundred and forty one thousand British pounds) is to be paid within 30 Working Days of receipt by the Owners of written evidence from the County Council that an award of the construction contract for the Off Site Footpath and Cycleway Works has occurred; and

6.2.3 £441,000 (four hundred and forty one thousand British pounds) is to be paid within 80 Working Days of receipt by the Owners of written evidence from the County Council that an award of the construction contract for the Off Site Footpath and Cycleway Works has occurred.

6.3 Following each and every payment by the Owners pursuant to 6.2.1 to 6.2.3 the Bond or element of the Bond provided in respect of that sum shall be discharged or reduced accordingly and will be discharged absolutely on the date of payment of the final instalment pursuant to paragraph 6.2.3.

### 7. TRAVEL PLAN

7.1 Prior to the Occupation of any Dwelling the Owners shall undertake the following in respect of the Framework Travel Plans:

7.1.1 Create website pages to publicise the aims of the Framework Travel Plans;

7.1.2 Provide pedestrian and cycle route maps that show linkages to the wider existing public rights of way and cyclepath network; and

7.1.3 On written request provide the Travel Plan Steering Group with practical and technical support in terms of graphics and printing up to the maximum cost of the Travel Plan Steering Group Costs Cap save that this obligation shall not be interpreted to extend to requiring the Owners to pay any monies from the Travel Plan Steering Group Costs Cap to the Travel Plan Steering Group

PROVIDED THAT the obligations in this paragraph 7.1 shall cease to apply on the date that the first Dwelling is Occupied.

7.2 The Owners shall or shall procure that:

7.2.1 Commencement of any Development Parcel shall not take place until the Parcel Travel Plan in respect of that Development Parcel has been submitted to the County Council for its written approval or deemed approval in accordance with the provisions of clause 18; and

7.2.2 Occupation of any Dwelling within any Development Parcel shall not take place until the relevant Parcel Travel Plan have been approved or have been deemed to have been approved pursuant to clause 18 by the County Council; and

7.2.3 each of the relevant Parcel Travel Plans as provided for in paragraph 7.4 is implemented operated and complied with.

7.3 Each Parcel Travel Plan will include inter alia the following sustainable travel measures:

7.3.1 The provision of residential travel information packs to those who first Occupy any Dwelling with a maximum of one pack per Dwelling; and

7.3.2 A scheme for the distribution of bike vouchers on written application to those who first Occupy any Dwelling up to a maximum value of £50 (fifty British pounds) per Dwelling; and

7.3.3 A scheme for the distribution of vouchers for one month’s free travel on bus services in the vicinity of the Development on written application to those who first Occupy any Dwelling with a maximum of one voucher per Dwelling; and

7.3.4 A scheme for the provision of cycle training for children and adults on written application to those who first Occupy any Dwelling.

7.4 The Owners shall implement operate and comply with or will procure the implementation operation and compliance with each of the relevant Parcel Travel Plans in accordance with the relevant measures and actions as referred to in each of the Travel Plans SUBJECT ALWAYS to the overall cost to the Owners of implementing operating and complying with or procuring the implementation operation and compliance with the measures in all of the relevant Parcel Travel Plans not exceeding the Travel Plan Cost Cap.

7.5 If the Owners or the developers of the individual Development Parcels wish to vary any of the relevant Parcel Travel Plans it shall only do so if it has first:

7.5.1 notified the County Council in writing of the proposed variations and reasons for such variations being proposed; and

7.5.2 taken account of any reasonable comments or proposals made by the County Council in response to the variations provided such comments or proposals are submitted in writing to the Owners within 20 Working Days of the receipt of the written variation proposals by the County Council served pursuant to paragraph 7.5.1; and

7.5.3 obtained the written approval of the County Council to the variations.

7.6 Subject to paragraphs 3.2 and 3.3 the Owners shall pay the Travel Plan Co-ordinator Contribution to the County Council as follows:

7.6.1 £15,000 (fifteen thousand British pounds) shall be paid on first Occupation of any Dwelling.

7.6.2 £15,000 (fifteen thousand British pounds) shall be paid on the date which is the first anniversary of first Occupation of any Dwelling;

7.6.3 £15,000 (fifteen thousand British pounds) shall be paid on the date which is the second anniversary of first Occupation of any Dwelling;

7.6.4 £15,000 (fifteen thousand British pounds) shall be paid on the date which is the third anniversary of first Occupation of any Dwelling; and

7.6.5 £15,000 (fifteen thousand British pounds) shall be paid on the date which is the fourth anniversary of first Occupation of any Dwelling;

### 8. CONSTRUCTION ENVIRONMENTAL MANAGEMENT PLAN COSTS

8.1 Following the approval of the site-wide Construction Environmental Management Plan pursuant to planning condition 31 imposed by the Planning Permission the Owners shall:

8.1.1 Submit a schedule setting out the methods for monitoring and managing the requirements as set out in the Construction Environmental Management Plan; and

8.1.2 Submit a schedule setting out the fees and costs associated with the monitoring and managing of the Construction Environmental Management Plan PROVIDED THAT any fees and costs shall not exceed the total CEMP Cost Cap; and

8.1.3 obtain the approval or deemed approval to the schedule of costs referred to in paragraph 8.1.2 before implementing any elements, works, measures, assessments procedures arrangements, actions and other provisions contained in the said site-wide Construction Environmental Management Plan; and

8.1.4 obtain the approval or deemed approval from SCDC before implementing any elements, works, measures, assessments procedures arrangements action and other provisions contained in the said respective detailed Construction Environmental Management Plan(s) for the Development Parcel(s) and Strategic Engineering Element(s).

SAVE THAT nothing shall require the Owners to implement any elements or actions in any Construction Environment Management Plan that would result in the combined cost of implementing the site-wide Construction Environmental Management Plan and Construction Environmental Management Plan(s) for the Strategic Engineering Element(s) exceeding the CEMP Cost Cap.

9. In the event that SCDC rejects the schedule of costs referred to in paragraph 8.1.2 the Owners may instead of pursuing the deemed approval mechanism in accordance with clause 18 serve notice on SCDC that it intends to pay the CEMP Monitoring Contribution to SCDC in order that SCDC can use the CEMP Monitoring Contribution for the purposes of monitoring and managing the requirements of the Construction Environmental Management Plan(s) and for no other purpose AND PROVIDED ALWAYS THAT on payment of the CEMP Monitoring Contribution to SCDC the Owners shall be released from any obligation to monitor and manage the Construction Environmental Management Plan(s).

# SCHEDULE 7 - AFFORDABLE HOUSING

## RELEVANT DEFINITIONS

In this Schedule the following words and phrases shall have the meanings ascribed to them:

"Affordable Housing"
means housing of a kind which (having regard to its
rent or other consideration) is affordable to those
people who cannot afford to rent or buy housing
generally available on the open market because of
their limited means and who are unable to access
private rented/owner occupier accommodation on the
open market at a cost low enough for them to afford
determined with regard to local incomes and local
house prices to include the following types of tenure or
combination thereof:

1. Affordable Rented Housing;

2. Intermediate Affordable Housing;

In each case referred to in Annex 2 of the National
Planning Policy Framework March 2012; and

3. Any other tenure or combination of tenure
approved by SCDC

"Affordable Housing Contribution"
means in respect of each Development Parcel the
commuted sum which may be payable by the Owners
in accordance with Part 3 of this Schedule which shall
be calculated in accordance with paragraphs 5.24,
5.25, 5.26, 5.27 and 5.28 of SCDC's Affordable
Housing Supplementary Planning Document (Adopted
March 2010) and having full regard to:

1. the difference between (i) the Open Market
Residential Value of the relevant
Development Parcel with no Affordable
Housing on such Development Parcel and (ii)
the Open Market Residential Value of the
relevant Development Parcel with the
provision of Affordable Housing on such
Development Parcel; and

2. the Approved Affordable Housing Scheme
for the Development Parcel; and

3. the Affordable Housing Price Table relevant
to the particular Development Parcel.

"Affordable Housing Price"
means in respect of each Development Parcel the sum
unless otherwise agreed in writing by the Owners of
the relevant Development Parcel and SCDC calculated
in accordance with the Affordable Housing Price Table
at Part 4 of this schedule 7 which shall be BCIS Index
Linked from a date commencing 730 days from
implementation to the date when contracts are
exchanged for the sale/purchase of the Affordable
Housing on the relevant Development Parcel;

"Affordable Housing Price Table" means the table at Part 4 of this schedule 7 setting out the sum payable in respect of each Affordable Housing Unit within a Development Parcel which prices are for an Affordable Housing Unit built in accordance with Design and Quality Standards (and such that it is ready for occupation) having regard to the approved Affordable Housing Scheme for the relevant Development Parcel;

"Affordable Housing Site" means the part or parts of the Site on which the Affordable Housing is to be provided and each Affordable Housing Site shall be sufficient in extent for the erection of the Affordable Housing Units including (without prejudice to the generality of the foregoing) adequate car parking spaces walkways gardens and other normal domestic facilities and with services to include the provision of vehicular access, drains, sewers, electricity, gas and telecommunication infrastructure provided that such services may be put in place after the date of transfer of the Affordable Housing Site to a Nominated Registered Provider or SCDC pursuant to the provisions of Part 2 of this Schedule and "Affordable Housing Sites" shall be construed accordingly and FOR THE AVOIDANCE OF DOUBT provided that there shall be a minimum of 13 Development Parcels across the Site there shall be no requirement for the Owners to provide any Affordable Housing Units on 3 of the Development Parcels;

"Affordable Housing Site Transfer" means a transfer by the Owners of the unencumbered freehold interest in any part of an Affordable Housing Site and each party shall bear their own legal costs incurred in connection with the negotiation and completion of such transfer on terms to be agreed but which shall nevertheless:

1.  not include any terms which would restrict use of the land for Affordable Housing and any and all purposes reasonably ancillary thereto; and
2.  not include any terms which would materially adversely and unreasonably affect the construction servicing or occupation of any part of the Site that is retained by the Owners; and
3.  subject to the provisions of paragraph 8 in Part 1 of this Schedule 7 include the reasonable reservation of rights of access and services over the Affordable Housing Site for the benefit of any other part of the Site where such rights are reserved for the purpose of laying managing maintaining replacing renewing cleaning and repairing services including but not limited to as applicable sustainable urban drainage measures, water, gas, sewerage, drainage or
electricity and
4. include for the benefit of the Affordable Housing Site the grant of any and all reasonable rights of access over any adjoining land which rights are reasonably required for the enjoyment, management and maintenance of the Affordable Housing Site;
5. consideration will be the Affordable Housing Price BCIS Index Linked 730 days from Implementation to the date when contracts are exchanged for the sale/purchase of the Affordable Housing on the relevant Development Parcel and
6. include a covenant that the Affordable Housing Site shall not subject to the provisions set out in clauses 11.2.2,11.2.3,11.2.4,11.2.5,11.2.6, 11.2.7, 11.2.8 and 11.2.9 of this Agreement mutatis mutandis be used for any purpose other than for use for Affordable Housing
7. the Affordable Housing Site shall be remediated so that the land is fit for residential use

"Affordable Housing Tenure Mix" means the mix of tenure types specified in paragraphs 1.1 and 1.2 of Part 7 of this Schedule 7;

"Affordable Housing Units" means those Dwellings which are identified for Affordable Housing purposes and which shall comprise 20% (twenty percent) of the total number of Dwellings across the whole of the Development and which shall comprise between 15% and 25% of the total number of Dwellings in respect of each of the Development Parcels that the Owners have notified SCDC shall include Affordable Housing Units and for the avoidance of doubt nothing in this Schedule shall require the Owners to provide 20% Affordable Housing in each Development Parcel and for the further avoidance of doubt provided that there shall be a minimum of 13 Development Parcels across the Site there is no requirement for the Owners to provide any Affordable Housing Units in up to 3 Development Parcels;

"Affordable Housing Scheme" means a scheme to be submitted for each Development Parcel in which Affordable Housing Units are to be located to provide the Affordable Housing Units in the locations as set out in the scheme and which in order to secure the provision by an Approved Registered Provider and/or SCDC of the Affordable Housing Units such scheme to identify the following for the Development Parcel concerned,

1. details of the types, size, number and broad locations of the Affordable Housing Units to be provided and confirming Clustering
arrangements and a programme for their
phased delivery; and
2. the proportion of units proposed to be
Affordable Rented Units and Intermediate
Housing Units; and
3. setting out the design of the Affordable
Housing Units which shall include details
which show the Affordable Housing Units
designed in a way which is materially
indistinguishable in external design and
appearance from the Market Housing Units
of similar size; and
4. a mix of Dwellings which is consistent to
ensure that the mix of the Affordable Housing
Units across the Development accords with
the mix as set out in Part 8 of this Schedule;
and
5. such other information as SCDC may
reasonably require.

**"Affordable Rent"** means the tenure which is referred to in the National
Planning Policy Framework published in March 2012
as offering flexible tenancies to households who are
eligible for social rented housing which is not subject to
national rent regimes but is subject to other controls
that require a rent of no more than 80% of the gross
local Market Rent or on such terms as Government
policy determines and which shall be offered for rent
by a Registered Provider to persons who are otherwise
unable to secure private sector housing for purchase
or rent in the prevailing economic circumstances and
who have in the opinion of SCDC need for such
housing as demonstrated by their position on the
Council's Choice Based Letting Scheme register or
equivalent list provided always that in any event the
rental levels of any individual Affordable Housing Unit
shall not exceed the Local Housing Allowance Level
(or any equivalent benefit level) for that Dwelling
unless otherwise agreed in writing by SCDC or unless
Government Policy dictates;

**"Approved Affordable Housing Scheme"** means the Affordable Housing Scheme approved or
deemed approved pursuant to the provisions of
paragraph 2 of Part 1 of this Schedule 7;

**"Chargee"** means any mortgagee or chargee of the Registered
Provider or the owner of any Intermediate Housing Unit
(excepting the Owners) or the successors in title to
such mortgagee or charge or any receiver or manager
(including an administrative receiver) appointed
pursuant to the Law of Property Act 1925;

"Choice Based Lettings Scheme" means a statutory allocations scheme which all housing authorities must adopt by 2010 in order to offer social housing tenants a choice of accommodation or the opportunity to express preferences about the accommodation to be allocated to them a copy of which is attached at Appendix 14;

"Clustering" means that the Affordable Housing Units shall (unless otherwise agreed with SCDC) be constructed in clusters throughout the Development of a minimum of 15 and a maximum of 30 Affordable Housing Units within each Affordable Housing Site/Cluster and the Owners shall (unless otherwise agreed with SCDC) secure that no less than 12 Clusters shall be provided across the Development;

"Design and Quality Standards" means the publication "Design and Quality Standards" April 2007 issued by the HCA (or successor publication issued by the HCA) which sets out the requirements and recommendations of the HCA for quality of housing probity and procurement and procedural compliance or any successor publication current at the date of the building contract for the construction of the Affordable Housing on any relevant Development Parcel;

"Intermediate Housing Units" means the Affordable Housing Units to be provided by a Registered Provider at a cost above social rent but below their value as Open Market Housing Units for sale or for rent and which meet the criteria set out in the National Planning Policy Framework as being Affordable Housing and includes shared equity (Shared Ownership and/or shared equity loans) other low cost homes for sale and intermediate rent and also other approved affordable home ownership products as may be agreed with SCDC and the Registered Provider;

"Market Rent" means market rent as defined in the latest edition of the RICS Red Book reflecting the properties' size and location as verified by a valuation in accordance with the Royal Institution of Chartered Surveyors Valuation and Appraisal Standards (Fifth Edition as updated and amended from time to time) certified by a member of the Royal Institution of Chartered Surveyors;

"Nominated Registered Provider" means a Registered Provider of Social Housing as nominated by the Owners and approved or deemed approved by SCDC in accordance with the provisions of clause 18, such approval not to be unreasonably withheld or delayed, which provider has been registered under section 80 of the Housing and Regeneration Act 2008 with the Homes and Communities Agency or other entity providing Affordable Housing under equivalent arrangements;
"Open Market Residential Value" means in absence of agreement the value (as determined by a surveyor jointly appointed (or appointed by the Chairman of the Royal Institution of Chartered Surveyors ("RICS") in absence of agreement) who shall value by reference to any relevant assumptions contained in the latest edition of the RICS Red Book;

"Registered Provider" means a Registered Provider of Social Housing registered under Section 80 of the Housing and Regeneration Act 2008 or other entity providing Affordable Housing under equivalent arrangements who is approved or accredited by the Homes and Communities Agency;

"Shared Ownership Units" means the intermediate Housing Units to be offered and occupied via a Registered Provider or SCDC pursuant to a lease in the model form issued by the Homes and Communities Agency and enabling the leaseholder to purchase an initial percentage of that Dwelling;

## PART 1 - AFFORDABLE HOUSING SCHEME

1. The Owners covenant with SCDC that the Affordable Housing Units shall only be used for the purposes of Affordable Housing in accordance with this Schedule save where provided otherwise in this Schedule or in clause 11.

2. Prior to the submission of any Reserved Matters Application for each Development Parcel the Owners shall either:

2.1 in the event that the Development Parcel is to include Affordable Housing Units submit in writing to SCDC the Affordable Housing Scheme for its approval pursuant to clause 18; or

2.2 the Owners shall notify SCDC in writing that the Development Parcel shall not include any Affordable Housing Units

AND FOR THE AVOIDANCE OF DOUBT the Owners may notify SCDC that the Development Parcel shall not include any Affordable Housing Units pursuant to paragraph 2.2 above in respect of up to 3 Development Parcels provided that there shall be a minimum of 13 Development Parcels across the Site.

2.3 In producing an Affordable Housing Scheme, the Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours to limit the extent of common facilities (including private unadopted roads and accesses) the maintenance of which is funded by a service charge levied on the owners or occupiers of the Affordable Housing Units and shall use Reasonable Endeavours to procure that:

2.3.1 any adoptable sewers serving the Affordable Housing Site are subject to agreements under Section 104 of the Water Industry Act 1991 as soon as reasonably practicable; and

2.3.2 any adoptable roads serving the Affordable Housing Site are subject to agreements under Section 38 of the Highways Act 1980 as soon as reasonably practicable.

2.4 Any service charge payable in respect of the Affordable Housing Units shall be kept to the minimum consistent with the limitations imposed by the Housing Act 1985 and (if required by SCDC) the Affordable Housing Scheme shall include proposals by the
Owners to ensure that the service charge does not exceed levels which may prevent the Affordable Housing Units qualifying as affordable under any guidelines or criteria of the Homes and Communities Agency or the Tenant Services Agency (or their respective successors) current at the date when the Affordable Housing Scheme is being submitted for approval.

3. The Owners covenant with SCDC not to Commence Development of any Development Parcel until either:

3.1 the Affordable Housing Scheme relevant to that Development Parcel has been approved or deemed approved by the SCDC pursuant to clause 18 of this Agreement or

3.2 the Owners have notified SCDC in writing that the Development Parcel shall not include any Affordable Housing Units.

4. The Owners covenant with SCDC that 20% (twenty percent) of the Dwellings constructed as part of the Development shall be provided as Affordable Housing and shall be provided on each Development Parcel where there is to be Affordable Housing in accordance with the Affordable Housing Tenure Mix (unless otherwise agreed by SCDC).

5. The Owners shall be permitted subject to para 6 below to seek to amend the Approved Affordable Housing Scheme at any time following the initial approval or deemed approval of the Affordable Housing Scheme pursuant to paragraph 2 above SAVE THAT in seeking to do so the Owners shall submit such amendments for approval to SCDC in writing and thereafter the provisions of clause 18 shall apply.

6. The Owners covenant with SCDC that they shall not seek to vary an Approved Affordable Housing Scheme other than in accordance with the following provisions:

6.1 SCDC shall be under no obligation to agree to vary or change an Affordable Housing Scheme which would result in less than 40% of the Affordable Housing Units on any particular Development Parcel being for Affordable Rent

6.2 Before the Owners seek to vary the approved Affordable Housing Scheme they shall have first satisfied SCDC that they have used Reasonable Endeavours to provide the Affordable Housing in accordance with the Affordable Housing Scheme as originally approved

6.3 The Owners shall submit such information reasonably requested by SCDC in order for SCDC to consider and determine any such application for a variation.

7. The Owners covenant with SCDC that any Reserved Matters Application for a Development Parcel shall reflect the provisions of the Approved Affordable Housing Scheme for that Development Parcel.

8. Where applicable within a relevant Affordable Housing Site the Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours to provide any Utilities and Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage within adoptable public highway or in common facilities but for the avoidance of doubt such Utilities and Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage shall not be laid underneath and/or within 3 metres of the actual or anticipated building footprint of any part of the Affordable Housing Site.

## PART 2 – AFFORDABLE HOUSING SITE TRANSFER

1. The Owners covenant with SCDC as follows:

1.1 If by implementation contracts have been exchanged with a Nominated Registered Provider for the transfer and build of 300 Affordable Housing Units in accordance with
an Approved Affordable Housing Scheme across the entire Site to a Nominated Registered Provider then the provisions of paragraph 2 of this Part 2 shall apply.

1.2 If by Implementation contracts have not been entered into between the Owners and Nominated Registered Provider for the transfer and build of 300 Affordable Housing Units in accordance with an Approved Affordable Housing Scheme across the entire Site to a Nominated Registered Provider then the provisions of paragraph 3 of this 0 shall apply.

2. In the event the provisions of paragraph 1.1 of this Part 2 apply then the freehold interest in each relevant Affordable Housing Site will be transferred to the Nominated Registered Provider after the grant of each Reserved Matters Approval for the Affordable Housing Units within the relevant Development Parcel.

3. In the event the provisions of paragraph 1.2 of this Part 2 apply then the Owners shall:

3.1 On or before Commencement of each relevant Development Parcel offer to transfer each of the Affordable Housing Units in such Development Parcel to SCDC for the relevant Affordable Housing Price as set out in the Affordable Housing Price Table (the "SCDC Affordable Housing Offer"); and

3.2 Where the provisions in paragraph 3.1 apply it shall subject to completion of the build of the relevant Affordable Housing Units in accordance with the Design and Quality Standards such that they are each ready for occupation be deemed that by transferring the Affordable Housing Units to SCDC the Owners will have discharged their obligation to provide the number of Affordable Housing Units within that Development Parcel as specified in the Approved Affordable Housing Scheme for that relevant Development Parcel; and

3.3 If within 40 Working Days of the SCDC Affordable Housing Offer being made the SCDC Affordable Housing Offer has been accepted in writing by SCDC either in whole or in part PROVIDED that that SCDC shall not elect to take any of the Affordable Housing in part unless they are in a block of at least 4 Affordable Housing Units the Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours to exchange legally binding contracts with SCDC within 3 months of the date when the written acceptance of the SCDC Affordable Housing Offer is received by the Owners.

3.4 Any SCDC Affordable Housing Offer shall be held open for a minimum of 40 Working Days

3.5 In the event that SCDC:

3.5.1 does not respond to the SCDC Affordable Housing Offer within the said period of 40 Working Days; or

3.5.2 rejects the SCDC Affordable Housing Offer
the Owners may elect in accordance with paragraph 4 below to pay the relevant Affordable Housing Contribution for that Development Parcel to SCDC in lieu of the provision of any relevant Affordable Housing Units in that Development Parcel.

4. In the event that paragraph 3.5 applies then where applicable the Owners shall pay the Affordable Housing Contribution to SCDC in respect of the relevant Development Parcel and in accordance with the timing arrangements set out in Part 3 of this Schedule 7.

5. Where the Owners shall have paid the Affordable Housing Contribution to SCDC in respect of a particular Development Parcel this shall not have the effect of requiring the Owners to provide extra Affordable Housing Units (i.e. above the relevant Approved Affordable Housing Scheme(s) on remaining Affordable Housing Sites
within the Development and it shall be deemed that by (i) paying the Affordable Housing Contribution to SCDC in respect of a particular Development Parcel and (ii) subject to having transferred the relevant Dwelling where there is an acceptance by SCDC of an offer which has been made as set out in para 3.3 in Part 3 of this Schedule 7 then subject to completion of the build of the relevant Affordable Housing Units in accordance with the Design and Quality Standards such that they are each ready for occupation the Owners will have discharged their obligation to provide the number of Affordable Housing Units within that Development Parcel as specified in the Approved Affordable Housing Scheme for that relevant Development Parcel.

## PART 3 - AFFORDABLE HOUSING CONTRIBUTION

1. Where the Owners pay or are due to pay the Affordable Housing Contribution to SCDC in lieu of the provision of any Affordable Housing Units in a Development Parcel:

1.1 the Owners shall in accordance with the Approved Affordable Housing Scheme submit details to SCDC identifying the Dwellings within the Development Parcel which would have been Affordable Housing Units; and

1.1.1 each Affordable Housing Contribution (payable in respect of a particular Development Parcel) shall be paid in the following instalments:

(a) 50% of the Affordable Housing Contribution shall be paid on the earlier of the following:

(i) Occupation of any Dwelling within the Development Parcel which would have been an Affordable Housing Unit; and

(ii) Occupation of no more than 50% of the Open Market Dwellings within that Development Parcel which for the avoidance of doubt does not include those Dwellings which would have been provided as Affordable Housing Units.

(b) The remaining 50% of the Affordable Housing Contribution shall be paid on the earlier of the following:

(i) Occupation of no more than 45% of the Dwellings within the Development Parcel which would have been an Affordable Housing Unit; and

(ii) Occupation of no more than 80% of the Open Market Dwellings within that Development Parcel which for the avoidance of doubt does not include those Dwellings which would have been an Affordable Housing Unit; and

1.1.2 offer for sale to SCDC each individual Dwelling within that Development Parcel which would have been an Affordable Housing Dwelling at the relevant part of the Affordable Housing Price (it being agreed by the parties that subject to being in a block of at least 4 SCDC may accept any of such individual Dwellings without having to accept them all); and

1.1.3 In the event that SCDC declines or does not respond to the offer for each individual Dwelling within 60 Working Days the Owners shall be entitled at the expiry of such period to dispose of any of such Dwellings which have been an Affordable Housing Unit where the offer has not been accepted by or on behalf of SCDC and to dispose of such Dwelling for sale on the Open Market; and
1.1.4 In the event that pursuant to paragraph 1.1.2 above SCDC accepts an offer for an individual Dwelling within 60 Working Days the Owners shall complete the transfer of that Dwelling to SCDC (or such party nominated by SCDC) and completion shall take place no later than 40 Working Days thereafter.

PROVIDED ALWAYS THAT in the event that SCDC does not spend all of the Affordable Housing Contribution received from the Owners for the Development Parcel on the purchase of Affordable Housing Dwellings within that Development Parcel then for the avoidance of doubt SCDC shall be entitled to use any unspent Affordable Housing Contribution for that Development Parcel on the purchase of Affordable Housing Dwellings on alternative Development Parcels.

# PART 4 - AFFORDABLE HOUSING PRICE TABLE

1. The Affordable Housing Price in respect of each Affordable Housing Unit within a Development Parcel shall be calculated in accordance with the following:

| Dwelling type             | Affordable Rent   | Shared ownership |
| :------------------------ | :---------------- | :--------------- |
| 1 Bed Flat up to 495ft²   | £48,640(15)       |                  |
| 2 Bed flat up to 624ft²   | £61,331 (15)      | £140,000(15)     |
| 2 Bed House up to 883ft²  | £86,707(45)       |                  |
| 2 Bed House 818 ft²       |                   | £190,000(51)     |
| 3 Bed House 1012ft²       | £99,396(75)       |                  |
| 3 Bed House 915ft²        |                   | £210,000(54)     |
| 4 Bed House 1227ft²       | £120,544(30)      |                  |

AND FOR THE AVOIDANCE OF DOUBT the Affordable Housing price shall be BCIS Index Linked from a date commencing 730 days from Implementation to the date when contracts are exchanged for the sale/purchase of the Affordable Housing on the relevant Development Parcel;

## PART 5 - TRIGGER CONTROLS

1. In respect of each Development Parcel containing Affordable Housing Units the Owners covenant that unless otherwise agreed with SCDC no more than 90% of the Open Market Dwellings within that Development Parcel shall be Occupied until:

    1.1 the Affordable Housing Units in respect of that Development Parcel have been Completed such that they are ready for occupation; and/or

    1.2 the Affordable Housing Contribution has been paid by the Owners in accordance with Part 3 of this Schedule 7 and in respect of that Development; and

    1.3 the sale of any relevant Dwelling to SCDC has been completed pursuant to para 3.3.3 above where an offer has been accepted by SCDC:

2. In respect of the Development the Owners covenant that unless otherwise agreed with SCDC no more than 90% of the Open Market Dwellings within the Development shall be Occupied until:

    2.1 100% of the Affordable Housing Units in respect of the Development have been Completed such that they are ready for occupation; and/or

    2.2 the Affordable Housing Contribution has been paid by the Owners in accordance with Part 3 of this Schedule 7 and in respect of all of the relevant Development Parcels; and

2.3 the sale of any relevant Dwelling to SCDC has been completed pursuant to para 3.3 in Part 2 of this Schedule 7 above where an offer has been accepted by SCDC.

## PART 6 - AFFORDABLE HOMES BUILD STANDARD

1. Where an Affordable Housing Site is transferred to a Nominated Registered Provider or where an Affordable Housing Unit is transferred to SCDC (or SCDC's nominee)the following provisions shall apply and the Owners covenant with SCDC as follows:

1.1 To construct and complete the Affordable Housing Units (together with all necessary services connections and infrastructure):

1.1.1 in accordance with the requirements of the Department of Communities and Local Government's "Code for Sustainable Homes" energy efficiency and sustainability standards (or if such standards are superseded, the applicable standards from time to time) with the completed Affordable Housing Units achieving Code Level 4 or higher; and

1.1.2 in accordance with Design and Quality Standards; and

1.1.3 in accordance with the relevant Affordable Housing Scheme.

2. The Affordable Housing Units shall be deemed to be completed either when the NHBC Buildmark Certificate (or equivalent) shall have been issued in respect of each of the Affordable Housing Units or when the local authority building control consultancy or approved inspector certifies them complete, if earlier.

## PART 7 - AFFORDABLE HOUSING TENURE MIX

1. The Affordable Housing Tenure Mix in each Development Parcel where there is Affordable Housing shall unless otherwise agreed in writing by SCDC be as follows:

1.1 A minimum of 60 % (sixty percent) of the Affordable Housing Units are to be Affordable Rented Housing Units, let and managed by an Affordable Housing Provider and/or by or on behalf of SCDC; and

1.2 A maximum of 40% (fifty percent) of the Affordable Housing Units are to be Shared Ownership Units or other Intermediate Housing Units approved by SCDC.

## PART 8 - AFFORDABLE HOUSING MIX (AS TO NUMBERS OF BEDROOMS AND AVERAGE SIZE)

The Affordable Housing Mix shall unless otherwise agreed in writing by SCDC be as follows:

| Beds | Sq ft | Ave m2 (unit) | No |

## PART 9 - CHOICE BASED LETTINGS SCHEME AND NOMINATIONS ARRANGEMENTS

1. The Owners covenant to ensure the retention at all times of the provision of:

1.1 any of the Affordable Rented Units on first Occupation; and

1.2 75% of the Affordable Rented Units on any subsequent Occupation in accordance with the Choice Based Lettings Allocation Scheme as produced by SCDC from time to time PROVIDED THAT if SCDC propose to amend the Choice Based Lettings Allocation Scheme from that appended to this Agreement then SCDC shall first consult with the Owners and any Registered Provider (as successors in title to the Owners of any part of the Development) in relation to any proposed amendments unless and until such amendments have first been approved by the Owners and the Registered Providers.

2. In circumstances where there is (i) no Choice Based Lettings allocation scheme for the Affordable Rented units, (ii) no equivalent scheme for the Intermediate Units exists and where a Nominated Registered Provider has agreed to purchase the Affordable Housing Units then no Dwelling designated for Intermediate Housing shall be occupied unless the Nominated Registered Provider has first entered into a document which reflects the arrangements set out in Part 10 of this Schedule or is in SCDC's then current form of Nomination Agreement which (inter alia) sets out the criteria for selecting those who qualify for occupation of the Dwelling, and the procedure for nominating prospective occupiers, the form of such agreement to be subject to any amendments reasonably required by the Nominated Registered Provider or its funders and agreed by SCDC acting reasonably.
PART 10 – NOMINATIONS ARRANGEMENTS

1.  Pursuant to Paragraph 2 of Part 9 of this Schedule 7, as between the Nominated Registered Provider and SCDC the following provisions shall govern the nominations/ allocations of occupation of those Affordable Housing Units allocated as Intermediate Affordable Housing.

2.  The Nominated Approved Registered Provider shall notify SCDC in writing from time to time of any Affordable Housing Unit allocated as Intermediate Affordable Housing which shall become vacant and available for occupation.

3.  The Nominated Registered Provider and SCDC shall liaise as necessary with regard to local housing need generally and the list of Qualifying Persons (as defined in Paragraph 7 of Part 10 of this Schedule 7).

4.  In cases where SCDC shall have the right of nomination: -

    4.1 SCDC will normally submit 3 names of nominees to the Nominated Registered Provider within seven (7) working days of receipt of written notice of a vacancy from the Nominated Registered Provider

    4.2 SCDC may however nominate the Nominated Registered Provider's preferred choice unless there shall in the opinion of SCDC be good reasons for not doing so; in such cases SCDC may within seven (7) days as aforesaid nominate another person to whom the dwelling shall be allocated by the Nominated Registered Provider unless there shall in the opinion of the Nominated Registered Provider be good reasons for not so allocating; and if the Affordable Housing Unit is not so allocated, the Nominated Registered Provider and SCDC shall discuss further nominations by SCDC for that dwelling

5.  The Occupation of any Affordable Housing Unit shall be limited to a Qualifying Person in the Cambridgeshire Sub-Region "Cambridgeshire Sub-Region" (meaning the geographical area covered by the following Councils; Cambridge City Council, East Cambridgeshire District Council, Fenland District Council, Forest Heath District Council, Huntingdonshire District Council, South Cambridgeshire District Council, St Edmundsbury Borough Council) PROVIDED THAT the Nominated Registered Provider may allow persons who are not Qualifying Persons in the Cambridgeshire Sub-Region to occupy any dwelling if there are no Qualifying Persons in the Cambridgeshire Sub-Region available and willing to take up occupation within a period of two (2) months of when a dwelling becomes vacant and PROVIDED THAT such disposition is in accordance with the objects of the Nominated Registered Provider

6.  SCDC and the Nominated Registered Provider shall have the following nomination rights in respect of the Affordable Housing Units

|                                         | Nominations                            |
| :-------------------------------------- | :------------------------------------- |
| The initial allocation of each Affordable Dwelling | SCDC 100%                              |
| Thereafter                              | SCDC 75%                               |
|                                         | The Nominated Registered Provider 25%  |

7.  "Qualifying Person" means either: -

7.1 an individual who shall satisfy SCDC that he or she is in housing need as defined by and/or described in SCDC's latest adopted policies (so far as may be relevant) and who shall prior to the grant of any tenancy or lease or any agreement for a tenancy or a lease or any assignment produced to the Nominated Registered Provider or other grantor written confirmation by SCDC that such an individual is a Qualifying Person for this purpose

7.2 an individual on the current list (if any) of Qualifying Persons (deemed pre-certificated by SCDC) provided by SCDC ("District Council's List"). PROVIDED THAT the circumstances of the Qualifying Person shall not have materially changed from the date of the certificate or the District Council's List (as the case may be) to the date of grant of a tenancy, lease or assignment such that he or she would cease to be in housing need (as defined) or on the District Council's List

7.3 is registered on the Homebuy Shared Ownership Scheme

# SCHEDULE 8 - COMMUNITY CENTRE AND SPORTS PAVILION

## RELEVANT DEFINITIONS

"Community Building"
means a community centre building whose internal floor space shall be 950 square metres (unless otherwise agreed between the Owners and SCDC) together with associated car parking facilities and associated landscape or amenity space to be provided as part of the Development for the use as a multi-use flexible community facility accommodating a variety of activities including but not limited to meeting space, play group area, functions room, recreation uses, health, community learning and library facilities and which may as agreed between the Owners and SCDC and subject to part 10 of this Schedule 8 include the Informal Single Court MUGA as part of the Community Building but such Informal Single Court MUGA shall otherwise be provided under Schedule 14 PROVIDED THAT for the avoidance of doubt SCDC and the Owners agree that nothing in this definition or Schedule shall prevent SCDC from extending the Community Building by way of future ground floor extensions outside the scope of this Agreement which for the avoidance of doubt shall not be at the Owners' cost and shall not be covered by any Contribution payable pursuant to this Agreement;

"Community and Sports Contribution Cap"
means subject to part 10 of this Schedule 8 £2,622,500 (two million six hundred and twenty two thousand five hundred British pounds) being the maximum amount of money that the Owners shall be obliged by the terms of this Agreement to use towards the design, construction and fitting out of either or both of the following:

1. the Community Building Purpose; and/or
2. the Sports Pavilion Purpose;

and in calculating whether the said cap has been exceeded the Costs shall be taken into account

"Community Building Actual Contribution"
means subject to part 10 of this Schedule 8 the sum to be spent on the Community Building and if applicable calculated pursuant to service of the notice by SCDC pursuant to paragraph 1 of part 9 of this Schedule 8;

"Community Building Contribution"
means subject to parts 9 and 10 of this Schedule 8 the sum of £1,522,500 (one million five hundred and twenty two thousand and five hundred British pounds) or any part thereof which is where relevant to be paid by the Owners to SCDC and which can be used by SCDC for the Community Building Purpose and which for the avoidance of doubt shall not exceed the Community Building Costs Cap where the Community Building is delivered without use of any monies from the Sports Pavilion Contribution;

"Community Building Maximum Spend"
means subject to part 10 of this Schedule 8 the combined
sum of the Community Building Actual Contribution and the Flexible Sum

**"Community Building Purpose"**
means for the construction completion and fitting out and equipping of the Community Building in accordance with the approved or deemed approved Community Building Detailed Specification or to an alternative written specification which is approved or deemed approved by the Owners

**"Community Building Cost Cap"**
means subject to parts 9 and 10 of this Schedule £1,522,500 (one million five hundred and twenty two thousand five hundred British pounds) and the Costs shall be permitted to be included within any calculation to establish whether the said cap has been exceeded

**"Community Building Land"**
means the area of Serviced land within the Site not less than 0.207 hectares in size and more than 0.277 hectares in size PROVIDED THAT only in the circumstances where the Informal Single Court MUGA is located adjacent to or in the vicinity of the Community Building will the upper figure be applied and such land to be of the general shape and located within one of the areas shown indicatively edged orange on Plan 5 Indicative Locations -- Land Uses Plan which shall be provided by the Owners or such other area of Serviced land of a similar shape and in a location as may be submitted by the Owners to and approved by SCDC and capable of providing the Community Building, ancillary external servicing, circulation, storage, landscape, amenity space directly associated with the Community Building and essential car parking spaces being not less than 5 spaces and which may include the Informal Single Court MUGA and PROVIDED THAT in any event the Community Building Land shall be accessed by a road which will be built to an adoptable standard AND PROVIDED FURTHER that the Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours to enter into a section 38 highway Agreement as to future adoption of such road within a reasonable timeframe having regard to the anticipated date of Completion of the Community Building :

**"Community Building Land Transfer"**
means a transfer by the Owners to SCDC (or the Town Council if applicable) of the unencumbered freehold in the form of the draft transfer appended hereto at Appendix 15 or such other form as the parties may otherwise agree relating to the Community Building Land and each party shall bear their own legal costs incurred in connection with the negotiation and completion of such transfer which shall nevertheless:

1. not include any terms which would restrict use of the land as a Community Building and any and all purposes reasonably ancillary thereto; and
2. not include any terms which would materially adversely and unreasonably affect the construction servicing or occupation of any part
of the Site that is retained by the Owners; and
3. include reasonable reservation of rights of access and services over the Community Building Land for the benefit of any other part of the Site where such rights are reserved for the purpose of laying managing maintaining replacing renewing cleaning and repairing services including but not limited to as applicable sustainable urban drainage measures, water, gas, sewerage, drainage or electricity and telecommunications subject to the provisions of paragraph 12 of Part 1 of this Schedule 6; and
4. include for the benefit of the Community Building Land the grant of any and all reasonable rights of access and services over any adjoining land which rights are reasonably required for the use, management and maintenance of the Community Building Land for its purpose for the benefit of the community which shall include permitting voluntary sector use;

5. be at nil consideration; and

6. include a covenant that the Community Building Land shall not be used for any purpose other than primarily for community use or for supporting community activity and development and use for a community building and associated ancillary facilities and which may include any and all purposes reasonably ancillary thereto; and

7. include a covenant that the Community Building that has or will be constructed on the Community Building Land shall after construction subject to suitable funding be maintained for the benefit of the community arising from the Development which shall include permitting voluntary sector use; and

8. provisions dealing with the arrangements set out in paragraph 5 of Part 2 of this Schedule.

"Community Building Detailed Specification" means a detailed specification to be prepared by or on behalf of the Owners by a suitably qualified and professionally approved person experienced in preparing and reviewing specifications for buildings of this sort and the detailed specification shall be approved or deemed to have been approved by SCDC which specification shall include room shapes sizes and heights as well as details of external and internal work specifications and finishes and also including details of services and heating and subject to there being sufficient funds available within the Community and Sports Contribution Cap or subject to Part 9 of this Schedule the Community Building Maximum Spend shall provide for a reasonable level of internal
fitting out works and equipment consistent with the
intended use as a Community Building PROVIDED THAT
the specification shall not require or otherwise specify
standards materials or equipment associated with the
design or construction or fit out or equipping of the
Community Building that:

1. in the case of the construction of the Community
Building being undertaken by SCDC that would
result in a combined cost to construct and fit out
and equip that would exceed the Community
Building Maximum Spend

2. in the case of the construction of the
Community Building being undertaken by the
Owners that would result in a combined cost to
construct and fit out and equip that would
exceed the Community Building Maximum
Spend

AND PROVIDED FURTHER that subject as hereinafter
provided the Community Building Detailed Specification
shall be substantially in accordance with the Initial
Community Building Specification.

"Costs" means those costs applicable to the relevant Facility
which shall be permitted to be included within any
calculation to establish whether the Community and
Sports Contribution Cap the Community Building Cost
Cap the Community Building Maximum Spend or the
Sports Pavilion Cost Cap have been exceeded shall
include:

1. any costs arising in connection with any
construction contract let in connection with the
construction and completion of the Community
Building which shall also include the provision of
landscaping and access arrangements internal
to the Community Building Land, boundary
treatments and car parking and for the
avoidance of doubt unless such costs are
already covered in the main construction
contract the costs shall include costs of any
inspections by the Surveyor as provided for in
this Schedule 8 and the costs of inspections by
such other person or company undertaking
inspections of the Community Building for the
purpose of issuing the Certificate of Practical
Completion and/or the Certificate of Final
Completion; and

2. any costs arising in connection with any
construction contract let in connection with the

landscaping, access arrangements, boundary
treatments and car parking and for the
avoidance of doubt unless such costs are
already covered in the main construction
contract the costs shall include costs of any

this Schedule 8 and the costs of inspections by
such other person or company undertaking
inspections of the Community Building for the
purpose of issuing the Certificate of Practical

3. abnormal costs agreed by SCDC acting
reasonably which shall include responding
within a reasonable timescale having regard to
any programme including but not limited to

4. any reasonable commitment fees, procurement

5. design costs including all professional fees
associated with the Community Building and/or
Sports Pavilion which shall be no more than:

  5.1 where the contract is a design and build
  contract 12% of the total contract value; or

  5.2 12% of the total cost cap;

6. statutory fees;

7. costs associated with requirements from the

8. cost of fixtures and fitting and equipment;

9. such reasonable legal agents and consultancy
fees (whether such fees relate to the Owners'
(or SCDC's if the Community Building and/or
Sports Pavilion is constructed by SCDC)
internal staff or employees or external advisors)
as may be associated with the entering into of
the construction contract or any other contracts
for the design, construction, completion, fitting
out and equipping of the Community Building
and/or Sports Pavilion

PROVIDED THAT the parties acknowledge and agree
that in identifying the Costs as defined by this definition
no element of double counting of elements of costs shall
be permitted

"Defects" means any defects shrinkage or other faults in relation to
the construction and/or design of the Community Building
and/or Sports Pavilion (and including for the avoidance of
doubt any latent construction and/or design defects as
are defined or described within the design contract and/or
construction contract and such contracts shall contain
proper and reasonable provisions relating to (and
definitions of defects) relating to the Community Building
and/or Sports Pavilion as procured by the party to this

and construction of the Community Building and/or Sports
