Pavilion rests but which for the avoidance of doubt
defects shall not include any defects or faults in relation to
construction equipment or internal fitting out where the
relevant damage defect or fault is created as a result of
works or accidents undertaken by the subsequent owners
occupiers or users of the Community Building and/or
Sports Pavilion or where such construction equipment or
internal fitting out has itself been undertaken by the
subsequent owners occupiers or users of the Community
Building and/or Sports Pavilion;

"Flexible Sum"
means £220,000 (two hundred and twenty thousand
British Pounds);

"Informal Single Court
MUGA"
means the informal MUGA which will be approximately
700m² (35m-37m x 18.5- 20m) on a macadam base being
0.700 hectares in size (unless otherwise agreed between
SCDC and the Owners) within either the Community
Building Land or in the Formal Park as agreed between
SCDC and the Owners with both locations being shown
indicatively on Plan 9 attached to this Agreement. The
facility will be robust and vandal proof and will be both fit
for purpose and meet the standards set out in "Design
and Specification of Multi Use Games Areas MUGAs" by
Sport England and the Sports and Play Construction
Association (2006) or as otherwise agreed by SCDC and
which may be floodlit;

"Initial Community
Building Specification"
means the specification for the Community Building
appended to this Agreement at Appendix 16 together with
such variations as may be approved or deemed to have
been approved pursuant to paragraphs 4 and 5 of Part 1
of this Schedule 8;

"Initial Sports Pavilion
Specification"
means the specification for the Sports Pavilion appended
to this Agreement at Appendix 17;

"Option" and "Options"
means the option and options set out in Parts 2, 3, 4, 5
and 6 of this Schedule 8;

"Sports Pavilion"
means a building together with associated facilities as
provided for in the definition of Sports Pavilion
Specification such building to have an internal floor space
of 870 square metres (unless otherwise agreed between
SCDC and the Owners) to be provided within the Site and
which land building and associated facilities are to be
used for sports and recreational purposes together with
ancillary bar and/or function room together with
associated car parking facilities PROVIDED THAT for the
avoidance of doubt SCDC and the Owners agree that
nothing in this definition or Schedule shall prevent SCDC
from extending the Sports Pavilion by way of future ground floor extensions outside the scope of this Agreement which for the avoidance of doubt shall not be at the Owners' cost and shall not be covered by any Contribution payable pursuant to this Agreement:

"Sports Pavilion Actual Contribution" means the sum to be spent on the Sports Pavilion and if applicable calculated pursuant to service of the notice by SCDC pursuant to paragraph 1 of part 9 of this Schedule 8;

"Sports Pavilion Contribution" means the sum of £1,100,000 (one million one hundred thousand British pounds) which is where relevant to be paid by the Owners to SCDC and which can be used by SCDC for the Sports Pavilion Contribution Purposes;

"Sports Pavilion Contribution Purposes" means for the construction completion and fitting out of the Sports Pavilion in accordance with the approved or deemed approved Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification or to an alternative written specification which is approved or deemed approved by the Owners;

"Sports Pavilion Minimum Spend" means £880,000 (eight hundred and eight thousand British pounds)

"Sports Pavilion Cost Cap" means subject to Part 9 of this Schedule, the total cost of providing the Sports Pavilion pursuant to the approved Sports Pavilion Specification which shall not exceed £1,100,000 (one million one hundred thousand British pounds) and the Costs shall be permitted to be included within any calculation to establish whether the said cap has been exceeded

"Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification" means a detailed specification which shall include the building floorspace, the height of the building, the number of storeys, the number of car parking spaces, the total site area, other relevant size details, materials, equipment and all other relevant details necessary for the provision of the Sports Pavilion on the Site to be prepared by or on behalf of the Owners by a suitably qualified and professionally approved person experienced in preparing and reviewing specifications for such Sports Pavilions which specification shall be submitted and approved by SCDC which specification shall include room shapes, sizes and heights as well as details of external and internal work specifications including services as may be required and are appropriate pursuant the provisions of this Agreement and subject to there being sufficient funds available within the Sports Pavilion Actual Contribution shall provide for a reasonable level of internal fitting out works and equipment consistent with the intended use as a Sports Pavilion PROVIDED THAT the specification shall not require or otherwise specify standards materials or equipment associated with the design or construction or fit out or equipping of the Sports Pavilion that:

1.  in the case of the construction of the Sports
Pavilion being undertaken by SCDC that would
result in a combined cost to construct and fit out
and equip that would exceed the Sports Pavilion
Actual Contribution

2. in the case of the construction of the Sports
Pavilion being undertaken by the Owners that
would result in a combined cost to construct and
fit out and equip that would exceed the Sports
Pavilion Actual Contribution

AND PROVIDED FURTHER that subject to 1 and 2
above and subject as hereinafter provided including
Part 9 of this Schedule the Sports Pavilion Detailed
Specification shall be substantially in accordance with the
Initial Sports Pavilion Specification;

"Sports Pavilion Land" means the area of land Serviced within the Site not less
than 0.3 hectares in size and more than 0.5 hectares in
size to be provided by the Owners in a location within the
area shown indicatively edged with a thick red line on
Plan 5 attached to this Agreement and the final area to be
approved in accordance with the provisions of this
Schedule and PROVIDED THAT in any event the Sports
Pavilion Land shall be accessed by a road which will be
built to an adoptable standard AND PROVIDED
FURTHER that the Owners shall use reasonable
endeavours to enter into a section 38 highway Agreement
as to future adoption of such road within a reasonable
timeframe having regard to the anticipated date of
Completion of the Sports Pavilion;

"Sports Pavilion Land
Transfer" means a transfer by the Owners of the unencumbered
freehold in the form of the draft transfer appended hereto
at Appendix 18 or such other form as the parties may
otherwise agree relating to the Sports Pavilion Land on
terms to be agreed and each party shall bear their own
legal costs incurred in connection with the negotiation and
completion of such transfer but which shall nevertheless:

1. not include any terms which would restrict public
access; and

2. not include any terms which would materially
adversely and unreasonably affect the
construction servicing or occupation of the part
of the Site that is retained by the Owners; and

3. subject to the provisions of paragraph 12 of Part
1 include the reasonable reservation of rights of
access and services over the Sports Pavilion
Land for the benefit of any other part of the Site
for the purpose of laying managing maintaining
replacing renewing cleaning and repairing
services including but not limited to as
applicable sustainable urban drainage
measures, water, gas, sewerage, drainage or
electricity; and

4. include for the benefit of the Sports Pavilion
Land the grant of any and all reasonable rights of access and services which are reasonably required for the use, management and maintenance of the Sports Pavilion Land over any adjoining land for its intended purpose as set out in this Schedule; and

5. be at nil consideration; and

6. include a covenant that the Sports Pavilion that has or will be constructed on the Sports Pavilion Land shall after construction subject to suitable funding be maintained for the benefit of the general public arising from the Development; and

7. the Sports Pavilion Land shall not be used for any other purpose other than for sports, community and recreation activities and which may include any and all purposes reasonably ancillary thereto or which are directly associated and ancillary facilities and which may include any ancillary bar and/or function room that may be constructed and

8. provisions dealing with the arrangements set out in paragraph 5 of Part 2 of this Schedule

“Surveyor” means such person or persons as may be notified in writing by SCDC to the Owners and suitably qualified in accordance with the general requirements of the Royal Institution of Chartered Surveyors

## PART 1 - COMMUNITY CENTRE AND SPORTS PAVILION LOCATION AND SPECIFICATIONS

1. In the event that the Owners submit the Reserved Matters Application for the Community Building then prior to submission of the Reserved Matters Application the Owners shall undertake pre-application discussion with SCDC in relation to the exact location and boundaries for the Community Building and Community Building Land and the exact location and boundaries for the Community Building and the Community Building Land shall subject to paragraph 7 of this Part 1 of this Schedule 8 below be submitted to and approved by SCDC either:

1.1 as part of Reserved Matters Applications for any of the Development Parcel(s) adjacent to which the Community Building is located; or

1.2 as a Reserved Matters Application solely for the Community Building.

2. In the event that SCDC submits the Reserved Matters Application or other form of planning application for the Community Building then prior to submission of the Reserved Matters Application or other form of planning application it shall have obtained the prior written approval or deemed approval of the Owners to the exact size and location of the Community Building and in the event that such Reserved Matters Application or other form of planning application is submitted before the Community Building Land Transfer is completed then SCDC shall have also obtained the prior written approval or deemed approval of the Owners to the extent of the site in such Reserved Matters Application or other form of planning application is made.

3. The Owners shall as soon as they reasonably can notify SCDC in writing of the date the Owners anticipate they will submit the Community Building Detailed Specification to SCDC for approval pursuant to paragraph 6 of this Part 1 of Schedule 8 below PROVIDED THAT such written notification shall be served on SCDC no later than 60 Working Days before the anticipated date for submission of the Community Building Detailed Specification.

4. In the event that SCDC considers that there are exceptional circumstances which mean that a variation to the Initial Community Building Specification would be appropriate then SCDC shall notify the Owners in writing of the proposed variations for the Owners' approval or deemed approval and the exceptional circumstances that apply which warrant the variation PROVIDED FURTHER that:

4.1 any proposed variations shall not be permitted where the Owners are to build the Community Building and their effect would be such that the combined cost to construct and fit out and equip would exceed the Community Building Cost Cap (or if applicable the Community Building Maximum Spend) SAVE THAT this paragraph 4.1 shall not preclude SCDC from requesting any variations so long as such variation include an offer to fund any additional construction fit out and equipping arising from the variations the result of which would be to exceed the Community Building Costs Cap (or if applicable the Community Building Maximum Spend) and in those circumstances proposed variations shall be permitted; and

4.2 for the purposes of this paragraph 4 "exceptional circumstances" shall mean:

4.2.1 for reasons of health and safety; or

4.2.2 where changes to Building Regulations result in there being a need for such variations; or

4.2.3 where an Expert appointed pursuant to clause 17 makes a determination that he is satisfied that there are other special grounds which justify a variation to the Initial Community Building Specification; or

4.2.4 where additional funding is available which shall for the avoidance of doubt be over and above the Community Building Costs Cap (or if applicable the Community Building Maximum Spend)

and in those circumstances proposed variations shall be permitted.

4.3 SCDC shall not unless otherwise agreed by the Owners be entitled to serve a notification pursuant to this paragraph 4.3 of this Part 1 of Schedule 8 after the expiry of a period of 20 Working Days calculated from date of receipt or deemed receipt of the written notification served pursuant to paragraph 1 of Part 2 of this Schedule 8; and

5. The Owners shall at any time before the Community Building Detailed Specification is submitted to SCDC pursuant to paragraph 6 notify SCDC in writing that it wishes to vary the Initial Community Building Specification because of exceptional circumstances and in such notification shall set out the exceptional circumstances and the proposed variations and the Owners shall not be entitled to submit the Community Building Detailed Specification to SCDC pursuant to paragraph 6 until such time as SCDC has approved or is deemed to have approved pursuant to clause 18 the proposed variations or the Owners withdraw their proposed variations and notify SCDC in writing of such withdrawal.

6. The Community Building Detailed Specification shall be submitted to SCDC for approval and subject to para 7 below such Community Building Detailed Specification shall be approved by SCDC (or in relation to 6.3 only deemed to have been approved by SCDC) either:

6.1 as part of Reserved Matters Applications for the Development Parcel adjacent to which the Community Building is located; or

6.2 as part of a Reserved Matters Application solely for the Community Building; or

6.3 as a standalone submission to SCDC solely for the approval of the Community Building Detailed Specification which may be submitted to SCDC at any time prior to Reserved Matters Applications being submitted to SCDC in relation to any area of land that includes land which such Reserved Matters Applications seek to designate as the Community Building Land.

7. The Community Building Detailed Specification may give rise to matters that will not affect approval or refusal of a Reserved Matters Application involving the Community Building and in those circumstances the approval or deemed approval of the Community Building Detailed Specification will be considered as if the Community Building Detailed Specification has been made as a standalone submission as opposed to part of a Reserved Matters Application and as a consequence the approval or deemed approval of the Community Building Detailed Specification may occur at a different time from the approval of any Reserved Matters Application involving the Community Building and for the avoidance of doubt any approval or deemed approval shall not be taken to imply approval of any Reserved Matters Application.

8. In the event that the Owners submit the Reserved Matters Application for the Sports Pavilion then prior to submission of the Reserved Matters Application the Owners shall undertake pre-application discussion with SCDC in relation to the exact location and boundaries for the Sports Pavilion and Sports Pavilion Land and the exact location and boundaries for the Sports Pavilion and Sports Pavilion Land shall be submitted to and approved byor deemed to have been approved by SCDC) either:
8.1 as part of Reserved Matters Applications for the Development Parcel adjacent to which the Sports Pavilion is located; or
8.2 as a Reserved Matters Application solely for the Sports Pavilion.

9. In the event that SCDC submits the Reserved Matters Application or other form of planning application for the Sports Pavilion then prior to submission of the Reserved Matters Application or other form of planning application it shall have obtained the prior written approval or deemed approval of the Owners to the exact size and location of the Sports Pavilion and in the event that such Reserved Matters Application or other form of planning application is submitted before the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer is completed then SCDC shall have also obtained the prior written approval or deemed approval of the Owners to the extent of the site in relation to which such Reserved Matters Application or other form of planning application is made.

10. Subject to para 11 below the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification shall be submitted to and approved by SCDC (or in relation to 10.3 only deemed to have been approved by SCDC) either:

10.1 as part of Reserved Matters Applications for the Development Parcel adjacent to which the Sports Pavilion is located; or

10.2 as part of a Reserved Matters Application solely for the Sports Pavilion; or

10.3 as a standalone submission to SCDC solely for the approval of the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification which may be submitted to SCDC at any time prior to Reserved Matters Applications being submitted to SCDC in relation to any area of land that includes land which such Reserved Matters Applications seek to designated as the Sports Pavilion Land.

11. The Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification may give rise to matters that will not affect approval or refusal of a Reserved Matters Application involving the Sports Pavilion and in those circumstances the approval or deemed approval of the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification will be considered as if the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification has been made as a standalone submission as opposed to part of a Reserved Matters Application and as a consequence the approval or deemed approval of the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification may occur at a different time from the approval of any Reserved Matters Application involving the Sports Pavilion and for the avoidance of doubt any approval or deemed approval shall not be taken to imply approval of any Reserved Matters Application.

12. The Owners shall:

12.1 use Reasonable Endeavours not to locate any Service Corridor on the Community Building Land; and

12.2 subject to paragraph 12.1 above the Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours to locate any Utilities and Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage within a Service Corridor (which shall have been previously approved or deemed to have been approved by SCDC relevant for the Community Building or Sports Pavilion) which shall not be located underneath the actual or anticipated building footprint of the Community Building or Sports Pavilion (as applicable) PROVIDED THAT if SCDC has not advised the Owners in writing of the actual or anticipated building footprint before the said Service Corridor has been approved or deemed to have been approved then the Owners shall be permitted to locate any Utilities and/or Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage within the approved or deemed approved Service Corridor notwithstanding that the footprint of the Community Building or Sports Pavilion (as applicable) may ultimately be constructed within the Service Corridor; and

12.3 save as otherwise specifically agreed by an Expert, not locate Utilities and/or Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage in under or over the Community Building Land or Sports Pavilion Land which are outside of the Service Corridor and/or which are under an existing or known building footprint.

## PART 2 - COMMUNITY BUILDING LAND TRANSFER AND SPORTS PAVILION LAND TRANSFER GENERIC ARRANGEMENTS

1. Prior to Occupation of any Dwelling the Owners shall serve written notice on SCDC which sets out which of the options set out in paragraphs 1.1, 1.2, 1.3 and 1.4 of this Part 2 of this Schedule 8 the Owners intend to elect to progress but subject always to the provisions as to the use of the Flexible Sum or part of the Flexible Sum as set out in Part 9 of this Schedule 8:

1.1 Option 1:

1.1.1 SCDC to construct the Community Building and for the Owners to pay the Community Building Actual Contribution to SCDC to be used for the Community Building Purpose PROVIDED THAT if the Owners elect to pursue this option the written notice shall also include an offer to transfer to SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC and if such Town Council is constituted at the time of the written notice being served on SCDC) the Community Building Land pursuant to the Community Building Land Transfer PROVIDED THAT if SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC and if such Town Council is constituted at the time of the written notice being served on SCDC) accepts the offer of the Community Building Land the actual transfer will be completed prior to Occupation of 750 Dwellings; and

1.1.2 The Owners to construct the Sports Pavilion to the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification SUBJECT TO the Sports Pavilion Cost Cap not being exceeded.

1.2 Option 2:

1.2.1 SCDC to construct the Sports Pavilion and for the Owners to pay the Sports Pavilion Actual Contribution to SCDC to be used for the Sports Pavilion Purpose PROVIDED THAT if the Owners elect to pursue this option the written notice shall also include an offer to transfer to SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC and if such Town Council is constituted at the time of the written notice being served on SCDC) the Sports Pavilion Land pursuant to the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer PROVIDED THAT if SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC and if such Town Council is constituted at the time of the written notice being served on SCDC) accepts the offer of the Sports Pavilion Land the actual transfer will be completed prior to Occupation of 350 Dwellings; and

1.2.2 The Owners to construct the Community Building to the Community Building Detailed Specification SUBJECT TO the Community Building Cost Cap (or if the Community Building Maximum Spend) not being exceeded

1.3 Option 3:- SCDC to construct both the Community Building and Sports Pavilion and the Owners to pay the Community Building Actual Contribution and Sports Pavilion Actual Contribution to SCDC to be used for the Community Building Purpose and Sports Pavilion Purpose PROVIDED THAT if the Owners elect to pursue this option the written notice shall also include an offer to transfer to SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC and if such Town Council is constituted at the time of the written notice being served on SCDC) the Community Building Land pursuant to the Community Building Land Transfer and the Sports Pavilion Land pursuant to the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer PROVIDED THAT if SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC and if such Town Council is constituted at the time of the written notice being served on SCDC) accepts the offer of the Community Building Land and the Sports Pavilion Land the actual transfer of the Community Building Land will be completed prior to Occupation of 750 Dwellings and the actual transfer of the Sports Pavilion Land will be completed prior to Occupation of 350 Dwellings

1.4 Option 4:-the Owners to construct both the Community Building to the Community Building Detailed Specification and the Sports Pavilion to the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification and to a Cost not exceeding the Community and Sports Contribution Cap

2. In the event that the Owners notify SCDC that they :

2.1 elect to pursue Option 1 then the provisions of Part 3 and Part 9 shall apply;

2.2 elect to pursue Option 2 then the provisions of Part 4 and Part 9 shall apply;

2.3 elect to pursue Option 3 then the provisions of Part 5 and Part 9 shall apply;

2.4 elect to pursue Option 4 then the provisions of Part 6 and Part 9 shall apply;

3. In the event that SCDC declines or does not accept any of the offers made to it pursuant to Options 1 2 or 3 to have the Community Building Land or Sports Pavilion Land or both areas of land transferred to it within 40 Working Days for SCDC to construct (as applicable) the Community Building or the Sports Pavilion or both the Owners shall not be obliged to transfer the Community Building Land or Sports Pavilion Land or both areas of land to SCDC nor will the Owners be obliged to pay the Community Building Contribution and Sports Pavilion Contribution (as applicable) to SCDC and the provisions of and restrictions in the relevant Options referred to above
as in respect of each of the Options 1, 2 and 3 (as applicable) shall cease to apply and
for the avoidance of doubt in any of these circumstances the Owners shall not be
obliged to construct the Community Building nor the Sports Pavilion nor otherwise
transfer or manage the Community Building Land nor the Sports Pavilion Land save
where the relevant Option indicates that the Owners have elected to construct the
Community Building or the Sports Pavilion rather than elect for SCDC to construct the
Community Building or the Sports Pavilion.

4. Whether the Owners or SCDC construct the Community Building and/or the Sports
Pavilion the following provisions shall apply and the party with whom the responsibility
to construct the Community Building and/or the Sports Pavilion rests ("the
Constructing Party") shall seek and consult with SCDC or the Owners whichever is
not the Constructing Party ("the Other Party") to obtain the prior input and
representations in writing from the Other Party in relation to the following and where
received the Contracting Party shall act in good faith in taking into account any such
input and representations before making any decisions or preparing details or taking
steps referred to below PROVIDED THAT any such representations are received
within 40 Working Days of any such request.

4.1.1 Tendering or making appointments as regards any consultants or
contractors whether internal appointments made informally or
formally of the Owners or the SCDC's staff or employees in relation
to the procurement construction and completion of the Community
Building and/or the Sports Pavilion PROVIDED THAT the written
notification from the Contracting Party to the Other Party shall also
include supporting information justifying the need for the costs or
appointments, how the costs or appointments present best value for
the project and how the anticipated notified expenditure or
appointments support the overall aim for high quality design for the
Development.

5. Where SCDC (or the Town Council as its nominee) are to construct the Sports
Pavilion and/or Community Building SCDC covenants with the Owners to use
reasonable endeavours to efficiently design, layout and construct the Sports Pavilion
and/or Community Building (as applicable) including any use of the balance of the
Land or Community Building Land where such use does not involve
the construction of building(s) with the overriding aim and commitment to the
Owners that SCDC shall only use such part of the Sports Pavilion Land and/or
Community Building Land (as applicable) as is reasonably necessary to deliver the
and/or Community Building to applicable building standards and in
the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification and Community Building
Detailed Specification (as applicable) AND PROVIDED THAT in the event that the
Reserved Matters Approvals indicate or it is otherwise agreed between SCDC and
the Owners that any part of the Community Building Land is not required for the
construction and laying out of the Community Building then SCDC covenants with
the Owners to offer to transfer such land back to Gallagher for nil consideration and
if Gallagher accepts such an offer SCDC shall complete such a transfer as soon as
practicably possible with each party bearing their own legal costs and there being no
retained in the transfer on the future use that Gallagher to put such
with such other terms restrictions and rights as may be agreed
between the parties. In the event that the Reserved Matters Approvals indicate or it
is otherwise agreed between SCDC and the Owners that any part of the Sports
Pavilion Land is not required for the construction and laying out of the Sports
Pavilion then unless otherwise agreed it shall be used as Public Open Space

## PART 3 - OPTION 1 TRANSFER AND CONSTRUCTION ARRANGEMENTS

1. In the event that the Owners notify SCDC that they elect to pursue Option 1 then
subject to both paragraph 3 in Part 2 and Part 9 of this Schedule the following
provisions shall apply.

2. The Owners shall:

2.1 execute and deliver the Community Building Land Transfer to SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) no later than Occupation of 750 Dwellings.

2.2 pay to SCDC subject to part 9 of this Schedule the Community Building Contribution in the following instalments:

2.2.1 £152,250 (one hundred and fifty two thousand two hundred and fifty British pounds) to be paid prior to Occupation of 630 Dwellings or within 20 Working Days of receipt by the Owners of written evidence from SCDC that the award of the design contract for the Community Building has occurred whichever is the later;

2.2.2 £989,625 (nine hundred and eighty nine thousand six hundred and twenty five British pounds) to be paid within 20 Working Days of receipt by the Owners of written evidence from SCDC that the award of the construction contract for the construction of the Community Building in accordance with the Community Building Detailed Specification has occurred or to a written specification which has been approved by the Owners PROVIDED THAT where such a construction contract is entered into prior to the date of the Community Building Land Transfer then payment of this sum shall be the later of 20 Working Days after the date of the Community Building Land Transfer or 20 Working Days after the date of receipt by the Owners of written evidence referred to above; and

2.2.3 £380,625 (three hundred and eighty thousand six hundred and twenty five British pounds) is to be paid within 140 Working Days of the date of the payment made pursuant to paragraph 2.2.2 above.

3. SCDC shall accept the offer of the Community Building Land Transfer (or the Town Council may accept the offer instead of SCDC if so nominated by SCDC) and:

3.1 SCDC shall use Reasonable Endeavours to construct the Community Building no later than 18 calendar months after the date the Community Building Land Transfer is completed subject always to having received the payments in accordance with paragraph 2.2 above; and

3.2 SCDC shall construct the Community Building in accordance with the approved Community Building Detailed Specification or to an alternative written specification which is approved or deemed to have been approved by the Owners pursuant to clause 18 PROVIDED ALWAYS THAT SCDC shall only be required to construct and complete the Community Building to a standard of construction and fit out where the cost of construction fit out and completion to the point where the Community Building can be opened to the general public and that meets the Community Building Actual Contribution and does not exceed the Community Building Maximum Spend unless SCDC elects to use funds available to it from other sources to construct fit out and complete to a higher standard PROVIDED THAT such funds shall not be permitted to be ones derived from the mitigation and/or contributions received by SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) or the County Council from the Owners in relation to the Development.

4. The Owners shall construct the Sports Pavilion in accordance with the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification and in accordance with the provisions of paragraphs 8, 10, 11 and 12 of Part 1 of this Schedule 8 and shall complete such construction and provide a Certificate of Practical Completion for the Sports Pavilion prior to the Occupation of 500 Dwellings.

5. The written notice referred to in paragraph 1 of Part 2 setting out the Owners' election to proceed with Option 1 shall also include and provide for one of the following as regards future management and transfer arrangements:

5.1 An offer to transfer to SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) the Sports Pavilion Land and completed Sports Pavilion PROVIDED THAT if SCDC accepts the offer of the Sports Pavilion Land and completed Sports Pavilion the Owners shall execute and deliver to SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer after the Certificate of Practical Completion is issued; or

5.2 The written notice shall notify SCDC that the Owners wish to offer Sports Pavilion Land and the Sports Pavilion pursuant to the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer to an Alternative Body PROVIDED THAT in such notification to set out the name, address, company registration number (where appropriate) and evidence that the Alternative Body is constituted and this information shall be provided to SCDC in order that SCDC can make a decision whether or not to approve the Alternative Body a suitable entity as regards the management and maintenance of the Sports Pavilion Land and the Sports Pavilion PROVIDED FURTHER THAT the written offer of a transfer of the Sports Pavilion Land and the Sports Pavilion to the Alternative Body will not be permitted until such time as the Alternative Body has been approved or deemed approved by SCDC pursuant to the Approval Process AND PROVIDED FURTHER if the Alternative Body accepts the offer of the Sports Pavilion Land the Owners shall execute and deliver to the Alternative Body the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer after the Certificate of Practical Completion is issued but in any event subject to Part 7 of this Schedule; or

5.3 The written notice will confirm that the Owners will either:

5.3.1 retain and maintain the Sports Pavilion Land and Sports Pavilion and to appoint a Management Company to maintain and manage the Sports Pavilion Land and Sports Pavilion on the Owners' behalf; or

5.3.2 transfer the Sports Pavilion Land and Sports Pavilion to the Management Company subject to Part 7 of this Schedule

PROVIDED THAT the Owners shall not be required to commit to which of these two options the Owners will pursue at the date the written notice is served and the Owners shall only be required to notify SCDC that they intend to pursue an option involving a Management Company PROVIDED always that the Owners shall, unless otherwise agreed with SCDC notify which of those options is being chosen within 12 months of the initial written notice or the letting of the construction contract whichever is the earlier.

6. The Owners shall construct and equip the Sports Pavilion in accordance with the Detailed Specification approved or deemed to have been approved and the provisions of paragraphs 10 and 11 of Part 1 of this Schedule 8 shall be applied equally in relation to the approval of the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification to the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification and the construction of the Sports Pavilion shall not require or otherwise specify standards materials or equipment associated with the design or construction or fit out or equipping of the Sports Pavilion that would result in a combined cost to construct and fit out and equip the Sports Pavilion Cost Cap.

7. No more than 500 Dwellings shall be Occupied until:

7.1 a Certificate of Practical Completion for the Sports Pavilion has been issued; and

7.2 subject to Part 7 of this Schedule 8 where the Owners have elected to transfer the Sports Pavilion Land and Sports Pavilion Land to either SCDC or an Alternative Body and
the Owners have executed and delivered the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer to SCDC or the Alternative Body (as applicable); or

7.3 subject to Part 7 of this Schedule where the Owners have elected to transfer the Sports Pavilion Land and Sports Pavilion Land to a Management Company the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer has been completed; or

7.4 where the Owners have elected to retain ownership of the Sports Pavilion and Sports Pavilion Land that a Management Company has been appointed to manage and maintain the Sports Pavilion Land and Sports Pavilion on the Owners' behalf.

8. In the event that SCDC or the Alternative Body declines or does not respond to the offer to have transferred to it the Sports Pavilion Land and Sports Pavilion within 40 Working Days of service of the respective offer to transfer the Owners shall :

8.1 where the offer has been made to SCDC the Owners will notify SCDC in writing that it intends either to

  8.1.1 transfer the Sports Pavilion Land to an Alternative Body subject to Part 7 of this Schedule in which case any such notification the Owners shall set out the name, address, company registration number (where appropriate) and evidence that the Alternative Body is constituted and this information shall be provided to SCDC in order that SCDC can make a decision whether or not to approve the Alternative Body a suitable entity or organisation as regards the management and maintenance of the Sports Pavilion Land and the Sports Pavilion; or

  8.1.2 transfer the Sports Pavilion Land a Management Company subject to Part 7 of this Schedule or

  8.1.3 retain the Sports Pavilion Land and Sports Pavilion and appoint a Management Company to manage and maintain the Sports Pavilion Land and Sports Pavilion on the Owners' behalf

AND the Owners shall be entitled to transfer or otherwise deal with the Sports Pavilion Land and Sports Pavilion in accordance with the provisions of the notice served pursuant to paragraph 1 of Part 2 of this Schedule 8 PROVIDED THAT a transfer to the Alternative Body will not be permitted until such time as the Alternative Body has been approved or deemed approved by SCDC pursuant to the Approval Process.

8.2 Where the offer has been made to an Alternative Body the Owners will notify SCDC in writing that it intends to either

  8.2.1 transfer the Sports Pavilion Land to a Management Company subject to Part 7 of this Schedule; or

  8.2.2 retain the Sports Pavilion Land and Sports Pavilion and appoint a Management Company to manage and maintain the Sports Pavilion Land and Sports Pavilion on the Owners' behalf

and the Owners shall be entitled to transfer or otherwise deal with the Sports Pavilion Land and Sports Pavilion in accordance with the provisions of the notice served pursuant to paragraph 8 2 of Part 3 of this Schedule 8.

## PART 4 - OPTION 2- TRANSFER AND CONSTRUCTION ARRANGEMENTS

1. In the event that the Owners notify SCDC that they elect to pursue Option 2 then subject to paragraph 3 in Part 2 and Part 9 of this Schedule 8 the following provisions shall apply:

2. The Owners shall:

  2.1 execute and deliver the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer to SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) no later than Occupation of 350 Dwellings; and

  2.2 pay subject to Part 9 of this Schedule the Sports Pavilion Contribution Pay to SCDC in
        the following instalments:

  2.2.1 £110,000 (one hundred and ten thousand British pounds) is to be paid prior
            to Occupation of 230 Dwellings or within 20 Working Days of receipt by the
            Owners of written evidence from SCDC that the award of the design contract
            for the Sports Pavilion has occurred whichever is the later;

  2.2.2 £715,000 (seven hundred and fifteen thousand British pounds) to be paid
            within 20 Working Days of receipt by the Owners of written evidence from
            SCDC that an award of the construction contract for the construction of the
            Sports Pavilion in accordance with the Sports Pavilion Building Detailed
            Specification or to a written specification which stipulates levels of design,
            standards, materials and equipment that are not less then that set out in the
            Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification PROVIDED THAT where such a
            construction contract is entered into prior to the date of the Sports Pavilion
            Land Transfer then payment of this sum shall be the later of 20 Working
            Days after the date of the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer or 20 Working Days
            after the date of receipt by the Owners of written evidence referred to above;
            and

  2.2.3 £275,000 (two hundred and seventy five thousand British pounds) to be paid
            within 140 Working Days of the date of the payment made pursuant to
            paragraph 2.2.2 of this Part 4 above.

3. SCDC shall accept the offer of the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer (or the Town Council
    may accept the offer instead of SCDC if so nominated by SCDC) and

  3.1 SCDC shall use Reasonable Endeavours to construct the Sports Pavilion no later than
        18 calendar months after the date the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer is completed
        subject always to having received the payments in accordance with paragraph 2.2
        above;

  3.2 SCDC shall construct the Sports Pavilion in accordance with the approved Sports
        Pavilion Detailed Specification or to an alternative written specification which is
        approved or deemed to have been approved by the Owners pursuant to clause 18
        PROVIDED ALWAYS that SCDC shall only be required to construct and complete the
        Sports Pavilion to a standard of construction and fit out where the cost of construction
        to the point the Sports Pavilion can be opened to the general
        public that that meets the Sports Pavilion Minimum Spend but does not exceed the
        Sports Pavilion Contribution unless SCDC elects to use funds available to it from other
        sources to construct fit out and complete to a higher standard PROVIDED THAT such
        funds shall not be permitted to be ones derived from the mitigation and/or
        contributions received by SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) or
        from the Owners in relation to the Development.

4. The Owners shall construct the Community Building in accordance with the
    Community Building Detailed Specification and in accordance with the provisions of
    Part 8 of this Schedule 8 and shall complete such construction and provide a
    Certificate of Practical Completion for the Community Building prior to the Occupation
    of 900 Dwellings.

5. The written notice referred to in paragraph Paragraph 1 of Part 2 setting out the
    Owners election to proceed with Option 2 shall also include an offer to transfer to
    SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC and if such Town Council is
constituted at the time of the written notice being served on SCDC) the Community Building Land and the completed Community Building pursuant to the Community Building Land Transfer PROVIDED THAT if SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) accepts the offer of the Community Building Land and the Community Building the actual transfer will be completed 28 days after the date the Certificate of Practical Completion is issued unless otherwise agreed in writing by the Council.

6. In the event that SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) declines in writing or does not respond to the offer to have transferred to it the Community Building and Community Building Land within 40 Working Days of service of the offer the Owners shall serve written notice on SCDC which contains one of the following options that the Owners intend to pursue:

6.1 the Owners wish to offer Community Building Land and the Community Building Building Land Transfer to an Alternative Body PROVIDED THAT such notification shall set out the name, address, company registration number (where appropriate) and evidence that the Alternative Body is constituted and this information shall be provided to SCDC in order that SCDC can make a decision whether or not to approve the Alternative Body as a suitable entity or organisation for the purposes of the management and maintenance of the Community Building Land and the Community Building PROVIDED FURTHER THAT the written offer of a Community Building Land and the Community Building to the Alternative Body will not be permitted until such time as the Alternative Community Body has been approved or deemed approved by SCDC pursuant to the Approval Process AND PROVIDED FURTHER if the Alternative Body accepts the offer of the Building Land and the Community Building the actual transfer will be completed 28 days after the date the Certificate of Practical Completion is issued; or

6.2 the written notice may confirm that the Owners will either:

6.2.1 retain and maintain the Community Building Land and Community Building and to appoint a Management Company to maintain and manage the Community Building Land and Community Building on the Owners’ behalf; or

6.2.2 transfer the Community Building Land and Community Building to the Management Company subject to Part 7 of this Schedule.

PROVIDED THAT the Owners shall not be required to commit to which of these two options in paragraph 5.2 the Owners will pursue at the date the written notice is served and the Owners shall only be required to notify SCDC that they intend to pursue an option involving a Management Company PROVIDED ALWAYS that the Owners shall unless otherwise agreed with SCDC notify which of those options is being chosen within 12 months of the initial written notice or the letting of the construction contract whichever is the earlier.

7. The Owners shall construct and equip the Community Building in accordance with the Community Building Detailed Specification approved or deemed to have been pursuant to paragraph 6 of Part 1 of this Schedule 8 and in doing so to comply with the requirements of Part 8 this Schedule PROVIDED THAT the Community Building Detailed Specification and the construction of the Community Building shall not require or otherwise specify standard materials or equipment associated with the design or construction or fit out or equipping of the Community Building that would result in combined Costs that would exceed the Community Building Maximum Spend.

8. No more than 900 Dwellings shall be Occupied:

8.1.1 until a Certificate of Practical Completion for the Community Building has been issued; and

8.1.2 subject to the provisions of paragraph 9 of this Part 4 of Schedule 8 where the Owners have elected to transfer the Community Building and Community Building Land to either SCDC or an Alternative Body subject to Part 7 of this Schedule until the Owners have executed and delivered to SCDC or the Alternative Body (as applicable) the Community Building Land Transfer, or

8.1.3 where pursuant to para 6.2.2 of this Part 4 above the Owners have elected to transfer the Community Building and Community Building Land to a Management Company subject to Part 7 of this Schedule 8 the Community Building Land Transfer has been completed; or

8.1.4 where pursuant to para 6.2.1 of this Part 4 above the Owners have elected to retain ownership of the Community Building and Community Building Land until Management Company has been appointed to manage and maintain the Community Building Land and Community Building on the Owners' behalf.

9. In the event that SCDC or the Alternative Body declines or does not respond to the offer to have transferred to SCDC it the Community Building Land and Community Building within 40 Working Days of service of the respective offer to transfer the Owners shall either:

9.1 Where the offer has been made to SCDC the Owners will notify SCDC in writing that it intends either to

9.1.1 transfer the Community Building Land to an Alternative Body subject to Part 7 of this Schedule in which case any such notification the Owners shall set out the name, address, company registration number (where appropriate) and evidence that the Alternative Body is constituted and this information shall be provided to SCDC in order that SCDC can make a decision whether to approve the Alternative Body a suitable entity or organisation as regards the management and maintenance of the Community Building Land and the Community Building; or

9.1.2 transfer the Community Building Land to a Management Company subject to Part 7 of this Schedule; or

9.1.3 retain the Community Building Land and Community Building and appoint a Management Company to manage and maintain the Community Building Land and Community Building on the Owners' behalf.

AND the Owners shall be entitled to transfer or otherwise deal with the Community Building Land and Community Building in accordance with the provisions of the notice served pursuant to paragraph 9.1 of this Part 4 of Schedule 8 PROVIDED THAT a transfer to the Alternative Body will not be permitted until such time as the Alternative Body has been approved or deemed approved by SCDC pursuant to the Approval Process.

9.2 Where the offer has been made to an Alternative Body the Owners will notify SCDC in writing that it intends to either

9.2.1 transfer the Community Building Land to a Management Company; or

9.2.2 retain the Community Building Land and Community Building and appoint a Management Company to manage and maintain the Community Building Land and Community Building on the Owners' behalf;

And the Owners shall be entitled to transfer or otherwise deal with the Community Building Land and Community Building in accordance with the provisions of the notice served pursuant to this paragraph 9.2. of this Part 4 of this Schedule 8.

## PART 5 - OPTION 3 - TRANSFER AND CONSTRUCTION ARRANGEMENTS

1. In the event that the Owners notify SCDC that they elect to pursue Option 3 then subject to both paragraph 3 in Part 2 and Part 9 of this Schedule the following provisions shall apply:

2. SCDC shall accept the offer of the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer (or the Town Council may accept the offer instead of SCDC if so nominated by SCDC) and

2.1 SCDC shall use Reasonable Endeavours to construct the Sports Pavilion no later than 18 calendar months after the date the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer is completed; subject always to having received the payments in accordance with paragraph 3.3 below

2.2 SCDC shall construct the Sports Pavilion in accordance with the approved Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification or to an alternative written specification which is approved or deemed to have been approved by the Owners pursuant to clause 18 PROVIDED ALWAYS that SCDC shall be required to construct and complete the Sports Pavilion to a standard of construction and fit out where the cost of construction fit out and completion to the point the Sports Pavilion can be opened to the general public that meets the Sports Pavilion Minimum Spend but does not exceed the Sports Pavilion Actual Contribution unless SCDC elects to use funds available to it from other sources to construct fit out and complete to a higher standard PROVIDED THAT such funds shall not be permitted to be ones derived from the mitigation and/or contributions received by SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) or the County Council from the Owners in relation to the Development.

3. SCDC shall accept the offer of the Community Building Land Transfer (or the Town Council may accept the offer instead of SCDC if so nominated by SCDC) and

3.1 SCDC shall use Reasonable Endeavours to construct the Community Building no later than 18 calendar months after the date the Community Building Land Transfer is completed; subject always to having received the payments in accordance with paragraph 4 below

3.2 SCDC shall construct the Community Building in accordance with the approved Community Building Detailed Specification or to an alternative written specification which is approved or deemed to have been approved by the Owners pursuant to clause 18 PROVIDED ALWAYS that SCDC shall be required to construct and complete the Community Building to a standard of construction and fit out where the cost of construction fit out and completion to the point the Community Building can be opened to the general public that meets the Community Building Actual Contribution and does not exceed the Community Building Maximum Spend unless SCDC elects to use funds available to it from other sources construct fit out and complete to a higher standard PROVIDED THAT such funds shall not be permitted to be ones derived from the mitigation and/or contributions received by SCDC (or the Town Council) or the County Council from the Owners in relation to the Development.

3.3 The Owners shall subject to Part 9 of this Schedule pay to SCDC the Sports Pavilion Contribution in the following instalments:

3.3.1 £110,000 (one hundred and ten thousand British pounds) is to be paid prior to Occupation of 230 Dwellings or within 20 Working Days of receipt by the Owners of written evidence from SCDC that the award of the design contract for the Sports Pavilion has occurred whichever is the later;

3.3.2 £715,000 (seven hundred and fifteen thousand British pounds) to be paid within 20 Working Days of receipt by the Owners of written evidence from SCDC that an award of the construction contract for the construction of the Sports Pavilion in accordance with the Sports Pavilion Building Detailed
Specification or to a written specification which stipulates levels of design, standards, materials and equipment that are not less then that set out in the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification PROVIDED THAT where such a construction contract is entered into prior to the date of the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer then payment of this sum shall be the later of 20 Working Days after the date of the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer or 20 Working Days after the date of receipt by the Owners of written evidence referred to above; and

3.3.3 £275,000 (two hundred and seventy five thousand British pounds) to be paid within 140 Working Days of the date of the payment made pursuant to paragraph 3.3.2 above.

4. The Owners shall SUBJECT TO Part 9 of this Schedule pay the Community Building Contribution to SCDC in the following instalments:

4.1 £152,250 (one hundred and fifty two thousand two hundred and fifty British pounds) is to be paid prior to Occupation of 630 Dwellings or within 20 Working Days of receipt by the Owners of written evidence from SCDC that the award of the design contract for the Community Building whichever is the later

4.2 £989,625 (nine hundred and eighty nine thousand six hundred and twenty five British pounds) is to be paid within 20 Working Days of receipt by the Owners of written evidence from SCDC that an award of the construction contract for the construction of the Community Building in accordance with the Community Building Detailed Specification or to a written specification which stipulates levels of design, standards, materials and equipment that are not less than that set out in the Community Building Detailed Specification PROVIDED THAT where such a construction contract is entered into prior to the date of the Community Building Land Transfer then payment of this sum shall be the later of 20 Working Days after the date of the Community Building Land Transfer or 20 Working Days after the date of receipt by the Owners of written evidence referred to above; and

4.3 £380,625 (three hundred and eighty thousand six hundred and twenty five British pounds) is to be paid within 140 Working Days of the date of the payment made pursuant to paragraph 4.2 of this Part 5 of Schedule 8 above.

## PART 6 - OPTION 4 - TRANSFER AND CONSTRUCTION ARRANGEMENTS

1. In the event that the Owners notify SCDC that they elect to pursue Option 4 then subject to both paragraph 3 in Part 2 and Part 9 of this Schedule the following provisions shall apply:

2. The written notice referred to in paragraph 1 of Part 2 of this Schedule 8 shall also include an offer to transfer to SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC and if such Town Council is constituted at the time of the written notice being served on SCDC) the Community Building Land and the completed Community Building Community Building Land Transfer and Sports Pavilion Land and the completed Sports Pavilion PROVIDED THAT if SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) accepts the offer of the Community Building Land and the Community Building and the Sports Pavilion Land and Sports Pavilion the actual transfer of each will be completed 28 days after the relevant Certificate of Practical Completion is issued unless otherwise agreed in writing by the Council PROVIDED FURTHER THAT SCDC shall be entitled to accept the offer of one and/or both of (i) the Community Building and Community Building Land or (ii) Sports Pavilion and Sports Pavilion Land.

3. In the event that SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) declines in writing or does not respond to the offer to have transferred to it the Community Building and Community Building Land and/or Sports Pavilion and Sports Pavilion
Land within 40 Working Days of service of the offer to transfer the Owners shall serve
written notice on SCDC which contains one of the following options that the Owners
intend to pursue for that land and building(s) that SCDC (or the Town Council) has
declined or in relation to which has not responded to the offer and for the purposes of
the remaining provisions of this Part 6 of Schedule 8 any reference to "Land and
Building" shall be taken to mean as applicable whichever of the Community Building
Land and Community Building and Sports Pavilion and Sports Pavilion Land is
relevant in relation to which SCDC or the Town Council has not accepted an offer;

3.1 the Owners wish to offer the Land and Buildings pursuant to the Community Building
Land Transfer or Sports Pavilion Land Transfer ( as applicable) to an Alternative Body
subject to Part 7 of this Schedule (PROVIDED THAT such notification shall set out the
name, address, company registration number (where appropriate) and evidence that
the Alternative Body is constituted and this information shall be provided to SCDC in
order that SCDC can make a decision whether or not to approve the Alternative Body
or organisation for the purposes of the management and
maintenance of the Land and Building PROVIDED FURTHER THAT the written offer
of a transfer of the Land and Building to the Alternative Body will not be permitted until
the Alternative Community Body has been approved or deemed
approved by SCDC pursuant to the Approval Process AND PROVIDED FURTHER
subject to Part 7 of this Schedule if the Alternative Body accepts the offer of the Land
and Building the actual transfer will be completed 28 days after the date the relevant
Certificate of Practical Completion is issued; or

3.2 the written notice may confirm that the Owners will either:

3.2.1 retain and maintain the Land and Building and to appoint a Management
Company to maintain and manage the Land and Building on the Owners'
behalf; or

3.2.2 transfer the Land and Building to the Management Company subject to Part
7 of this Schedule

PROVIDED THAT the Owners shall not be required to commit to which of these two
options in paragraph 3.2 the Owners will pursue at the date the written notice is
served and the Owners shall only be required to notify SCDC that they intend to
pursue an option involving an Alternative Community Body

4. The Owners shall construct and equip the:

4.1 Sports Pavilion in accordance with the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification approved
or deemed to have been approved pursuant to paragraph 10 of Part 1 if Schedule 8
and shall construct and complete the Sports Pavilion to Costs that meet at least the
Sports Pavilion Minimum Spend but shall not SUBJECT TO Part 9 of this Schedule
exceed the Sports Pavilion Cost Cap; and

4.2 Community Building in accordance with the Community Building Detailed Specification
approved or deemed to have been approved pursuant to paragraph 6 of Part 1 of this
Schedule and in doing so to comply with the requirements of part 8 of this Schedule
and shall construct and complete the Community Building to Costs that meet at least
the Community Building Actual Contribution but shall not SUBJECT TO Part 9 of this
Schedule exceed the Community Building Cost Cap (and if applicable the Community
Building Maximum Spend)

PROVIDED THAT SUBJECT TO Part 9 of this Schedule the Community Building
Detailed Specification and the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification and the
construction of either the Community Building and/or the Sports Pavilion shall not
require or otherwise specify standards materials or equipment associated with the
design or construction or fit out or equipping of the Community Building and/or Sports
Pavilion that would result in combined Costs that would exceed the Community and
Sports Contribution Cap.

5. No more than 800 Dwellings shall be Occupied:

5.1 until a Certificate of Practical Completion for the Community Building has been issued;
    and

5.2 subject to the provisions of paragraph 7.2 below where the Owners have elected to
    transfer the Community Building and Community Building Land to either SCDC or an
    Alternative Body subject to Part 7 of this Schedule until the Owners have executed
    and delivered to SCDC or the Alternative Body (as applicable) the Community Building
    Land Transfer; or

5.3 where the Owners have elected to transfer the Community Building and Community
    Building Land to a Management Company the Community Building Land Transfer has
    been completed; or

5.4 where the Owners have elected to retain ownership of the Community Building and
    Community Building Land until Management Company has been appointed to manage
    and maintain the Community Building Land and Community Building on the Owners'
    behalf.

6. The Owners shall not permit the Occupation of more than 500 Dwellings until:

6.1 a Certificate of Practical Completion for the Sports Pavilion has been issued; and

6.2 subject to the provisions of paragraph 7.2 below where the Owners have elected to
    transfer the Sports Pavilion and Sports Pavilion Land to either SCDC or an Alternative
    Body subject to Part 7 of this Schedule and the Owners have executed and delivered
    to SCDC or the Alternative Body (as applicable) the Sports Pavilion Land Transfer; or

6.3 where the Owners have elected to transfer the Sports Pavilion and Sports Pavilion
    Land to a Management Company subject to Part 7 of this Schedule the Sports
    Pavilion Land Transfer has been completed; or

6.4 where the Owners have elected to retain ownership of the Sports Pavilion and Sports
    Pavilion Land that a Management Company has been appointed to manage and
    maintain the Sports Pavilion Land and Sports Pavilion on the Owners' behalf.

7. In the event that SCDC or the Alternative Body declines or does not respond to the
    offer to have transferred to SCDC it the Land and Building within 20 Working Days of
    service of the respective offer to transfer the Owners shall either:

7.1 Where the offer has been made to SCDC the Owners will notify SCDC in writing that it
    intends either to

7.1.1 transfer the Land and Building to an Alternative Body subject to Part 7 of this
    Schedule in which case any such notification the Owners shall set out the
    name, address, company registration number (where appropriate) and
    evidence that the Alternative Body is constituted and this information shall
    be provided to SCDC in order that SCDC can make a decision whether or
    not to approve the Alternative Body a suitable entity or organisation as
    regards the management and maintenance of the Land and Building; or

7.1.2 transfer the Land and Building to a Management Company subject to Part 7
    of this Schedule; or

7.1.3 retain the Land and Building and appoint a Management Company to
    manage and maintain the Land and Building on the Owners' behalf
AND the Owners shall be entitled to transfer or otherwise deal with the Land and Building in accordance with the provisions of the notice served pursuant to paragraph 7.1 PROVIDED THAT a transfer to the Alternative Body will not be permitted until such time as the Alternative Body has been approved or deemed approved by SCDC pursuant to the Approval Process.

7.2 Where the offer has been made to an Alternative Body the Owners will notify SCDC in writing that it intends to either

7.2.1 transfer the Land and Building to a Management Company subject to Part 7 of this Schedule; or

7.2.2 retain the Land and Building and appoint a Management Company to manage and maintain the Land and Building on the Owners' behalf;

and the Owners shall be entitled to transfer or otherwise deal with the Land and Building in accordance with the provisions of the notice served pursuant to this paragraph 7.2 of this Part 6 of Schedule 8.

## PART 7 - TRANSFER OF LAND TO AN ALTERNATIVE BODY OR MANAGEMENT COMPANY

1. Where the Community Building or the Sports Pavilion is to be transferred by the Owners to an Alternative Body or a Management Company pursuant to this Schedule 8 the Owners shall transfer it on the following terms:

1.1 the transfer shall be of the leasehold interest and it shall be for no more than 10 years, or

1.2 the Owners shall retain the freehold interest in the Community Building and/or the Sports Pavilion and the Owners shall appoint a Management Company to manage and maintain the Community Building and/or the Sports Pavilion on the Owners' behalf; and

1.3 in the event of a transfer of a leasehold interest to a Management Company the Owners shall be under an obligation to use all Reasonable Endeavours to complete such leasehold transfer as soon as reasonably possible following the date of appointment of the Management Company and if within the first 6 months of the said appointment of the Management Company the leasehold transfer has not been completed the Owners shall, if requested, be required subject to SCDC acting reasonably in making any such request, provide evidence to demonstrate why there is any continuing delay in completion of the leasehold interest being transferred and for the avoidance of doubt the Owners shall thereafter continue to be under an obligation to use all Reasonable Endeavours to complete the leasehold transfer as soon as reasonably possible.

1.4 In the event that a Management Company is appointed by the Owners to manage the Community Building and/or the Sports Pavilion or the Management Company or an Alternative Body takes a transfer of a leasehold interest in the Community Building and/or the Sports Pavilion then after a period of 10 years of the earlier from (i) the date a Management Company is appointed by the Owners or (ii) the leasehold Management Company is completed the following shall apply:

1.4.1 the Owners shall offer to transfer all of the freehold interests that it retains in Community Building Land and/or the Sports Pavilion Land to SCDC or if SCDC so nominates the Town Council provided the Town Council has been properly constituted and has the ability to accept a transfer of land and in either case for nil consideration pursuant to the relevant Community Building Sports Pavilion Land Transfer and in the event SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) has not accepted the offer within 60
Working Days then the offer shall be treated as withdrawn and the Owners
shall be permitted to transfer all of the freehold interests that it retains in the
Community Building and/or the Sports Pavilion Land to the Management
Company.

1.4.2 If a Town Council has not been properly constituted and SCDC is not willing
to accept the offer of transfer then the Owners shall be permitted to transfer
all of the freehold interests that it remains in the Community Building and/or
the Sports Pavilion to the Management Company.

PROVIDED ALWAYS THAT nothing shall compel the Owners to terminate an existing
lease other than in accordance with any pre-existing break clause within that lease
and FOR THE AVOIDANCE OF DOUBT nothing within this paragraph shall require
the Owners to incur any expense in transferring the freehold interests to SCDC or the
Town Council save that the Owners and SCDC or the Town Council shall bear their
own legal and administrative costs in relation to the transfer of the freehold interests.

## PART 8 - REQUIREMENTS TO BE OBSERVED BY THE OWNERS WHEN CONSTRUCTING* THE COMMUNITY BUILDING AND SPORTS PAVILION**

1. The Owners shall notify the Surveyor of all site meetings relating to the construction or
equipping of the Community Building.

2. The Owners shall permit the Surveyor:

2.1 to attend and to put forward his/her views at all site meetings during the course of
construction and fitting out/equipping of the Community Building;

2.2 on reasonable notice to inspect the Community Building during the course of
construction/fitting out;

2.3 to suggest items for inclusion in any necessary schedule of defects issued to the
contractor in respect of the Community Building and the Owners acknowledge that the
costs of remedying any Defects shall be undertaken at the Owners' expense and shall
not be taken into account in determining if the Community Building Cost Cap has been
reached or exceeded;

2.4 to stipulate any items or areas where the Community Building does not meet the final
agreed Community Building Detailed Specification (which forms part of the contract for
the construction of the Community Building) then SCDC may serve on the Owners a
written notice which shall specify those parts of the Community Building Detailed
Specification that have not been met and the Owners shall within 20 Working Days
from the date of service of such notice (or such other period as SCDC may agree)
carry out such works as are necessary to secure compliance with the Community
Building Detailed Specification at the expense of the Owners and the Owners shall
fund any and all reasonable subsequent inspections of the Community Building until
the approved Community Building Detailed Specification shall have been met
PROVIDED FURTHER THAT the costs of such inspections do not result in the
Community Building Cost Cap being exceeded.

3. Not less than 20 Working Days prior to the anticipated issue of a Certificate of
Practical Completion in respect of the Community Building the Owners shall in writing
invite the Surveyor to inspect the Community Building and identify any Defects
requiring attention.

4. The Owners shall be entitled to issue the Certificate of Practical Completion if after the
expiry of 40 Working Days after the invitation made pursuant to paragraph 3 of this
Part 8 of this Schedule any of the following apply:

4.1 the Surveyor has failed to inspect the Community Building; or

4.2 the Surveyor has failed to notify the Owners in writing of any Defects such notification
having been received by the Owners within the said 20 Working Days; or

4.3 the Surveyor has indicated following an inspection there are no Defects to remedy; or

4.4 where the Surveyor (acting reasonably) has identified any Defects and where all such
Defects have been properly remedied to the Surveyor’s reasonable satisfaction
provided that the provisions of clause 17 shall apply in the event of dispute as to:

4.4.1 whether any Defects identified by the Surveyor are reasonable and should
be remedied or not; and/or

4.4.2 whether the Defects have been properly remedied.

5. The Owners shall send the Surveyor copies of all instructions issued to a contractor in
respect of the Community Building at the same time as issuing them and a copy of
any Certificate of Practical Completion, list of Defects or Certificate of Final
Completion issued in respect of the Community Building within five Working Days of
its receipt by the Owners AND for the avoidance of doubt the Owners shall obtain a
Certificate of Practical Completion and Certificate of Final Completion themselves and
shall not be issued by the Surveyor SAVE THAT the Owners shall
have complied with the provisions of paragraph 3 of this Part 8 above in relation to the
issue of the Certificate of Practical Completion only.

6. Notwithstanding any Transfer of the Community Building to SCDC or an Alternative
Body or Management Company when a Certificate of Practical Completion is issued:

6.1 the Owners shall be responsible for rectification of any and all material Defects which
manifest themselves during a period of 12 months after the issue of a Certificate of
Practical Completion is issued and thereafter (subject to the law relating to latent
defects) the Owners shall procure that a Certificate of Final Completion is obtained,
and

6.2 after the end of the 12 months period as aforesaid and before any Certificate of Final
Completion is issued the Owners will procure an independent surveyor’s report
addressed to SCDC or an Alternative Body or Management Company from a
reputable consultant (and which Consultant will enter into a collateral warranty in
favour of SCDC or Alternative Body or Management Company) and the Owners will
take account of and rectify any and all Defects set out by such Surveyor’s report.

6.3 the Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours to procure that with effect from the
Certificate of Practical Completion for the Community Building or if later from the date
of transfer the benefit of all guarantees and warranties relating to the design and
construction of the Community Building are assigned or otherwise, passed on to as
applicable:

6.3.1 SCDC; or

6.3.2 the Alternative Body; or

6.3.3 the Management Company

PROVDED THAT in the event of an insolvency Event on the part of the designers or
contractors of the Community Building such that the Owners having used Reasonable
Endeavours have not obtained assignment or otherwise of all guarantees and
warranties relating to the design and/or construction of the Community Building the
Owners shall obtain the necessary insurance for the benefit of SCDC the Alternative
Body or the Management Company (as applicable and if requested in writing by such
entities) in relation to defective construction and/or defective design as may be
available from commercial insurers at reasonably commercial rates and terms for a
insured term of not more than the period that the warranty or guarantee would otherwise endure for and on such insurance being put in place the obligation to obtain guarantees and warranties will cease to apply.

6.4 The Owners shall be responsible for the management and maintenance of any surface water drainage systems that serve the Community Building Land but which are in whole or part located outside of the boundaries of the Community Building Land until such time as they become adopted under an adoption Agreement with Anglian Water Services Limited or other appropriate drainage authority.

6.5 The Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours to procure that any foul and surface water drainage systems serving the Community Building Land shall be the subject of an adoption Agreement with Anglian Water Services Limited or other appropriate drainage authority by the time the Community Building Land Transfer is completed.

6.6 Where the Owners have been unable to secure the adoption of the foul and surface water drainage systems referred to under paragraph 6.5 of this Part 8 of Schedule 8 above prior to the date of the completion of the Community Building Land Transfer then such Community Building Land Transfer shall contain covenants in favour of the transferee from the Owners for themselves and their successors in title to the relevant parts of the remainder of the Site:

6.6.1 to keep those foul and surface water drainage systems referred to under paragraph 6.5 of this Part 8 of Schedule 8 above in good repair and condition; and

6.6.2 to indemnify the relevant transferee of the Community Building Land against all and any costs and claims made against the transferee in respect of the existence and condition of the same pending adoption by Anglian Water Services Limited or other appropriate drainage authority.

6.7 The responsibility for the maintenance and management of any foul and surface water drainage systems that are situated in on or under the Community Building Land which are for the use of the Community Building Land and which are not the subject of an adoption Agreement with Anglian Water Services Limited or other appropriate drainage authority shall pass to the transferee of the Community Building Land.

6.8 The Owners shall be responsible for the management and maintenance of a road intended for adoption that serves or will serve the Community Building Land and shall serving the Community Building Land and shall be built to an adoptable standard and the Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours to procure that the road serving the Community Building Land which is intended for adoption shall be the subject of an adoption Agreement with the highway authority to be entered into within a reasonable timeframe having regard to the anticipated timeframe of the completion of the Community Building.

7. The Owners shall bear the reasonable costs of the Surveyor incurred in association with the provisions of this Part 8 of Schedule 8 and for the avoidance of doubt such costs shall be taken into account as part of the Costs and associated costs caps referred to within the definition of Costs in this Schedule

8. The provisions of this Part 8 shall apply mutatis mutandis to the Sports Pavilion such that all references to Community Building in the proceeding paragraphs shall also be read as referring to the Sports Pavilion

## PART 9 – ARRANGEMENTS AS TO THE FLEXIBLE SUM (OR PART THEREOF)

1. In the event that SCDC intends to use the Flexible Sum in whole or in part towards the cost of construction and fitting out of the Community Building SCDC shall be entitled to serve a written notice on the Owners advising them of this and requiring that all of
the Flexible Sum (or such part of the Flexible Sum specified in the said notice and which is in accordance with paragraph 2 of this Part 9 AND PROVIDED ALWAYS THAT SCDC shall serve such notice as soon as reasonably possible following Commencement and in any event within 12 months of Commencement in which case (i) the Community Building Detailed Specification and the Sports Pavilion Detailed Specification shall reflect that extra monies are to be spent on the Community Building and less monies spent on the Sports Pavilion and (ii) to reflect adjustments to what would otherwise have been the Sports Pavilion Cost Cap and the Community Building Cost Cap

2. Subject to para 1 above the sums payable under para 1.2 of Part 3, para 1.2 of Part 4 , para 2.3 of Part 5 and para 4 in Part 5 shall each be adjusted on a pro rata basis depending on how much (if any) of the Flexible Sum is to be spent on the construction and fitting out of the Community Building and how much less is to be spent on the construction and fitting out of the Sports Pavilion AND FOR THE AVOIDANCE OF DOUBT the Flexible Sum shall be applied to the sums payable in the following percentages:

2.1 10% (ten percent) of any Flexible Sum payable will be paid with the first instalment; and

2.2 65% (sixty five percent) of any Flexible Sum payable will be paid with the second instalment; and

2.3 25% (twenty five percent) of any Flexible Sum payable will be paid with the final instalment

PROVIDED ALWAYS THAT subject to clause 29 nothing within this Schedule 8 shall require the Owners to exceed the Community and Sports Contribution Cap.

## PART 10 - INFORMAL SINGLE COURT MUGA

1.
