Where the Community Building Land provided pursuant to this Schedule 8 includes the Informal Single Court MUGA then the definition of the Community Building and Sports Pavilion Contributions Cap and Community Building Contribution and the Community Building Actual Contribution and the Community Building Costs Cap and the Community Building Maximum Spend for the purposes of this Schedule 8 shall increase by £37,675 PROVIDED ALWAYS that the Sports Pitches Contribution in Schedule 14 decreases by a corresponding amount.

# SCHEDULE 9 – ALLOTMENTS AND ORCHARDS

## RELEVANT DEFINITIONS

"Allotments" means the allotments which will be laid out and completed in accordance with the Allotments Specification;

"Allotments Certificate" means the certificate issued or deemed to have been issued by SCDC confirming the works to construct and complete the Allotments have been completed to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC and for the avoidance of doubt in relation to the Allotments no maintenance period will be required to be undertaken;

"Allotments Cost Cap" means £25,000 (twenty five thousand British pounds) and the costs which shall be permitted to be included within any calculation to establish whether the said cap has been exceeded shall include any costs arising in connection with any construction contract let in connection with the construction and completion of the Allotment which shall also include the provision of topsoil fencing and a single water supply;

"Allotment Land" means the area of land within the Site to be located within the area shown indicatively edged green on Plan 5 attached to this Agreement being 1.40 hectares in size (unless otherwise agreed between SCDC and the Owners) to be provided by the Owners or such other area of land of a similar shape and in a location as may be submitted by the Owners to and approved by SCDC;

"Allotment Land Transfer" means a transfer by the Owners of the:

1. unencumbered freehold interest in the Allotment Land in the event that the transfer is to SCDC or the Town Council in the form of the draft transfer appended hereto at Appendix 19 or such other form as the parties may otherwise agree; or

2. unencumbered leasehold interest in the Allotment Land in the event that the transfer is to an Alternative Body in a form of transfer to be agreed between the Owners and the Alternative Body on terms to be agreed

and each party shall bear their own legal costs incurred in connection with the negotiation and completion of such transfer but any such transfer to SCDC or the Town Council or Alternative Body shall nevertheless:

1. not include any terms which would restrict use as Allotments and any and all purposes reasonably ancillary thereto; and

2. not include any terms which would directly or
indirectly affect the construction servicing or occupation of any part of the Site that is retained by the Owners; and

3. include any reasonable reservation of rights of access and services over Allotment Land for the benefit of any other part of the Site where such rights are reserved for the purpose of laying managing maintaining replacing renewing cleaning and repairing services including but not limited to as applicable sustainable urban drainage measures, water, gas, sewerage, drainage or electricity subject to the provisions of paragraph 6 and 7 of this schedule; and

4. include for the benefit of the Allotment Land the grant of any and all reasonable rights of access and services (in relation to water supply only) over any adjoining land which rights are reasonably required for the use, management and maintenance of the Allotment Land for its purpose as allotments or public open space;

5. be at nil consideration;

6. include a covenant that the Allotment Land shall not be used for any purpose other than allotments or public open space and any and all purposes reasonably ancillary thereto to serve the residents of the Development only.

“Allotments Specification” means the specification for the Allotments which shall be agreed with SCDC in accordance with this schedule;

“Defects” means any defects in relation to the laying out and completion of the Allotments in accordance with the Allotments Specification and including the provision of an vehicular access and a single water supply to the Allotment Land;

“Orchard Land” means the area of land within the Site to be located within the area shown indicatively edged brown on Plan 5 attached to this Agreement being 0.18 hectares in size (unless otherwise agreed between SCDC and the Owners) to be provided by the Owners or such other area of land of a similar shape and in a location as may be submitted by the Owners to and approved by SCDC;

## PART 1 - ALLOTMENT LAND LOCATION AND SPECIFICATION

1. The exact location and boundaries of the Allotment Land shall be submitted to and approved by SCDC (or in relation to 1.3 only deemed to have been approved by SCDC) either:

1.1 as part of Reserved Matters Applications for the Development Parcel(s) adjacent to or within which the Allotment Land is located; or

1.2 as a Reserved Matters Applications solely for the Allotment Land.
2. The Owners shall notify SCDC in writing of the date the Owners anticipated they will
submit the Allotments Specification to SCDC for approval pursuant to paragraph 3
below PROVIDED THAT such written notification shall be served on SCDC no later
than 60 Working Days before the anticipated date for submission of the Allotments
Specification.

3. The Allotments Specification shall be submitted to SCDC either:

3.1 at the same time as a Reserved Matters Applications for any Development Parcel
adjacent to which the Allotments are located; or

3.2 at the same time as a Reserved Matters Application solely for the Allotments ; or

3.3 as a standalone submission to SCDC solely for the approval of the Allotments
Specification which may be submitted to SCDC at any time prior to Reserved Matters
Applications being submitted to SCDC in relation to any area of land that includes land
which such Reserved Matters Applications seek to designate as the Allotment Land.

4. The Allotments Specification may give rise to matters that will not affect approval or
refusal of a Reserved Matters Application involving the Allotments and in those
circumstances the approval or deemed approval of the Allotments Specification will be
considered as if the Allotments Specification has been made as a standalone
submission as opposed to part of a Reserved Matters Application and as a
consequence the approval or deemed approval of the Allotments Specification may
occur at a different time to the approval of any Reserved Matters Application involving
the Allotments.

5. The Owners shall ensure that the Allotment Land is connected to (as appropriate) the
drainage measures implemented for the rest of the Development unless the Owners
shall have previously demonstrated to SCDC’s reasonable satisfaction that the
Allotments are sufficiently free draining such that additional drainage measures are
not required.

6. The Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours:

6.1.1 to locate any Utilities within a Service Corridor (which shall have been
previously approved or deemed to have been approved by SCDC relevant
for the Allotment Land); and

6.1.2 not to locate Utilities in under or over the Allotment Land which are outside
the Service Corridor

SAVE THAT if at any time prior to completion of the Allotment Land Transfer the
Owners wish to locate Utilities in under or over the Allotment Land they shall first
obtain the prior written approval or deemed approval of SCDC to the proposed
location of such services and the provisions of clause 18 shall apply in relation to the
seeking of such approval.

7. The Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours not to locate any Foul/Surface Water
Drainage and Sewerage in under or over the Allotment Land SAVE THAT if at any
time prior to completion of the Allotment Land Transfer the Owners wish to locate the
Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage in under or over they shall first obtain the
prior written approval or deemed approval of SCDC to the proposed location and
depth of such services and the provisions of clause 18 shall apply in relation to the
seeking of such approval.

8. No later than Occupation of any Dwelling the Owners shall serve written notice on:

8.1 SCDC; or

8.2 in the event that it has been constituted and is able to accept land transfers the Town Council but only in the event that SCDC itself does not accept the transfer

offering to transfer the Allotment Land to SCDC or the Town Council pursuant to the Allotment Land Transfer PROVIDED THAT if SCDC or the Town Council provides written acceptance of the offer within a period of 60 Working Days then the actual Allotment Land Transfer will be completed by the parties following the issue of the Allotment Certificate.

9. Subject to paragraph 8 in the event that SCDC or the Town Council as appropriate declines in writing or does not respond to the offer to have transferred to it the Allotment Land within 60 Working Days of service of the offer to transfer the Owners shall offer the Allotment Land pursuant to the Alternative Body Allotment Land Transfer to an Alternative Body PROVIDED THAT such notification sets out the name, address, company registration number (where appropriate) and evidence that the Alternative Body is constituted and this information shall be provided and PROVIDED FURTHER THAT the written offer of a transfer of Allotment Land and Allotments to the Alternative Body will not be permitted until such time as the Alternative Body has been approved or deemed approved by SCDC pursuant to the Approval Process AND PROVIDED FURTHER if the Alternative Body accepts the offer of the Allotment Land and the Allotments the Owners shall execute and deliver to SCDC the Allotment Land Transfer after the Allotment Certificate is issued.

9.1 Where

9.1.1 SCDC or the Town Council or the Alternative Body decline or do not accept the offer to have transferred to it the Allotments Land as provided for in paragraphs 8 and 9 above; or

9.1.2 SCDC or the Town Council or the Alternative Body (as applicable depending on which has accepted the offer) have not exchanged contracts with the Owners in relation to the Allotments Land within 60 Working Days of contracts being issued by the Owners

the obligation to provide the Allotments and transfer the Allotment Land pursuant to this Schedule shall cease to have effect and for the avoidance of doubt the Owners shall be entitled to use the Allotment Land for alternative use as Open Space and will not be required to lay out and construct the Allotments.

10. The Owners will serve notify SCDC that it intends to commence works on the Allotments at least 20 working days before carrying out any works and shall thereafter permit the Surveyor on reasonable notice to inspect the Allotments during the course of laying out and completion.

11. Prior to the Occupation of 100 Dwellings the Owners shall complete the Allotments on the Allotment Land in accordance with the Allotments Specification PROVIDED THAT the completion of the Allotments shall not require works that would result in a combined cost to lay out and complete the Allotments that would exceed the Allotments Cost Cap.

12. Following completion of the Allotments the Owners will serve notice on SCDC inviting them to inspect the Allotments and issue an Allotments Certificate within 20 working days confirming that such works have been completed to their reasonable satisfaction.

12.1 If SCDC chooses to inspect the Allotments and identifies any Defects and necessary remedial works the Owners will:
12.1.1 complete at their own cost such necessary remedial works to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC as soon as reasonably practicable; and
12.1.2 upon completion of any remedial works, serve notice on SCDC inviting them to inspect the remedial works identified by them pursuant to paragraph 12.1 of this Part 1 of this Schedule 9 and issue an Allotment Certificate confirming that the Allotments have been completed to their reasonable satisfaction.

PROVIDED ALWAYS THAT if SCDC fails to inspect the Allotments within 25 Working Days of receipt of the notice of invitation from the Owners or fails to issue an Allotment Certificate within 25 Working Days of the inspection where no remedial works have been identified then the Allotment Certificate shall be deemed to have been issued at the end of those specified periods. PROVIDED FURTHER THAT the inspection procedure identified in paragraphs 12.1.1 and 12.1.2 shall be repeated until such time as SCDC issue an Allotment Certificate or an Allotment Certificate shall be deemed to have been issued in relation to the Allotments.

## PART 2 - ORCHARDS

1. In relation to the Orchard Land the provisions set out in Part 1 of this Schedule 9 shall apply mutatis mutandis including paragraph 5 in Part 1 unless the Owners shall have previously demonstrated to SCDC's reasonable satisfaction that the Orchard Land is sufficiently free draining and suitable for such use and such that additional drainage measures are not required in relation to the Orchard Land.

# SCHEDULE 10 - COMMUNITY SUPPORT AND DEVELOPMENT

## RELEVANT DEFINITIONS

"Community Support
Contribution" means the sum of £300,000 (three hundred thousand British pounds) to be paid by the Owners to SCDC on an instalment basis for the purpose of employing engaging and/or retaining (which for the avoidance of doubt shall include the salaries and associated costs of employment and engagement) any or all of the following: community workers, youth workers and sports workers or workers engaged in or carrying out activities associated with matters such as ecology and sustainability and/or health impact related matters and in each case whose time for the purposes of such sum shall be limited to the time that they are employed and/or engaged on activities directly related to the Development

1. COMMUNITY SUPPORT CONTRIBUTION

1.1 The Owners shall pay the SCDC the Community Support Contribution in the following instalments:

1.1.1 £60,000 (sixty thousand British pounds) prior to Implementation;

1.1.2 £120,000 (one hundred and twenty thousand British pounds) prior to Occupation of 50 Dwellings;

1.1.3 £120,000 (one hundred and twenty thousand British pounds) prior to Occupation of 250 Dwellings

1.2 Implementation shall not take place until the payment in paragraph 1.1.1 above has been made by the Owners to SCDC.

1.3 No more than 50 Dwellings shall be Occupied until the payment referred to in paragraph 1.1.2 above has been made by the Owners to SCDC.

1.4 No more than 250 Dwellings shall be Occupied until the payment referred to in paragraph 1.1.3 above has been made by the Owners to SCDC.

# SCHEDULE 11 - LAND FOR FAITH/VOLUNTARY GROUPS

RELEVANT DEFINITIONS

"Faith/Voluntary Groups"
means any faith, voluntary or community group which SCDC, the Alternative Body and/or Town Council as relevant has approved to use the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land;

"Faith/Voluntary Groups Land"
means the area or areas of Serviced land within the Site to be located within the area or areas shown indicatively with purple stars on Plan 5 attached to this Agreement being 0.25 hectares in size (unless otherwise agreed between the Owners and SCDC) to be provided as part of the overall On Site Public Open Space by the Owners in a location or locations as may be submitted by the Owners to and approved by SCDC which shall nevertheless be provided within areas of public realm including areas of formal and/or informal open space as shown on Masterplan and for the avoidance of doubt these shall not be located within any area shown on the Masterplan as for residential, retail, industrial or other commercial uses;

"Faith/Voluntary Groups Land Certificate"
means the certificate issued or deemed to have been issued by SCDC confirming the works to construct and complete the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land have been completed to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC and for the avoidance of doubt in relation to the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land no maintenance period will be required to be undertaken;

"Faith/Voluntary Groups Land Transfer"
means the transfer by the Owners of:

1. the unencumbered freehold interest in the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land in the event that it is transferred to SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC); or

2. the unencumbered leasehold interest in the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land in the event that it is transferred to an Alternative Body in accordance with paragraph 4.1 of this Schedule 11 below;

3. in the case of 1 above in the form of the draft transfer appended hereto at Appendix 20 or such other form as the parties may otherwise agree and each party shall bear their own legal costs incurred in connection with the negotiation and completion of such transfer but which shall nevertheless not include any terms which would restrict the use as Faith/Voluntary Groups Land and for the avoidance of doubt such use may include the construction of buildings on the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land; and

4. not include any terms which would directly or
5. indirectly affect the construction servicing or occupation of any part of the Site that is retained by the Owners; and
5. include reservation of rights of access and services over the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land for the benefit of any other part of the Site for the purpose of laying managing maintaining replacing renewing cleaning and repairing services including but not limited to as applicable sustainable urban drainage measures, water, gas, sewerage, drainage or electricity subject to the provisions of paragraph 4.4 of this Schedule 11 below; and
6. include for the benefit of the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land the grant of any and all reasonable rights of access and services over any adjoining land which rights are reasonably required for the use, management and maintenance of the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land for its purpose for the benefit of local faith/voluntary groups;
7. be at nil consideration; and
8. include a covenant that the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land shall not be used for any purpose other than for the benefit of local faith and voluntary groups or faith/voluntary/community groups associated with the Development and which may include any and all purposes reasonably ancillary thereto; and
9. include a covenant that any building subsequently constructed on the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land shall be used for the benefit of local faith and voluntary groups or faith/voluntary/community groups associated with the Development and which may include any and all purposes reasonably ancillary thereto.

## PART 1 - FAITH/VOLUNTARY GROUPS LAND

1. The exact location of the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land or a relevant part thereof shall be submitted to and approved by SCDC as part of the Reserved Matters Application for (i) the first Development Parcel for which a Reserved Matters Application is being submitted and which is adjacent to the relevant Faith/Voluntary Groups Land or (ii) the Development Parcel within which the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land is located. PROVIDED ALWAYS that the Owners may submit for approval each area of Faith/Voluntary Groups Land separately and differently to any other and nothing shall compel the Owners to provide the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land all together.
2. The Owners shall execute and deliver the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land Transfer prior to the Occupation of 75% of the Dwellings within the first Development Parcel adjacent to or within which the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land is located.
3. In the event that prior to Occupation of 50% of Dwellings within the first Development Parcel adjacent to or within which the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land is located the Town Council

3.1 has been established and is operational to the reasonable satisfaction of the Owners; and

3.2 is capable of accepting the transfer of land

the Owners shall serve written notice on SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) offering to transfer the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land to SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) pursuant to the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land Transfer PROVIDED THAT if SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) provides written acceptance of the offer within a period of 40 Working Days then the actual Faith/Voluntary Groups Land Transfer will be completed by the parties prior to Occupation of 75% of the Dwellings within the Development Parcel adjacent to or within which the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land is located or in the event that there is more than one the Development Parcel adjacent to or within which the relevant Faith/Voluntary Groups Land is located then it shall in respect of each transfer be in relation to the first Development Parcel which reaches Occupation of 75% of Dwellings within such Development Parcel.

4. In the event that SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) declines in writing or does not respond to the offer to have transferred to it the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land within 40 Working Days of service of the respective offer to transfer the written notice on SCDC which contains the following option that the Owners intend to pursue in respect of the leasehold interest of the relevant Faith/Voluntary Groups Land:

4.1 That the Owners wish to offer a leasehold interest of not more than 10 years in the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land to an Alternative Body PROVIDED THAT in such notification the Owners shall set out the name, address, company registration number (where appropriate) and evidence that the Alternative Body is constituted and this information shall be provided to SCDC in order that SCDC can make a decision whether or not to approve the Alternative Body as a suitable entity or organisation as regards the management and maintenance of the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land PROVIDED FURTHER THAT the written offer of a transfer of the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land to the Alternative Body will not be permitted until such time as the Alternative Body has been approved or deemed approved by SCDC pursuant to the Approval Process AND PROVIDED FURTHER if the Alternative Body accepts the offer of the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land the Owners shall execute and deliver to the Alternative Body the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land Transfer in accordance with paragraph 2 above.

4.2 Where

4.2.1 SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) or the Alternative Body decline or do not accept the offer to have transferred to it the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land as provided for in paragraphs 3 or 4.1 above; or

4.2.2 SCDC (or the Town Council if so nominated by SCDC) or the Alternative Body (as applicable depending on which has accepted the offer) have not exchanged contracts with the Owners in relation to the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land within 60 Working Days of contracts being issued by the Owners and provided the Owners have used Reasonable Endeavours on their part to achieve an exchange of contracts within such period

the obligations of this Schedule shall subject to (i) the proviso in 4.2.2 above and (ii) para 4.3 below cease to have effect unless any dispute in relation to this Schedule has
been referred to an Expert for determination in accordance with clause 18 and the obligations shall subject to para 4.3 below cease to have effect once the determination has been made by the Expert such that it does not result in the situations referred to in this paragraph 4.2 no longer applying and for the avoidance of doubt the Owners shall be entitled to use the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land for alternative uses as Public Open Space.

4.3 Irrespective of whether an Alternative Body takes a transfer of a leasehold interest in the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land pursuant to paragraph 4.1 or not then after a period of 10 years from the date of the leasehold transfer to the Alternative Body or 10 years after the Alternative Body have declined or not accepted such leasehold interest then the Owners shall offer to transfer all of the freehold interests that it retains in the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land to SCDC (or if so nominated by SCDC the Town Council and has been properly constituted and has the ability to accept a transfer of land) for nil consideration pursuant to the relevant Faith/Voluntary Groups Transfer and in the event that SCDC (or if so nominated by SCDC the Town Council) has not accepted the offer within 60 Working Days then the offer shall be treated as withdrawn and the Owners shall be permitted to transfer all of the freehold interests that it retains in the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land to an Alternative Body PROVIDED ALWAYS THAT nothing shall compel the Owners to terminate an existing lease other than in accordance with any pre-existing break clause within that lease and FOR THE AVOIDANCE OF DOUBT nothing within this paragraph shall require the Owners to incur any expense in transferring the freehold interests to SCDC (or if so nominated by SCDC the Town Council) save that the Owners and SCDC (or if so nominated by SCDC the Town Council) shall bear their own legal and administrative costs in relation to the transfer of the freehold interests

4.4 The Owners shall use Reasonable Endeavours:

4.4.1 to locate any Utilities and Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage within the Service Corridor (which shall have been previously approved or deemed to have been approved by SCDC relevant for the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land); and

4.4.2 not to locate Utilities and/or Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage in under or over the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land which are outside of the Service Corridor (which shall have been previously approved or deemed to have been approved by SCDC relevant for the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land)

SAVE THAT if at any time prior to completion of the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land Transfer the Owners wish to locate any Utilities and/or Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage in under or over the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land they shall first obtain the prior written approval or deemed approval of SCDC to the proposed location of such Utilities and/or Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage and the provisions of clause 18 shall apply in relation to the seeking of such approval.

5. Following completion of works to the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land the Owners will serve notice on SCDC inviting them to inspect the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land and issue a Faith/Voluntary Groups Land Certificate within 40 working days confirming that such works have been completed to their reasonable satisfaction and:

5.1.1 If SCDC chooses to inspect the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land and identifies any necessary remedial works the Owners will complete such remedial works to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC as soon as reasonably practicable; and

5.1.2 upon completion of any remedial works, serve notice on SCDC inviting them to inspect the remedial works identified by them pursuant to paragraph 5.1.1 of this Schedule 11 and issue a Faith/Voluntary Groups Land Certificate
confirming that the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land has been provided to their
reasonable satisfaction.

PROVIDED ALWAYS THAT if SCDC fails to inspect the Faith/Voluntary Groups Land
within 40 Working Days of receipt of the notice of invitation from the Owners or fails to
issue a Faith/Voluntary Groups Land Certificate within 40 Working Days of the
inspection where no remedial works have been identified then the Faith/Voluntary
Groups Land Certificate shall be deemed to have been issued at the end of those
specified periods PROVIDED FURTHER THAT the inspection procedure identified in
paragraphs 5.1.1 and 5.1.2 shall be repeated until such time as SCDC issue a
Faith/Voluntary Groups Land Certificate or a Faith/Voluntary Groups Land Certificate
shall been deemed to have been issued in relation to the Faith/Voluntary Groups
Land.

# SCHEDULE 12 - ENVIRONMENT & ORDNANCE
## RELEVANT DEFINITIONS

**"Air Quality Monitoring Contribution"**
means the sum of £78,550 (seventy eight thousand five
hundred and fifty British pounds) to be used for Air Quality
Monitoring Purposes;

**"Air Quality Monitoring Purposes"**
means the implementation of a monitoring programme to be
undertaken by SCDC to assess/verify the impact of the
Development (if any) both during construction and operational
phases on air quality on the area in the vicinity of the Site
(and including the local road network) and as appropriate
within the Site and which shall include the purchase
installation, operation and maintenance of such equipment as
shall be required to undertake such monitoring provided that
such equipment shall be solely used in connection with the
monitoring of the impact of the Development and to enable
SCDC to fulfil its obligations under Local Air Quality
Management as a result of the Development;

**"Longstanton Technical Purposes"**
means the procurement of technical assistance by SCDC
regarding design calculations for waterflows along the
Longstanton watercourses within the Site;

**"Longstanton Watercourse Flows Contribution"**
means the sum of £1,500 (one thousand five hundred British
pounds) to be used towards Longstanton Technical
Purposes;

**"Noise and Vibration Monitoring Equipment Contribution"**
means the sum of £31,625 (thirty one thousand six hundred
and twenty five British pounds) to be used for the purchase of
Noise and Vibration Monitoring Equipment Purposes;

**"Noise and Vibration Monitoring Equipment Purposes"**
means such noise and vibration monitoring equipment as
shall be required to accurately verify the impact of the
Development (if any) both during construction and operational
phases on noise and vibration levels in the area in the vicinity
of the Site and as appropriate within the Site provided that
such equipment shall be solely used in connection with the
monitoring of the impact of the Development

**"Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event(s)"**
means any evacuation of people and or livestock required as
a result of the discovery of unexploded ordnance on the Site

**"Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event Live Costs Demand"**
means a written demand sent jointly on behalf of SCDC
and/or the County Council that sets out the respective costs
incurred by or on behalf of both SCDC and/or the County
Council including written justification and a detailed
breakdown together with receipts setting out the separate and
individual costs incurred by or on behalf of SCDC and the
County Council and for the avoidance of doubt such a written
demand shall only seek to recover costs in arrears
PROVIDED THAT where either SCDC or the County Council
do not incur Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event Live
Costs then such written demand shall state this and must still
be submitted on behalf of both SCDC and the County Council
so that the Owners have certainty that there are no additional
costs outstanding or that will be claimed PROVIDED
FURTHER THAT only one such written demand may be
permitted to be made by SCDC and the County Council in
relation to each Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event and
which demand shall deal with any and all incurred
Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event Live Costs
associated with that Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event
SUBJECT ALWAYS that the combined costs that are the
subject of any and all such written demands shall not seek to
demand payment of more than the Unexploded Ordnance
Evacuation Live Costs Cap;

"Unexploded
Ordnance
Evacuation Event
Live Costs"
means any costs to SCDC and/or the County Council of
directly supporting and resourcing any and all Unexploded
Ordnance Evacuation Events PROVIDED THAT SCDC and
the County Council shall only be entitled to reimbursement of
their costs incurred in the required resourcing for all
Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Events;

"Unexploded
Ordnance
Evacuation Event
Live Costs Cap"
means the combined sum for the direct costs to SCDC and/or
the County Council of directly supporting and resourcing all
Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Events which shall not
total more than £60,000 (sixty thousand British pounds) per
Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event which shall be paid
to SCDC and/or the County Council in such proportions as
shall be set out in the Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Live
Costs Demand.

"Unexploded
Ordnance
Evacuation Event
Planned Costs
Demand"
means a written demand sent jointly on behalf of SCDC and
the County Council that sets out the respective costs incurred
by both SCDC and the County Council including written
justification and a detailed breakdown together with receipts
setting out the separate and individual costs incurred by
SCDC and the County Council and for the avoidance of doubt
such a written demand shall only seek to recover costs in
arrears PROVIDED THAT where either SCDC or the County
Council do not incur Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event
Planned Costs then such written demand shall state this and
must still be submitted on behalf of both SCDC and the
County Council so that the Owners have certainty that there
are no additional costs outstanding or that will be claimed
PROVIDED FURTHER THAT only one such written demand
may be permitted to be made by SCDC and the County
Council and which shall deal with any and all incurred
Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event Planned Costs
SUBJECT ALWAYS that such written demand shall not seek
to demand payment of more than the Unexploded Ordnance
Evacuation Planned Costs Cap;

"Unexploded
Ordnance
Evacuation
Planned Costs
Cap"
means the combined sum for forward planning of all
Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Events which shall not
total more than £10,000 (ten thousand British pounds) which
shall be paid to SCDC and/or the County Council in such
proportions as shall be set out in the Unexploded Ordnance
Evacuation Event Planned Costs Demand.

"Unexploded
Ordnance
means any costs of the forward planning of all Unexploded
Ordnance Evacuation Events PROVIDED THAT SCDC and
the County Council shall only be entitled to reimbursement of
Evacuation Event Planned Costs their costs incurred in pre-planning for all Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Events AND PROVIDED FURTHER THAT SCDC and the County Council shall only be entitled to recover costs that are properly and reasonably incurred by appointed SCDC and/or County Council officers.

1. ENVIRONMENTAL CONTRIBUTIONS
    1.1 Prior to Commencement of any Development Parcel the Owners shall pay SCDC the Air Quality Monitoring Contribution to be used for Air Quality Monitoring Purposes only.
    1.2 Prior to Implementation the Owners shall pay SCDC the Noise and Vibration Monitoring Equipment Contribution to be used for Noise and Vibration Monitoring Equipment Purposes only.
    1.3 Prior to Commencement of the Earthworks the Owners shall pay the Longstanton Watercourse Flows Contribution to be used for the Longstanton Technical Purposes only.

2. ORDNANCE
The Owners covenant with SCDC and the County Council:
    2.1 that if by implementation or following Implementation a contract has been awarded by the Owners to undertake intrusive ground investigations on the Site including boreholes, trial boreholes, test pits and window samples and in consequence any Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event is required then the Owners will pay SCDC and the County Council their respective proportions as set out in the Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Planned Demand Notice of the Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event Planned Costs within 40 Working Days of the receipt of the validly served Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Planned Costs Demand.
    2.2 to pay SCDC and the County Council their respective proportions as set out in the Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Live Costs Demand of the Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event Live Costs within 40 Working Days of the receipt of the validly served Unexploded Ordnance Evacuation Event Live Costs Demand.

# SCHEDULE 13 - GOVERNANCE

## RELEVANT DEFINITIONS

"Electoral Support
Contribution"
means the sum of £11,250 (eleven thousand two hundred and fifty pounds) to be used towards Electoral Support Purposes;

"Electoral Support
Purposes"
means the administrative costs associated with holding an election for a town council or parish council related to Northstowe provided that this will not include the costs of candidates for any town council or parish council position for which elections are held;

"Town Council
Contribution"
means the sum of £11,250 (eleven thousand two hundred and fifty British pounds) to be used towards Town Council Purposes;

"Town Council Purposes"
means the administrative costs associated with establishing pursuant to any statutory or policy guidance or requirements a town council/parish council/community council or other local representative body that will undertake any functions, roles, governance and/or responsibilities for Northstowe.

1. TOWN COUNCIL CONTRIBUTION

1.1 Prior to April 2015 the Owners shall pay to SCDC the Town Council Contribution to be used for Town Council Purposes only.

2. ELECTORAL SUPPORT CONTRIBUTION

2.1 Prior to April 2015 the Owners shall pay to SCDC the Electoral Support Contribution to Electoral Support Purposes.

# SCHEDULE 14 - OPEN SPACE

## RELEVANT DEFINITIONS

"the Account"
means an account to be opened by the Owners into which the Northstowe Phase 1 Maintenance Contribution will be paid by the Owners in accordance with paragraph 6 of Part 5 of this Schedule 14 and from which monies can be drawn down as provided for in this Schedule for the Northstowe Phase 1 Management and Maintenance Purposes;

"All Weather Pitch"
A senior size short pile 3G (40mm) rubber crumb artificial grass pitch designed by a specialist contractor in accordance with "Selecting the Right Artificial Pitch" which standards are jointly approved by English Hockey Board, The Football Association, The Football Foundation, The RFL, The RFU and Sport England or such variation or amendment to the standards or such other publication as may from time to time replace the standards and which may be jointly approved by the bodies for use in relation to artificial pitches;

"Alternative Public Realm Funding"
means any means of funding the Northstowe Phase 1 Management and Management Purposes other than by means of utilising the Northstowe Phase 1 Maintenance Contribution and such alternative sources of funding may include the following:
1. Annual Maintenance Charge; and/or
2. Hire charges levied on users of the Community Building, the Sports Pavilion and Sports Pitches; and/or
3. Any grants or donations whether from public or private sources.

"Annual Maintenance Charge"
means any annual maintenance charge or rent charge or other such financial charge imposed on and secured against the Dwellings whose specific purpose is to secure the financial contribution by owners of the Dwellings towards the management and maintenance of all or any part of the Public Realm where such financial charges may be levied by the Owners or a Management Company responsible for the Public Realm to which the financial charge relates and which will be imposed from the date each Dwelling is Occupied for the first time;

"Cricket Upgrade Contribution"
means the Remaining Sports Balance (if any) that may exist at the date that the Council's Final Certificate is issued in relation to the Sports Pitches SUBJECT TO a maximum of £78,000 (seventy eight thousand British pounds) PROVIDED THAT where the Remaining Sports Balance exceeds £78,000 (seventy eight thousand British pounds) the Cricket Upgrade Contribution shall be fixed at the aforesaid maximum and any remaining monies may be utilised pursuant to the provisions of paragraph 1.2 of Part 5 of this Schedule PROVIDED ALWAYS THAT the amount of the Cricket Upgrade Contribution may be nil where the Remaining Sports Balance is nil;

"Cricket Purpose" means towards the upgrading of the existing cricket square and outfield at the Longstanton Sports and Social Club or for new cricket facilities alongside the Sports Pitches;

"Formal MUGA" means a multi-use games area sufficient in size to cater for two courts/pitches;

"Formal Park" means the area of land being 0.75 hectares (unless otherwise agreed between the Owners and SCDC) within the Development whose location is shown indicatively on Plan 9 attached to this Agreement to be dedicated for use by the public as informal open space within the Development (and additionally the Informal Single Court MUGA if not provided under Schedule 8) to be laid out in accordance with the Formal Park Specification;

"Formal Park Specification" means a specification for the size materials equipment and all other relevant details necessary for the provision of the Formal Park on the Site to be prepared by or on behalf of the Owners by a suitably qualified and professionally approved person experienced in landscape and design in preparing and reviewing specifications for such formal parks which specifications shall be submitted and approved pursuant the provisions of this Agreement PROVIDED THAT the total combined cost of providing the Formal Park (excluding the Informal Single Court MUGA) and NEAP pursuant to the Formal Park Specification and NEAP Specification shall not exceed £260,000 (two hundred and sixty thousand pounds);

"Incidental Open Space" means any area of land immediately surrounding any part of the Open Space Land or On Site SUDS and land which would not otherwise form part of a garden of a Dwelling;

"LAP" means a local area of play being an area of play within the Development the exact location(s) of which will be the subject of approval as part of Reserved Matters Approvals (for relevant Development Parcels) for use by children and young people which for the avoidance of doubt shall not include the provision of any equipment but which unless otherwise agreed in writing by SCDC shall be in accordance with paras 4.48 and 4.49 (inclusive) of SCDC's SPD on Open Space in New Developments ("the Open Space SPD");

"LAP Specification" means a specification for the size and any other relevant details necessary for the provision of a LAP on the Site to be prepared by or on behalf of the Applicants for any Reserved Matters Approvals by a person suitably experienced in landscape and design in preparing and reviewing specifications for LAPs;

"LEAP" means Local Equipped Areas for Play for children of early school age up to 2 of which may be provided as SIPs to be located within the areas shown indicatively edged red on Plan 9 attached to this Agreement which are to be designated and equipped in accordance with the approved LEAP Specification and which unless otherwise agreed in writing by SCDC shall be provided in accordance with (i) paras 4.50-4.55 (inclusive) of the Open Space SPD and (ii) the locational and design criteria set out in Sections 6.1 and 6.2 of Fields in Trust document "Planning and Design for Outdoor Sport and
Play 2008” which directly relate to provision of a LEAP and PROVIDED THAT no regard shall be had to the content of any documents or other sources which may be referred to the Fields in Trust document in order to set criteria AND PROVIDED ALWAYS that in the event of any discrepancies and/or inconsistencies between (i) the Open Space SPD and (ii) the Fields in Trust document "Planning and Design for Outdoor Sport and Play 2008” then the Open Space SPD shall take precedence;

"LEAP
Specification" means a specification for the size materials equipment and all other relevant details necessary for the provision of the LEAPs on the Site to be prepared by or on behalf of the Owners by a suitably qualified and professionally approved person experienced in landscape and design in preparing and reviewing specifications for such LEAPs which specifications shall be submitted and approved pursuant the provisions of this Agreement PROVIDED THAT the total cumulative cost of providing all LEAPs and SIPs to be provided as part of the Development pursuant to the LEAP Specification and SIP Specification shall not exceed £260,000 (two hundred and sixty thousand pounds);

"NEAP" means a Neighbourhood Equipped Area for Play and which are mainly for older children to be located within the areas shown indicatively edged blue on Plan 9 attached to this Agreement which is to be designated and equipped in accordance with the approved NEAP Specification and which unless otherwise agreed in writing by SCDC shall be provided in accordance with (i) paras 4.56-4.63 (inclusive) of the Open Space SPD and (ii) the locational and design criteria set out in Sections 6.1 and 6.2 of the Fields in Trust document "Planning and Design for Outdoor Sport and Play 2008” which directly relate to provision of NEAP and PROVIDED THAT no regard shall be had to the content of any documents or other sources which may be referred to the Fields in Trust document in order to set criteria AND PROVIDED ALWAYS that in the event of any discrepancies and/or inconsistencies between (i) the Open Space SPD and (ii) the Fields in Trust document "Planning and Design for Outdoor Sport and Play 2008” then the Open Space SPD shall take precedence;

"NEAP
Specification" means a specification for the size materials equipment and all other relevant details necessary for the provision of the NEAP on the Site to be prepared by or on behalf of the Owners by a suitably qualified and professionally approved person experienced in landscape and design in preparing and reviewing specifications for such NEAPs which specification shall be submitted and approved pursuant the provisions of this Agreement PROVIDED THAT the total combined cost of providing the Formal Park and NEAP pursuant to the Formal Park Specification and NEAP Specification shall not exceed £260,000 (two hundred and sixty thousand pounds);

"Northstowe Phase 1 Maintenance
Contribution" means the sum of £1,500,000 (one million five hundred thousand British pounds to be used to fund the Northstowe Phase 1 Management and Maintenance Purposes only;

"Northstowe Phase 1 Management and Maintenance Purposes" means any management and/or maintenance works reasonably required to be carried out in respect of the Public Realm;

"Northstowe Phase 1 Management and Maintenance Strategy" means a strategy to be approved or deemed approved by SCDC which shall be designed to ensure the effective management and maintenance of the Public Realm, promoting matters including accessibility to the Public Realm, a comprehensive approach to management of the Public Realm and promoting community engagement and consultation in respect of the Public Realm and which strategy shall include detail regarding those matters at Appendix 22;

"Northstowe Sports Purpose" means towards the enhanced provision of the Sports Pitches;

"Open Space Land" means the Play Areas, the Sports Pitches, the Formal Park and any Incidental Open Space;

"Open Space Land Transfer" means the transfer by the Owners of the unencumbered freehold or leasehold interest in any Play Area, or part of the Sports Pitches or Formal Park in the form of the draft transfer appended hereto at Appendix 21 or such other form as the parties may otherwise agree and each party shall bear their own legal costs incurred in connection with the negotiation and completion of such transfer but which shall nevertheless:

1. not include any terms which would restrict public access save for the purposes of maintenance works, ecological or similar environmental requirements or in the case of emergency; and

2. not include any terms which would directly or indirectly affect the construction servicing or occupation of the part of the Site that is retained by the Owners; and

3. include any reasonable reservation of rights of access and services over the Play Area Sports Pitches or Formal Park (as applicable) for the benefit of any other part of the Site for the purpose of laying managing maintaining replacing renewing cleaning and repairing services including but not limited to as applicable sustainable urban drainage measures, water, gas, sewerage, drainage or electricity; and

4. include for the benefit of the Play Area Sports Pitches or Formal Park (as applicable) the grant of any and all reasonable rights of access and services which are reasonably required for the use, management and maintenance of the Play Area Sports Pitches or Formal Park (as applicable) over any adjoining land for its intended purpose as set out in this Schedule; and be at nil consideration, and

5. In relation to LEAPs and SIPs, NEAP and Formal Park (excluding the Informal Single Court MUGA
(as defined in Schedule 5)), that the LEAPs and
SIPs, NEAP and Formal Park (excluding the
Informal Single Court MUGA) shall not be used for
any purpose other than for a publicly accessible free
at the point of use games area or publically
accessible free at the point of use recreational
facilities; and

6. In relation to Sports Pitches: that the Sports Pitches
shall not be used for any purpose other than for
publicly accessible sport and recreation facilities;
and

7. In relation to any LAP: that the LAP shall not be
used for any purpose other than for public open
space; and

8. In relation to the Informal Single Court MUGA (as
defined in Schedule 8) should it form part of the
Formal Park: the Informal Single Court MUGA shall
not be used for any purpose other than as publicly
accessible sports and recreation facilities.

"Play Areas" means collectively the LAPs LEAPs, SIPs, and the NEAP to
be located on the Site;

"Public Realm" means those areas, land and buildings within the Site as set
out below:
1. On-site SUDS (as defined in Schedule 4);

2. the Off Site Flood Mitigation Works (as defined in
Schedule 5);

3. the Community Building and/or Sports Pavilion
(both as defined in Schedule 8);

4. the Allotments and/or Orchard Land (both as
defined in Schedule 9); and

5. the Open Space Land.

"Remaining Sports
Balance" means the amount of the Sports Pitches Cost Cap remaining
after the date that the Council Final Certificate is issued in
relation to the Sports Pitches and all costs fees and other
expenditure incurred by the Owners in constructing the
Sports Pitches in accordance with the Sports Pitches
Specification have been deducted from the amount of money
identified as the Sports Pitches Cost Cap PROVIDED
ALWAYS THAT for the avoidance of doubt the Remaining
Sports Balance may be nil;

"SIPs" means a Space for Imaginative Play to complement the
LEAPs to be located within the areas shown indicatively
edged yellow on Plan 9 attached to this Agreement which
unless otherwise agreed with SCDC are to be designated in
accordance with (i) paras 4.54-4.6S (inclusive) of the Open
Space SPD (ii) chapter D10 of the Northstowe Area Action
Plan (adopted July 2007) and (iii) the approved SIP
Specification and based on the locational and design criteria set out in sections 6.1 and 6.2 of the Fields Trust document "Planning and Design for Outdoor Sport and Play 2008" which directly relate to provision of a SIP and PROVIDED THAT no regard shall be had to the content of any documents or other sources which may be referred to in these documents in order to set criteria AND PROVIDED ALWAYS that in the event of any discrepancies and/or inconsistencies between (i) the Open Space SPD and (ii) the Fields in Trust document "Planning and Design for Outdoor Sport and Play 2008" then the Open Space SPD shall take precedence;

**"SIP Specification"** means a specification for the size materials equipment and all other relevant details necessary for the provision of the SIPs on the Site to be prepared by or on behalf of the Owners by a suitably qualified and professionally approved person experienced in landscape and design in preparing and renewing specifications for such SIPs which specifications shall be submitted and approved pursuant to the provisions of this Agreement PROVIDED THAT the total cumulative cost of providing all LEAPs and SIPs to be provided as part of the Development pursuant to the LEAP Specification and the SIP Specification shall not exceed £260,000 (two hundred and sixty thousand pounds);

**"Sports Pitches Costs Cap"** means subject to part 11 of this Schedule 14 the total combined cost of providing the Sports Pitches (pursuant to the approved Sports Pitches Specification) and either the Cricket Purpose or the Northstowe Sports Purpose which shall not exceed £1,516,263 (one million five hundred and sixteen thousand two hundred and sixty three pounds);

**"Sports Pitches"** means sites for formal sports pitch space which unless otherwise agreed with SCDC are to be provided in accordance with paras 4.10-4.32 (inclusive) of the Open Space SPD (located on the Site with a means of access provided, and which shall be further detailed within the Sports Pitches Specification and which shall include:
1. 2 mini football pitches
2. 2 junior pitches
3. 1 senior football pitches
4. 1 senior rugby pitch
5. 1 bowling green
6. Synthetic Artificially Turfed Pitch
7. Formal MUGA
8. Informal Single Court MUGA (where provided for pursuant to this Schedule 14)

AND PROVIDED ALWAYS that in the event of any discrepancies and/or inconsistencies between (i) the Open Space SPD and (ii) the standards set out by Sport England in relation to such Sports Pitches than the standards set out by
Sports England shall take precedence;

"Sports Pitches Specification" means the specification for the design laying out and maintaining of the Sports Pitches to the standards set out by Sport England in relation to such Sports Pitches or as agreed by SCDC which specification shall be submitted and approved pursuant the provisions of this Agreement PROVIDED THAT the total combined cost of providing the Sports Pitches pursuant to the Sports Pitches Specification shall not exceed the Sports Pitches Costs Cap;

"Sports Strategy" means a strategy setting out the provision of the Sports Pavilion the Sports Pitches the Formal MUGA and other associated facilities including details of implementation;

"Youth and Play Strategy" means a strategy setting out the provision of the Formal Park, LEAPs and SIPs, NEAP, LAPs and other associated facilities including details of implementation of such provisions and facilities.

## PART 1 - SPORTS STRATEGY

1. Prior to or simultaneously with submitting the first Reserved Matters Application for any Development Parcel within which Dwellings are to be constructed the Owners shall submit the Sports Strategy to SCDC for approval.

2. The Owners shall implement or procure implementation of those aspects of the Sports Strategy reasonably within its control following approval of the Sports Strategy pursuant to paragraph 1 of this Part 1 of Schedule 14 and subject thereto shall thereafter comply with the Sports Strategy.

## PART 2 - YOUTH AND PLAY STRATEGY

1. Prior to or simultaneously with submitting the first Reserved Matters Application for any Development Parcel within which Dwellings are to be constructed the Owners shall submit the Youth and Play Strategy to SCDC for approval.

2. The Owners shall implement or procure implementation of those aspects of the Youth and Play Strategy reasonably within its control following approval of the Sports Strategy pursuant to paragraph 1 of this Part 1 of Schedule 14 and subject thereto shall thereafter comply with the Youth and Play Strategy.

## PART 3 - LAP, LEAP and SIP, NEAP AND FORMAL PARK SPECIFICATIONS

1. The Sports Pitch Specification, Formal Park Specification, NEAP Specification, LEAP Specification and the SIP Specification shall be submitted by the Owners to SCDC and approved by SCDC (or deemed approved in relation to the matters outlined at paragraphs 1.3 and/or 1.4 only to have been approved by SCDC) either:
    1.1 as part of Reserved Matters Applications for the Sports Pitches Formal Park NEAP LEAP or SIPs adjacent to which the Sports Pitches Formal Park NEAP LEAP or SIPs (as applicable) are located; or
    1.2 as part of a Reserved Matters Application solely for the Sports Pitches Formal Park NEAP LEAP or SIPs (as applicable); or
    1.3 as a standalone submissions to SCDC solely for the respective approval of the Sports Pitches Specification Formal Park Specification NEAP Specification or various LEAP Specification or SIP Specification (as applicable) which may be submitted to SCDC at
any time prior to Reserved Matters Applications being submitted to SCDC in relation
to any area of land that includes land which such Reserved Matters Applications
identify as Sports Pitches, Formal Park, NEAP, LEAPs or SIPs.

1.4 The Sports Pitch Specification, Formal Park Specification, NEAP Specification, LEAP
Specification and SIP Specification may give rise to matters that will not affect
approval or refusal of a Reserved Matters Application involving the Sports Pitch,
Formal Park, NEAPs, LEAPs or SIPs and in those circumstances the approval or
deemed approval of the Sports Pitch Specification, Formal Park Specification,
NEAP Specification, LEAP Specification and SIP Specification will be considered as if the
Specification and SIP Specification has been made as a standalone submission as
opposed to part of a Reserved Matters Application and as a consequence the
deemed approval of the Sports Pitch Specification, Formal Park Specification, NEAP
Specification, LEAP Specification and SIP Specification may occur at a different time from the approval of any Reserved Matters Application
involving the Sports Pitch, Formal Park, NEAPs, LEAPs or SIPs and for the avoidance
of doubt any approval or deemed approval shall not be taken to imply approval of any
Reserved Matters Application.

1.5 Unless otherwise agreed in writing by SCDC as not required the LAP Specification
shall (if not organised previously) be submitted for approval by the Applicant as part of
a Reserved Matters Application for a relevant Development Parcel.

## PART 4 - OPEN SPACE LAND TRANSFER

1. Prior to Occupation of any Dwelling to serve written notice on SCDC which contains
one of the following options that the Owners intend to pursue in respect of the freehold
or the leasehold of the relevant Open Space Land:

1.1 The written notice shall include an offer to transfer to SCDC (or the Town Council on
the election of SCDC) the Sports Pitches, Formal Park, NEAP, LEAPs or SIPs (as
applicable) pursuant to the relevant Open Space Land Transfer PROVIDED THAT if
SCDC (or the Town Council on the election of SCDC) accepts the offer of the Sports
Pitches, Formal Park, NEAP, LEAPs or SIPs (as applicable) the actual transfer will be
executed and delivered to the appropriate body after the relevant Council Final
Certificate is issued; or

1.2 The written notice shall notify SCDC that the Owners wish to offer a leasehold interest
of not more than 10 years in the Sports Pitches, Formal Park, NEAP, LEAPs or SIPs
(as applicable) and relevant part of the Open Space Land pursuant to the relevant
Open Space Land Transfer to an Alternative Body PROVIDED THAT in such
notification it shall set out the name, address, company registration number (where
appropriate) and evidence that the Alternative Body is constituted and this information
shall be provided to SCDC in order that SCDC can make a decision whether or not to
approve the Alternative Body a suitable entity or organisation as regards the
management and maintenance of the Sports Pitches, Formal Park, NEAP, LEAPs or
SIPs (as applicable) and relevant part of the Open Space Land PROVIDED
FURTHER THAT the written offer of a transfer of the relevant part of the Open Space
Land to the Alternative Body will not be permitted until such time as the Alternative
Body has been approved or deemed approved by SCDC pursuant to the Approval
Process AND PROVIDED FURTHER if the Alternative Body accepts the offer of the
Sports Pitches, Formal Park, NEAP, LEAPs or SIPs (as applicable) and relevant part
of the Open Space Land the Owners shall execute and deliver to the Alternative Body
the relevant Open Space Land Transfer after the Council Final Certificate is issued; or

1.3 The written notice will confirm that the Owners will either:

1.3.1 retain the freehold interest and maintain the Sports Pitches, Formal Park,
NEAP, LEAPs or SIPs (as applicable) and relevant part of the Open Space
Land and to appoint a Management Company to maintain and manage the
Sports Pitches, Formal Park, NEAP, LEAPs or SIPs (as applicable) and
relevant part of the Open Space Land on the Owners' behalf; or

1.3.2 transfer a leasehold interest of not more than 10 years in Sports Pitches,
Formal Park, NEAP, LEAPs or SIPs (as applicable) and relevant part of the
Open Space Land to the Management Company.

PROVIDED THAT the Owners shall not be required to commit to which of these two
options in this paragraph 1.3 of this Part 4 of this Schedule 14 the Owners will pursue
at the date the written notice is served and the Owners shall only be required to notify
SCDC that they intend to pursue an option involving a Management Company.
PROVIDED always that the Owners shall unless otherwise agreed with SCDC notify
SCDC which of these options is being chosen within 12 months of the initial written
notice or the letting of the relevant contract whichever is the earlier.

1.4 In the event of a transfer of a leasehold interest to a Management Company the
Owners shall be under an obligation to use all Reasonable Endeavours to complete
such leasehold transfer as soon as reasonably possible following the date of
appointment of the Management Company and if within the first 6 months of the said
appointment of the Management Company the leasehold transfer has not been
completed the Owners shall, if requested, be required by SCDC acting reasonably in
making any such request, to provide evidence to demonstrate why there is any
continuing delay in completion of the leasehold interest being transferred and for the
avoidance of doubt the Owners shall thereafter continue to be under an obligation to
use all Reasonable Endeavours to complete the leasehold transfer as soon as
reasonably possible.

1.5 In the event that for the purposes of paragraph 1.1 of this Part 4 of this Schedule 14
above SCDC (or the Town Council on the election of SCDC) declines or does not
respond to the offer to have transferred to it the Sports Pitches, Formal Park, NEAP,
LEAPs or SIPs (as applicable) and relevant part of the Open Space Land within 40
Working Days of service of the respective offer to transfer the Owners shall either:

1.5.1 Where the offer has been made to SCDC (or the Town Council on the
election of SCDC) the Owners will notify SCDC in writing that it intends
either to

(a) transfer a leasehold interest of not more than 10 years in the
Sports Pitches, Formal Park, NEAP, LEAPs or SIPs (as applicable)
and relevant part of the Open Space Land to an Alternative Body in
which case any such notification the Owners shall set out the
name, address, company registration number (where appropriate)
and evidence that the Alternative Body is constituted and this
information shall be provided to SCDC in order that SCDC can
make a decision whether or not to approve the Alternative Body a
suitable entity or organisation as regards the management and
maintenance of the Sports Pitches, Formal Park, NEAP, LEAPs or
SIPs (as applicable) and relevant part of the Open Space Land; or

(b) transfer a leasehold interest of not more than 10 years in the
Sports Pitches, Formal Park, NEAP, LEAPs or SIPs (as applicable)
and relevant part of the Open Space Land to a Management
Company; or

(c) retain the freehold interest in the Sports Pitches, Formal Park,
NEAP, LEAPs or SIPs (as applicable) and relevant part of the
Open Space Land and appoint a Management Company to
manage and maintain the Sports Pitches, Formal Park, NEAP,
LEAPs or SIPs (as applicable) and relevant part of the Open Space Land on the Owners' behalf.

1.5.2 The Owners shall be entitled to transfer or otherwise deal with the Sports Pitches, Formal Park, NEAP, LEAPs or SIPs (as applicable) and relevant part of the Open Space Land in accordance with the provisions of the notice served pursuant to paragraph 2.5.1 of this Part 4 of this Schedule 14 PROVIDED THAT a transfer to the Alternative Body will not be permitted until such time as the Alternative Body has been approved or deemed approved by SCDC pursuant to the Approval Process.

1.5.3 Where the offer has been made to an Alternative Body and the Alternative Body declines or does not respond the Owners will notify SCDC in writing that it intends to either

(a) transfer a leasehold interest of not more than 10 years in the Sports Pitches, Formal Park, NEAP, LEAPs or SIPs (as applicable) and relevant part of the Open Space Land to a Management Company; or

(b) retain the freehold interest in the Sports Pitches, Formal Park, NEAP, LEAPs or SIPs (as applicable) and relevant part of the Open Space Land and appoint a Management Company to manage and maintain the Sports Pitches, Formal Park, NEAP, LEAPs or SIPs (as applicable) and relevant part of the Open Space Land on the Owners' behalf

PROVIDED THAT the Owners shall not be required to commit to which of these two options in paragraph 2.5.2 of this Part 4 of this Schedule 14 the Owners will pursue at the date the written notice is served and the Owners shall only be required to notify SCDC that they intend to pursue an option involving a Management Company PROVIDED always that the Owners shall unless otherwise agreed with SCDC notify SCDC which of these options is being chosen within 12 months of the initial written notice or the letting of the relevant contract whichever is the earlier

1.6 Unless otherwise agreed with SCDC the Owners shall make any offer to transfer the Open Space Land pursuant to this paragraph conditional upon the relevant Open Space Land thereafter being managed in accordance with the Northstowe Phase 1 Management and Maintenance Strategy; and

1.7 In the event that a Management Company is appointed by the Owners to manage the Open Space Land or the Management Company takes a transfer of a leasehold interest in the Open Space Land then after a period of 10 years from the date a Management Company is appointed by the Owners or the leasehold transfer to the Management Company is completed the following shall apply:

1.7.1 then the Owners shall offer to transfer all of the freehold interests that it retains in the Open Space Land to SCDC (or if so nominated by SCDC to the Town Council if the Town Council has been properly constituted and has the ability to accept a transfer of land) for nil consideration pursuant to the relevant Open Space Land Transfer; and

1.7.2 in the event SCDC (or if so nominated by SCDC the Town Council) has not accepted the offer within 60 Working Days

then the offer shall be treated as withdrawn and the Owners shall be permitted to transfer all of the freehold interests that it retains in the Open Space Land to the Management Company PROVIDED ALWAYS THAT nothing shall compel the Owners to terminate an existing lease other than in accordance with any pre-existing break clause within that lease and FOR THE AVOIDANCE OF DOUBT nothing within this
paragraph shall require the Owners to incur any expense in transferring the freehold interests to SCDC (or if so nominated by SCDC the Town Council) save that the Owners and SCDC (or if so nominated by SCDC the Town Council) shall bear their own legal and administrative costs in relation to the transfer of the freehold interests.

1.8 In the event that an Alternative Body takes a transfer of a leasehold interest in the Open Space Land pursuant to paragraph 2.5.2 of Part 4 of this Schedule 14 then after a period of 10 years from the date of the leasehold transfer to the Alternative Body the following shall apply:
1.8.1 the Owners shall offer to transfer all of the freehold interests that it retains in the Open Space Land to SCDC (or if so nominated by SCDC the Town Council if the Town Council has been properly constituted and has the ability to accept a transfer of land) for nil consideration pursuant to the relevant Open Space Land Transfer; and
1.8.2 in the event that SCDC (or if so nominated by SCDC the Town Council) has not accepted the offer within 60 Working Days then the offer shall be treated as withdrawn and the Owners shall be permitted to transfer all of the freehold interests that it retains in the Open Space Land to an Alternative Body or to a Management Company

PROVIDED ALWAYS THAT nothing shall compel the Owners to terminate an existing lease other than in accordance with any pre-existing break clause within that lease and FOR THE AVOIDANCE OF DOUBT nothing within this paragraph shall require the Owners to incur any expense in transferring the freehold interests to SCDC (or if so nominated by SCDC the Town Council) save that the Owners and SCDC (or if so nominated by SCDC the Town Council) shall bear their own legal and administrative costs in relation to the transfer of the freehold interests.

2. The above provisions in Part 4 of this Schedule 14 shall likewise apply mutatis mutandis to the Transfer/management of all LAPs but the timing under para 1 above as to service of the notice as to which option is being pursued rather than being by reference to "prior to Occupation of any Dwelling" shall instead be by reference to "prior to Occupation of any Dwelling in the particular Development Parcel in which the relevant LAP is situated".

3. For the avoidance of doubt the Owners may locate any Utilities and/or Foul/Surface Water Drainage and Sewerage in under or over any part of the Open Space Land.

## PART 5 - MANAGEMENT AND MAINTENANCE OF THE PUBLIC REALM

1. The Owners shall prior to Occupation of any Dwelling comprised in the Development submit the Northstowe Phase 1 Management and Maintenance Strategy to SCDC for approval or deemed approval.

2. The Owners shall ensure that each area of Open Space Land shall be maintained in accordance with the Northstowe Phase 1 Management and Maintenance Strategy.

3. No Occupation of any Dwelling shall be permitted until SCDC has approved or be deemed to have approved the Northstowe Phase 1 Management and Maintenance Strategy in writing.

4. No Dwellings shall be permitted to be Occupied until the Management Company has been employed to ensure that the mechanism for recovery from future owners of the Dwellings of the maintenance costs for the Open Space Land as detailed in the Northstowe Phase 1 Management and Maintenance Strategy can be achieved.

5. The Owners shall implement or procure implementation of those aspects of the Northstowe Phase 1 Management and Maintenance Strategy reasonably within its
control and subject thereto shall thereafter comply with the Northstowe Phase 1
Management and maintenance Strategy in all respects.

6. Once the Northstowe Phase 1 Management and Maintenance Strategy has been
approved or deemed approved by SCDC the Owners shall set up the Account and pay
the Northstowe Phase 1 Maintenance Contribution into the Account as follows or as
otherwise agreed with SCDC having regard to the end of the relevant contract
maintenance period in relation to the Public Realm so as to ensure that there is no
gap of maintenance and/or funding in such regard:

6.1 £375,000 (Three Hundred and Seventy Five Thousand British Pounds) to be paid prior
to Occupation of 200 Dwellings

6.2 £375,000 (Three Hundred and Seventy Five Thousand British Pounds) to be paid prior
to Occupation of 500 Dwellings

6.3 £375,000 (Three Hundred and Seventy Five Thousand British Pounds) to be paid prior
to Occupation of 800 Dwellings; and

6.4 £375,000 (Three Hundred and Seventy Five Thousand British Pounds) to be paid
prior to Occupation of 1100 Dwellings.

7. The details of the Account and its operation shall be approved as part of the
Northstowe Phase 1 Management and Maintenance Strategy and PROVIDED THAT
the Account shall not be opened until the Northstowe Phase 1 Management and
Maintenance Strategy has been approved.

7.1 The Account will operate as a form of escrow account;

7.2 Withdrawals from the Account shall only be permitted by those persons or entities that
have been approved or deemed approved by Gallagher and SCDC at the time the
Account is set up PROVIDED THAT such persons or entities shall always include the
following:

7.2.1 Gallagher; and

7.2.2 SCDC where SCDC has accepted an offer or taken a transfer of any of the
Public Realm; and

7.2.3 The County Council where the County Council has accepted an offer or
taken a transfer of any of the Public Realm

AND FURTHER where it is possible to establish the escrow account with flexibility to
add other persons or entities that may be nominated to have the right to make
withdrawals from the Account then additional persons or entities shall accordingly be
added from time to time where they have first been approved or deemed approved by
Gallagher and SCDC as providing any management or maintenance of the Public
Realm in accordance with the Northstowe Phase 1 Management and Maintenance
Strategy.

7.3 That a maximum of £180,000 (one hundred and eighty thousand British pounds) shall
be permitted to be withdrawn from the Account during both the lifetime of the
Development and if shorter during the period that the Account is open and operational
to be used towards the management and maintenance of the Community Building
and/or the Sports Pavilion PROVIDED THAT unless otherwise agreed by the Steering
Group (set up in accordance with Part 10 of this Schedule) no more than £60,000
(sixty thousand British pounds) shall be entitled to be withdrawn from the Account in
any one calendar year towards the management and maintenance of the Community
Building and/or the Sports Pavilion]
8. The Owners SCDC and the County Council covenant with one another and agree that:
8.1 no withdrawals shall be permitted to be made from the Account unless full use has first been made of any Alternative Public Realm Funding available at the time that a withdrawal is proposed to be made from the Account;

8.2 priority will be given by all parties to pursuing and utilising Alternative Public Realm Funding to undertake the Northstowe Phase 1 Management and Maintenance Purposes PROVIDED THAT nothing in this Agreement shall be deemed to require or permit the use of any Annual Maintenance Charge for the management and maintenance of any part of the Public Realm in relation to which the payment of the Annual Maintenance Charge was not described as being collected towards.

PART 6 - MAINTENANCE SCHEME PROVISIONS

1. The Northstowe Phase 1 Management and Maintenance Strategy shall be prepared by the Owners in writing and which shall include details of the following:

1.1 Responsibility for maintaining the areas of Open Space Land which may involve transfer of the liability to a Management Company (consistent with the other provisions of this Agreement )of all or any parts of the aforementioned areas;

1.2 frequency of maintenance of the different areas of Open Space Land;

1.3 measures to replace any trees shrubs turf or other agreed planting which may die or become diseased following implementation of the Northstowe Phase 1 Management and Maintenance Strategy;

1.4 standards of maintenance and repair to be achieved in respect of the different areas of Open Space Land.

2. The Northstowe Phase 1 Management and Maintenance Strategy shall also include provisions regarding how the costs of the maintenance shall be funded.

PART 7 - COVENANTS RELATING TO LAYING OUT AND CERTIFICATION

1. In relation to the Sports Pitches, Play Areas and Formal Park the Owners and SCDC covenant with each other as follows:

1.1 The Owners shall lay out and install the Sports Pitches, Play Areas and Formal Park (as applicable) to the satisfaction of SCDC and in accordance with:

1.1.1 for the Sports Pitches the approved Sports Pitch Specification;

1.1.2 for the Formal Park the approved Formal Park Specification;

1.1.3 for the NEAP the approved NEAP Specification;

1.1.4 for the LEAP the approved LEAP Specification;

1.1.5 for the SIP the approved SIP Specification; and

1.1.6 for any LAP the Reserved Matters Approval relevant to the Development Parcel within which the LAP is located and details submitted pursuant to planning condition 8 imposed by the Planning Permission (as applicable) and if applicable the LAP Specification

PROVIDED THAT in laying out and installing the Sports Pitches the Owners use a contractor that has been approved by Sports and Play Construction Association (or such other body or association as may from time to time carry out the same or similar
functions to the Sports and Play Construction Association as regards its members professional competency and performance in installation of sports and play pitches and/or sports fields (or such other contractor as may be approved by SCDC as being suitably experienced in laying out and installing sports pitches and fields) AND PROVIDED ALWAYS that in the event of any discrepancies and/or inconsistencies between (i) the Open Space SPD and (ii) the Fields in Trust document "Planning and Design for Outdoor Sport and Play 2008" then the Open Space SPD shall take precedence.

2. The Owners shall notify the Surveyor of all site meetings relating to the laying out of the Sports Pitches, LEAPS and NEAPS.

3. The Owners shall permit the Surveyor:

3.1 to attend and to put forward his/her views at all site meetings during the course of laying out the Sports Pitches, LEAPS and NEAPS;

3.2 on reasonable notice to inspect the Sports Pitches, LEAPS and NEAPS during the course of construction/fitting out;

3.3 to suggest items for inclusion in any necessary schedule of defects issued to the contractor in respect of the Sports Pitches, LEAPS and NEAPS and the Owners acknowledge that the costs of remedying any defects shall be undertaken at the Owners' expense and shall not be taken into account in determining if the relevant Cost Cap has been reached or exceeded;

3.4 to stipulate any items or areas where the Sports Pitches, LEAPS and NEAPS do not meet the relevant Specification (which forms part of the contract for the laying out of the relevant areas) then SCDC may serve on the Owners a written notice which shall specify those parts of the relevant Specification that have not been met and the Owners shall within 60 Working Days from the date of service of such notice (or such other period as SCDC may agree) to carry out such works as are necessary to secure compliance with the relevant Specification at the expense of the Owners and the Owners shall fund any and all reasonable subsequent inspections of the Sports Pitches, LEAPS and NEAPS until the relevant approved Specification shall have been met PROVIDED FURTHER THAT the costs of such inspections do not result in the Sports Pitches Cost Cap being exceeded.

4. Following completion of the Sports Pitches, Play Areas and Formal Park (as applicable) or any part of these areas the Owners will:

4.1 serve notice on SCDC inviting them to inspect the Sports Pitches, Play Areas and Formal Park (as applicable) and issue a Council Completion Certificate confirming that such works have been completed to their reasonable satisfaction;

4.2 if SCDC chooses to inspect the Sports Pitches, Play Areas and Formal Park (as applicable) and identifies necessary remedial works, complete such remedial works to the reasonable satisfaction of SCDC as soon as reasonably practicable;

4.3 upon completion of any remedial works, serve notice on SCDC inviting them to inspect the remedial works identified by them pursuant to paragraph 4.2 of this Part 7 of this Schedule 14 and issue a Council Completion Certificate confirming that the Sports Pitches, Play Areas and Formal Park (as applicable) have been completed to their reasonable satisfaction,
